Experiments on FLUX.1-dev and PIE-Bench show that SelFix improves fixed-point inversion, achieving stronger real-image reconstruction and better source-preserving prompt-based editing than prior inversion baselines. The code is available at https://github.com/seminkim/selfix.

</details>


### [109] [Human-in-the-Loop Atlas-Based 3D Asset Segmentation for Interactive Content Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17824)
*Paul Julius Kühn,Saptarshi Neil Sinha,Jakob Hansen,Robin Horst*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种人机协同的3D模型分割管道，通过贪心视角选择、SAM~2交互式分割和UV反投影，生成可用于材质分配、风格迁移等下游任务的2D参数化分割图集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D资产分割标准依赖具体应用场景且需要用户控制，现有方法难以兼顾灵活性与自动化需求。

Method: 1) 基于贪心集覆盖策略选择紧凑的渲染视图集；2) 结合SAM~2和Label Studio进行交互式2D分割；3) 将分割掩码反投影到UV参数化空间，生成统一的分割图集。

Result: 在8个文化遗产物体上的评估表明该方法能生成可用分割图集，但细结构、空洞和弱外观边界处仍需要人工修正。

Conclusion: 该管道有效支持交互式3D分割，适合交互媒体、游戏和XR等工作流，但复杂几何区域的分割精度仍有提升空间。

Abstract: Segmenting 3D assets into meaningful regions remains challenging, especially when segmentation criteria are application-dependent and require user control. We present a human-in-the-loop pipeline for generating a segmented 2D parameterized atlas from a 3D model for interactive media, game, and XR content workflows. Our method first selects a compact set of rendered views using a greedy set cover strategy over sampled surface points, and then supports interactive segmentation of these views with SAM~2 and Label Studio. The resulting masks are back-projected onto the model's UV parameterization to produce a unified segmented atlas that supports downstream production tasks such as segment-wise material assignment, style transfer, and semantic labeling. We assess the pipeline through a demonstration-based technical evaluation on eight cultural heritage objects. The results show that the approach can generate usable segmented atlases across diverse geometries while revealing recurring sources of manual correction, particularly fine structures, cavities, and weak appearance boundaries.

</details>


### [110] [High-Fidelity 3D Geometric Reconstruction of Pelvic Organs from MRI: A Hybrid Deep Learning and Iterative Optimization Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17836)
*Hui Wang,Xiaowei Li,Chenxin Zhang,Yifan Feng,Jianwei Zuo,Yumeng Tang,Xiuli Sun,Jianliu Wang,Bing Xie,Jiajia Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合可变形形状建模框架，结合深度学习预测与迭代优化，用于从MRI重建膀胱、子宫和直肠的高保真三维几何结构，在几何精度、网格质量和计算效率方面均优于现有主流方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要聚焦于图像分割或三维模型的下游应用，但高保真、高质量的患者特定骨盆器官几何重建仍依赖大量人工、缺乏标准化流程，亟需一种高效且精确的重建方法。

Method: 提出混合可变形形状建模框架，包含三个核心组件：1）几何感知多层级深度学习架构，保持骨盆器官的拓扑一致性；2）两阶段摊销优化训练策略，兼顾全局形状捕捉与局部表面细化；3）整体协同机制——训练阶段迭代优化为深度学习提供监督，推理阶段深度学习快速预测全局器官形态后由迭代优化细化局部表面和网格质量。

Result: 在膀胱、直肠和子宫的重建中，该方法在Chamfer Distance和Dice Similarity Coefficient指标上显著优于主流深度学习方法；在保持高计算效率的同时，整体体网格质量更优；在患者层面，最差10个单元的minSICN和minSIGE均值均高于传统几何后处理算法。

Conclusion: 该混合框架有效解决了骨盆器官三维重建中精度与效率的权衡问题，实现了高几何保真度与高质量网格的统一，为患者特异性盆底建模和分析提供了标准化、自动化的解决方案。

Abstract: Patient-specific 3D reconstruction of pelvic organ geometry from MRI is important for pelvic floor modeling and downstream patient-specific analysis. However, while previous studies have focused primarily on either image segmentation or downstream use of 3D models, the reconstruction of high-fidelity, high-quality geometries remains labor-intensive and poorly standardized. The study introduced a hybrid deformable shape modeling framework that integrates deep learning prediction with iterative optimization for the reconstruction of the bladder, uterus, and rectum. The framework consists of three core components: a geometry-aware multi-level deep learning architecture that preserves topological consistency of pelvic organs; a two-stage amortized optimization training strategy that balances global shape capture and local surface refinement; and a holistic synergy mechanism--where iterative optimization provides supervision for deep learning during the training phase, and during inference, deep learning rapidly predicts the global organ morphology, followed by iterative optimization to refine local surfaces and mesh quality. This framework demonstrated marked superiority in geometric fidelity than current mainstream deep learning-based organ reconstruction models. For individual anatomical structures, the reconstructed 3D geometries for the bladder, rectum, and uterus achieved significantly lower Chamfer Distance values and higher Dice Similarity Coefficient scores. In addition, while maintaining high computational efficiency, the proposed architecture yielded superior overall volumetric mesh quality. At the patient level, the framework achieved higher mean values for the 10 worst elements for both minSICN and minSIGE compared to traditional geometric post-processing algorithms.

</details>


### [111] [A Quantitative Analysis of Multimodal Biomarkers in Alzheimer's Disease](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17867)
*Antonio Scardace,Daniele Ravì*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对阿尔茨海默病（AD）的多模态生物标志物（tau-PET、结构MRI、认知评分、APOE4）进行了系统量化分析，揭示了跨模态的冗余性、预测依赖关系以及神经退行性轨迹，有助于改进生物标志物选择并减少患者负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AD研究越来越多地采用多模态方法整合分子、结构、临床和遗传生物标志物，但这些模态之间的动态关系仍缺乏系统理解，亟需定量分析以改进疾病建模、识别冗余评估并降低患者负担和采集成本。

Method: 利用ADNI数据集的789名受试者，整合tau-PET、结构MRI、认知评分（MMSE和CDR）及APOE4数据，进行了四项分析：(A)量化跨模态互信息和解释方差；(B)分析tau拓扑与结构萎缩的区域关联；(C)分解tau-认知关联为萎缩相关与萎缩独立成分；(D)识别与认知衰退一致的神经退行性主导轨迹。

Result: 研究提供了跨模态关系的系统表征，量化了多模态生物标志物之间的冗余性和预测依赖性，识别了与认知衰退相关的神经退行性主导轨迹，并筛选出具有信息价值的感兴趣区域（ROIs）。

Conclusion: 该研究系统性地表征了AD多模态生物标志物之间的相互关系，提升了生物标志物的可解释性和选择效率，代码已开源。

Abstract: Despite increasing adoption of multimodal approaches in Alzheimer's Disease (AD) research -- aimed at integrating molecular, structural, clinical, and genetic biomarkers to enhance disease characterization -- the relationships among these modalities remain poorly understood. A systematic analysis of their dynamic interaction is essential for improving disease modeling, identifying redundant assessments, and reducing patient burden and acquisition costs. In this paper, we present a quantitative analysis of multimodal AD biomarkers by integrating tau-PET, structural MRI, cognitive scores (MMSE and CDR), and APOE4 data from 789 subjects drawn from the ADNI dataset. In our analyses, we (A) quantify cross-modal mutual information and explained variance to assess redundancy and predictive dependencies; (B) examine associations between tau topologies and structural atrophy across brain regions to select informative ROIs; (C) perform a statistical decomposition of the tau-cognition association into atrophy-related and atrophy-independent components; (D) and identify a dominant neurodegenerative trajectory that aligns with cognitive decline. This study provides a systematic characterization of cross-modal relationships, improving the interpretability and selection of biomarkers in AD. Code is publicly available at: https://github.com/antonioscardace/Multimodal-AD.

</details>


### [112] [Revisiting Structural Dependency in Autoregressive Multi-Task Table Recognition via Order-Independent Cell-Level Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17874)
*Takaya Kawakatsu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种结构精炼模块，通过非因果注意力生成顺序无关的单元格特征，实现并行推理和全局一致性，在表格识别任务中提升定位与识别效果并减少约3倍推理时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于自回归解码器的多任务表格识别方法中，单元格表示依赖于生成顺序，导致全局一致性下降。

Method: 提出结构精炼模块，利用非因果注意力生成顺序无关的单元格特征，使每个单元格能基于全局上下文进行并行推理。

Result: 在两个大型数据集上，单元格定位和端到端识别性能持续提升，推理时间减少约3倍。

Conclusion: 非因果注意力的结构精炼模块能有效消除自回归产生的顺序依赖，提升表格识别的全局一致性和推理效率。

Abstract: Multi-task table recognition jointly addresses table structure prediction, cell localization, and cell content recognition within a unified framework. Existing approaches often rely on autoregressive decoders to generate table structures and reuse their hidden states for cell localization and content recognition. This autoregressive generation process can make cell representations order-dependent, degrading global consistency across cells. This paper proposes a structural refinement module that produces order-independent cell features through non-causal attention. This design enables parallel inference of cell contents while conditioning each cell on global context encoded in the refined features. Experiments on two large datasets demonstrate consistent gains in cell localization and end-to-end recognition, while reducing overall inference time by around threefold.

</details>


### [113] [MoonSplat: Monocular Online Gaussian Splatting with Sim(3) Global Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17935)
*Guo Pu,Yixuan Han,Haofeng Li,Yao Zhang,Hui Zhou,Zhouhui Lian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MoonSplat，一种集成全局Sim(3)优化的在线体素化3DGS重建框架，解决了现有在线3DGS方法中相机位姿估计脆弱和大规模场景优化效率低的问题，通过颜色残差学习加速收敛，在室内外数据集上实现了SOTA性能，并在真实无人机主动重建系统中验证了鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线3DGS方法存在两个关键挑战：1）由于缺乏全局优化，相机位姿估计脆弱；2）在大规模或长序列场景中优化效率低。

Method: 1）提出在线体素化3DGS重建框架，集成全局Sim(3)优化，实现可靠的相机跟踪和全局闭环（同时优化相机位姿和体素化3DGS）；2）引入颜色残差学习策略，加速体素化3DGS收敛并提升渲染质量。

Result: 在多种室内外数据集上，该方法在相机位姿估计精度和渲染质量上均达到SOTA，同时保持实时效率；在真实无人机主动重建系统中验证了鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: MoonSplat通过全局Sim(3)优化和颜色残差学习，有效解决了在线3DGS的位姿估计脆弱性和优化效率问题，为机器人、AR/VR等实际应用提供了可靠的在线3D重建方案。

Abstract: Online 3D reconstruction from monocular image sequences is a challenging and ongoing research topic. 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS), leveraging its high-quality real-time rendering capability, empowers online 3D reconstruction to represent dense scenes with enhanced expressiveness, and thus holds great promise for a wide range of applications such as robotics and AR/VR. However, existing online 3DGS methods still suffer from some key challenges: fragile camera pose estimation due to the lack of global optimization, and low optimization efficiency in large-scale or long-sequence scenarios. To address these issues, we propose a robust and efficient online voxelized 3DGS reconstruction framework integrated with global $\text{Sim}(3)$ optimization, which enables reliable camera tracking and efficient global loop closure for both camera poses and voxelized 3DGS. To accelerate the convergence of the voxelized 3DGS, we further introduce a color residual learning strategy, which not only boosts optimization speed but also enhances rendering quality. Extensive experiments on diverse indoor and outdoor datasets demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance in both camera pose estimation accuracy and rendering quality, while retaining real-time efficiency. Additionally, we develop and deploy a real-world UAV-based active reconstruction system grounded on our proposed method, validating its robustness and generalizability for practical online 3D reconstruction tasks. Our code and data are available at https://github.com/TrickyGo/MoonSplat.

</details>


### [114] [Plug-and-Adapt: Multimodal Coreference Resolution at First Sight with a Pretrained Alignment Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17950)
*Jinghan Wu,Jing Li,Ivor W. Tsang,Xuetao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种即插即用的多模态指代消解方法，通过预训练文本-视觉对齐模型并利用证据理论融合视觉和类别信息，无需目标数据集训练或依赖大型视觉语言模型，在CIN基准上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MCR方法需要目标数据集标注训练，泛化能力差；大型VLLMs虽具有零样本能力但体量巨大、部署困难或仅能通过付费API访问。因此需要一种无需训练、不依赖大模型、可直接使用的MCR方法。

Method: 1) 利用视觉-语言对齐数据集预训练细粒度文本与视觉上下文对齐模型；2) 通过证据理论融合视觉线索和类别信息进行相似度聚合，将对齐模型迁移至MCR任务。

Result: 在CIN基准上，CoNLL F1超越专用SOTA方法5.31%、超越流行VLLMs 2.12%；在掩码CIN和VCR-MCR数据集上验证了鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 所提方法在无需目标域训练和不依赖大型VLLMs的前提下，实现了MCR任务的性能提升，同时具有良好的鲁棒性和泛化性。

Abstract: Visual information helps resolve ambiguity in coreference resolution, leading to notable performance gains. However, existing Multi-modal Coreference Resolution (MCR) methods require training with (partially) annotated data from the target dataset before they can be applied, preventing their direct usability and raising concerns about generalization. While Vision-Language Large Models (VLLMs) with billions of parameters offer promising zero-shot capabilities, they remain largely inaccessible. Their massive size limits deployability, and many are only accessible through paid APIs. In this paper, we propose a plug-and-adapt method that strategically adapts a carefully pre-trained \emph{alignment model} for immediate use in MCR tasks, designed to eliminate the need for training on scarce benchmark datasets or relying on resource-intensive VLLMs. Specifically, we first pre-train a fine-grained alignment model between textual and visual contextual information using vision-language alignment datasets. We then repurpose the alignment model to MCR through similarity aggregation by fusing visual and categorical cues with evidence theory, thereby enhancing effectiveness. Experiments on the Coreference Image Narratives (CIN) benchmark dataset demonstrate the effectiveness of our method, achieving a 5.31\% and 2.12\% improvement in CoNLL F1 over SOTA dedicated methods and popular VLLMs, respectively. We further evaluate our method on a masked CIN dataset for robustness testing and on a specially constructed VCR-MCR dataset for generalization assessment, with results confirming both capabilities.

</details>


### [115] [Beyond Visual Cues: CoT-Enhanced Reasoning for Semi-supervised Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17958)
*Yuming Chen,Yuxin Xie,Tao Zhou,Yi Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CERS框架通过引入Chain-of-Thought推理和大语言模型生成的语义知识，解决半监督医学图像分割中视觉-语义不匹配的问题，在边界模糊和语义不一致场景下显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有半监督医学图像分割方法过度依赖像素级视觉相似性进行一致性正则化，在视觉-语义不匹配的临床场景（视觉相似的病灶需要不同诊断结论）中表现不佳，无法捕捉专家使用的底层诊断逻辑。

Method: 1) 构建由大语言模型生成的推理描述组成知识库；2) 语义感知参考选择策略，先按形态学筛选候选，再通过CoT一致性消除难负样本；3) 多尺度坐标注意力模块(MCAM)将推理上下文融合到解码过程。

Result: 在多项实验中，CERS优于最先进方法，尤其在解决边界模糊和语义不一致方面表现突出。

Conclusion: 超越纯视觉模式匹配，通过融入CoT推理语义知识有效提升了半监督医学图像分割在复杂临床场景下的性能。

Abstract: Semi-supervised medical image segmentation has emerged as a dominant research problem in medical image analysis, mitigating annotation scarcity by leveraging consistency regularization on unlabeled data. However, existing approaches operate predominantly via visual pattern matching, relying heavily on pixel-level similarities. This visual-centric dependency often falters in clinical scenarios characterized by the visual-semantic mismatch, where visually similar lesions warrant distinct diagnostic conclusions, thus failing to capture the underlying diagnostic logic used by experts. To address this, we move beyond visual cues and propose CERS (CoT-Enhanced Reasoning Segmentation), a framework that integrates Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning to distinguish pathologically distinct cases. Specifically, we construct a knowledge pool enriched with linguistic reasoning descriptions generated by large language models (LLMs). A semantic-aware reference selection strategy is introduced to identify historical evidence, filtering candidates first by morphology, and then refining them via CoT consistency to eliminate hard negatives. Furthermore, a multi-scale coordinate attention module (MCAM) is designed to effectively fuse this reasoning-derived context into the decoding process. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superiority of CERS against state-of-the-art approaches, particularly in resolving boundary ambiguities and semantic inconsistencies. The code is available at https://github.com/cymasuna/CERS.

</details>


### [116] [MLLMs Get It Right, Then Get It Wrong: Tracing and Correcting Late-Layer Textual Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17953)
*Xingming Li,Ao Cheng,Qiyao Sun,Xixiang He,Xuanyu Ji,Runke Huang,Qingyong Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 多模态大语言模型存在"后层文本覆盖"现象：模型初始层能基于视觉信息做出正确预测，但最后输出层被文本信息覆盖导致错误。通过检测预测方向变化可识别是否正确（85%失败转向文本，89%成功转向视觉），并提出无需训练的解码方法CALRD，恢复被覆盖的正确视觉预测，在五个模型上实现最高9.4%的绝对性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示多模态大语言模型偏向文本而忽视视觉信息的根本原因，发现这一问题源于模型后层对视觉信息的覆盖而非编码失败，并基于此发现设计轻量级干预方法。

Method: 通过分析模型中间层输出，发现"晚期文本覆盖"现象；基于预测方向变化（向文本或视觉偏移）作为正确性信号，提出Conflict-Aware Layer Reference Decoding (CALRD)，在推理时检测并恢复被抑制的正确视觉预测。

Result: 在五种不同架构的多模态大语言模型上，针对冲突场景的基准测试实现最高9.4%的绝对性能提升，同时基本保持标准任务性能不变。

Conclusion: 模型并非缺乏视觉理解能力，而是未能将已编码的正确视觉信息保留到最终输出。CALRD无需训练或外部知识，通过恢复模型自身已知道但未保留的正确预测，有效缓解文本偏见问题。

Abstract: When vision contradicts text, multimodal large language models (MLLMs) consistently favor text, even when images provide clear evidence otherwise. This bias poses risks for applications requiring visual grounding, yet its cause remains unclear. In this paper, we uncover a surprising finding: models often get it right initially, forming correct vision-based predictions in their intermediate layers, before changing their minds and favoring text in the final output. We call this "late-layer textual override". The visual information is encoded, it simply does not survive to the output. More intriguingly, we find that how predictions change reveals whether they're correct: 85% of failures shift toward text, while 89% of successes shift toward vision. This directional signature enables a simple but powerful intervention: when we detect a confident visual prediction being suppressed, we restore it. We propose CALRD (Conflict-Aware Layer Reference Decoding), a training-free method that recovers overridden predictions at inference time. Experiments across five MLLMs of varying architectures demonstrate up to 9.4% absolute improvements on conflict benchmarks while largely preserving standard performance, without training or external knowledge. It recovers what the model already knew but failed to preserve.

</details>


### [117] [Robustness of Similarity-based Positional Encoding Under Rotations: Theoretical Analysis and Experimental Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17961)
*Andrea Santomauro,Luigi Portinale,Giorgio Leonardi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究相似性位置编码（simPE）在旋转变换下的鲁棒性，通过理论证明和实验验证表明，simPE虽非旋转不变，但在温和Lipschitz假设下对旋转扰动具有稳定性，并在四个数据集上优于标准位置编码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: simPE最初为医学影像设计，对几何鲁棒性有重要需求，但其在几何扰动（特别是旋转）下的理论行为尚未被充分刻画。

Method: 首先理论上证明simPE不具备旋转不变性，然后在温和Lipschitz假设下推导其关于旋转扰动的Frobenius范数显式界；实验上在四个可控数据集上比较simPE与标准可学习位置编码在不同旋转角度下的性能。

Result: simPE在所有数据集上，尤其在中小旋转角度范围内，在准确率、F1分数、精确率和召回率上均一致优于标准可学习位置编码，验证了理论稳定性保证。

Conclusion: simPE对旋转扰动具有理论保证的稳定性，尽管不是严格旋转不变，但在实际应用中能有效抵抗几何扰动，优于标准位置编码。

Abstract: Positional encoding is a fundamental component of Transformer architectures, as it injects information about the spatial or sequential arrangement of inputs. Among recent alternatives to standard absolute and sinusoidal encodings, similarity-based positional encoding (simPE) has emerged as a flexible framework for representing positional structure through pairwise relations. simPE was originally designed for medical imaging applications, where geometric robustness is especially relevant: small rotations naturally arise during image acquisition, induced by imaging instruments, patient positioning, or slight acquisition misalignments. Despite its empirical promise, the theoretical behavior of simPE under geometric perturbations has not been fully characterized. In this paper, we study the robustness of simPE with respect to rotations, combining formal theoretical analysis with experimental validation. We first show that simPE is generally not rotation-invariant. We then prove that, under mild Lipschitz assumptions on the elementary components, simPE is stable under rotational perturbations and derive explicit perturbation bounds in Frobenius norm. We validate these findings experimentally on four controlled datasets--a synthetic Arrow dataset, a synthetic Shapes dataset (four geometric shape categories), a synthetic Digits dataset, and a benchmark image classification dataset (FashionMNIST)--in which training and validation images are kept in a fixed canonical orientation while test images are subjected to increasing rotation angles. Across all datasets, simPE consistently outperforms standard learned positional encoding in terms of accuracy, F1 score, precision, and recall under rotation, particularly in the small-to-moderate angle regime, corroborating the theoretical stability guarantees.

</details>


### [118] [Reload-Mamba: Hierarchical Anti-Dilution State-Space Modeling for Multi-Class Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17966)
*Sheng-Wei Chan,Hsin-Jui Pan,Jen-Shiun Chiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Reload-Mamba 是一个基于 Mamba 状态空间模型的语义分割框架，通过边界监督的局部细节先验、类别不确定性感知的 Reload Gate 和层级多级 Reload 机制解决状态空间传播导致的响应稀释问题，在 ADE20K、Cityscapes 和 PASCAL VOC 2012 上取得了优异的分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mamba 状态空间模型虽能实现线性时间的长程建模，但其顺序状态传播会削弱高分辨率密集预测中关键的边界敏感和细节敏感响应，影响多类语义分割性能。

Method: 提出三个分割专用设计：(i) 边界监督的局部细节先验，使用真实边界掩码训练以识别需响应恢复的区域；(ii) 类别不确定性感知的 Reload Gate，利用预重载辅助头的逐像素类别熵作为额外门控信号；(iii) 层级多级 Reload 机制，在三个解码器层级应用抗稀释细化并自上而下融合恢复的表征。整体采用 ConvNeXt-Tiny 编码器、多尺度解码器和四方向 Mamba 扫描。

Result: ADE20K 上单尺度 47.9% mIoU（多尺度 48.9%），Cityscapes 上单尺度 83.2% mIoU，PASCAL VOC 2012 val 上达 87.8% mIoU。三个设计累积提升直接移植基线 +2.2 mIoU。

Conclusion: 所提三项分割专用设计均有效贡献于性能提升，Reload-Mamba 框架在多个语义分割基准上达到先进水平。

Abstract: Mamba-based state space models offer linear-time long-range modeling for high-resolution dense prediction, but sequential state-space propagation can attenuate boundary-sensitive and detail-sensitive responses that are critical in multi-class semantic segmentation. We propose Reload-Mamba, a semantic segmentation framework that addresses this propagation-induced response dilution through three segmentation-specific designs: (i) a boundary-supervised local detail prior that is explicitly trained with ground-truth boundary masks to identify regions requiring response restoration; (ii) a class-uncertainty-aware Reload Gate that incorporates per-pixel class entropy from a pre-reload auxiliary head as an additional gating signal, a formulation that is informative only under multi-class dense prediction; and (iii) a hierarchical multi-level Reload mechanism that applies anti-dilution refinement at three decoder levels and fuses the restored representations top-down. Built upon a ConvNeXt-Tiny encoder with a multi-scale decoder and four-directional Mamba scanning with pixel-wise directional attention, Reload-Mamba achieves 47.9% single-scale (48.9% multi-scale) mIoU on ADE20K and 83.2% single-scale mIoU on Cityscapes. With ResNet-101 + COCO pre-training under the standard DeepLab-style protocol, Reload-Mamba reaches 87.8% mIoU on PASCAL VOC 2012 val. Controlled ablations show that each of the three segmentation-specific designs contributes beyond a direct port of the prior anti-dilution architecture proposed for binarization, cumulatively improving over the direct-port baseline by +2.2 mIoU on ADE20K.

</details>


### [119] [When LLMs Analyze Scars: From Images to Clinically-Meaningful Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18063)
*Ruman Wang,Hangting Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ScaFE框架，将大语言模型重新定位为知识驱动的特征工程师，通过生成可执行的Python代码来提取临床可解释的低维特征，在病理疤痕分类等小样本场景下优于端到端深度学习方法，同时具备数据效率高、隐私保护好和可解释性强的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分类面临数据稀缺的根本困境（标注成本高、隐私限制、疾病罕见），尤其是在病理性疤痕分类中，区分瘢痕疙瘩与增生性疤痕需要专业专家知识，且标注图像极其有限。现有深度学习方法在大规模数据下表现优异，但在小样本临床场景中效果不佳。

Method: 提出ScaFE（Scar Feature Engineering）框架，将LLM作为知识驱动的特征工程师而非端到端分类器。利用LLM编码的丰富医学知识，通过提示工程结合既定的疤痕评估标准（如温哥华疤痕量表），生成确定性的Python特征提取代码，将高维图像转化为低维、临床可解释的特征表示。

Result: 在疤痕分类任务上的大量实验表明，在有限数据条件下，该方法一致性地优于端到端深度学习基线以及将LLM作为黑盒分类器的方案。

Conclusion: ScaFE展示了将LLM整合到数据高效且临床透明的医学AI系统中的有前景方向，具有三个关键优势：(1)数据效率，通过将知识获取与统计学习解耦；(2)隐私保护，原始图像在本地处理不暴露给外部LLM；(3)可解释性，基于临床推理的显式特征。

Abstract: Medical image classification faces a fundamental dilemma: while deep learning models achieve remarkable performance at scale, real-world clinical scenarios often suffer from severe data scarcity due to annotation costs, privacy constraints, and disease rarity. This challenge is particularly pronounced in pathological scar classification, where differentiating keloids from hypertrophic scars requires subtle expert knowledge and labeled images are extremely limited. We propose a novel paradigm that repositions large language models (LLMs) as knowledge-driven feature engineers rather than end-to-end classifiers. We call this framework ScaFE (Scar Feature Engineering). Our key insight is that LLMs encode rich medical knowledge that can be externalized as executable feature extraction code, enabling the transformation of high-dimensional images into low-dimensional, clinically interpretable representations. Specifically, we prompt an LLM with established scar assessment criteria to generate deterministic Python code that extracts features aligned with clinical scoring systems such as the Vancouver Scar Scale. Our approach offers three key advantages: (1) data efficiency, achieving robust performance with limited training samples by decoupling knowledge acquisition from statistical learning; (2) privacy preservation, as raw images are processed locally without exposure to external LLMs; and (3) interpretability, through explicit features grounded in clinical reasoning. Extensive experiments on scar classification demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms end-to-end deep learning baselines or using LLMs as black-box classifiers under limited data conditions, establishing a promising direction for integrating LLMs into data-efficient and clinically transparent medical AI systems.

</details>


### [120] [SegDINO: Introducing Multi-Scale Structure into DINO for Efficient Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17972)
*Sicheng Yang,Hongqiu Wang,Zhaohu Xing,Sixiang Chen,Qiuxia Yang,Yize Mao,Guang Yang,Lei Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SegDINO是一个基于DINOv3骨干网络的高效分割框架，通过Token Pyramid Adaptation（TPA）重组中间特征形成伪多尺度层次结构，并结合Scale-Aware Decoding（SAD）进行尺度内细化和自上而下的多尺度传播，在多个基准上实现SOTA结果且参数量更少。同时提出了包含284例患者胰腺肿瘤标注的新数据集PanCT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督DINO模型直接用于图像分割时，通常依赖带有复杂上采样的重型解码器，引入大量参数和计算开销。作者观察到在DINO特征中引入尺度信息比增加解码器容量更为关键，因此希望设计一个轻量级的高效分割框架。

Method: 1) Token Pyramid Adaptation (TPA)：将DINOv3中间特征重组为伪多尺度层次结构；2) Scale-Aware Decoding (SAD)：实现高效的尺度内细化和自上而下的多尺度传播；3) 构建PanCT数据集：包含284例患者、专家标注的胰腺肿瘤CT数据集，用于评估模型处理困难小病灶的能力。

Result: 在PanCT和三个公开基准上进行的大量实验表明，SegDINO在高效的前提下达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 在DINO特征中引入尺度信息比重型解码器更重要，SegDINO通过轻量级尺度建模实现了高效且SOTA的分割性能。

Abstract: Self-supervised DINO models provide strong transferable visual representations, yet applying them directly to image segmentation remains challenging. Existing approaches commonly rely on heavy decoders with complex upsampling, introducing substantial parameter and computational overhead. We observe that introducing scale into DINO features is far more critical than increasing decoder capacity. In this work, we present SegDINO, an efficient segmentation framework that integrates a DINOv3 backbone with lightweight scale modeling. SegDINO introduces Token Pyramid Adaptation (TPA) to reorganize intermediate DINO features into a pseudo multi-scale hierarchy, and Scale-Aware Decoding (SAD) for efficient intra-scale refinement and top-down multi-scale propagation. We further curate PanCT, a new CT dataset containing 284 patients with expert-annotated pancreatic tumors, to assess SegDINO's ability to handle difficult small-lesion cases. Extensive experiments on PanCT and three public benchmarks demonstrate that SegDINO achieves state-of-the-art results with high efficiency. The code is available at https://github.com/script-Yang/segdino_v2.

</details>


### [121] [Gaussian Light Field Splatting: A Physical Prior-Driven Vision Transformer for Unsupervised Low-Light Image Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17985)
*Yuhan Chen,Wenxuan Yu,Guofa Li,Fuchen Li,Kunyang Huang,Yicui Shi,Ying Fang,Wenbo Chu,Keqiang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GLFS，一种基于高斯光场溅射的Vision Transformer，通过将连续物理光照建模（各向异性高斯基函数叠加）引入Transformer架构，结合物理引导的自注意力机制和颜色-向量角损失+亮度-边缘损失，解决了非均匀光照下的局部曝光失衡和颜色失真问题，在低光图像增强上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督低光图像增强方法在复杂非均匀光照下存在局部曝光失衡和颜色失真；多数Vision Transformer缺乏对光照退化物理先验的显式建模机制。

Method: 提出GLFS框架：1) 用各向异性高斯基函数叠加表示场景光照；2) 在自注意力中引入物理引导偏置，自适应推断空间增益场；3) 设计颜色-向量角损失和亮度-边缘损失，分别约束色调一致性和局部细节结构保真度。

Result: 大量消融实验和定量评估表明GLFS在光照校正和细节保持上具有明显优势，达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: GLFS为低光图像增强提供了新的表示范式，有效解决了非均匀光照下的曝光失衡和颜色失真问题。

Abstract: Existing unsupervised low-light image enhancement methods often encounter local exposure imbalance and color distortion under complex non-uniform illumination. In addition, most Vision Transformers lack an explicit mechanism for modeling the physical priors of illumination degradation. To address these limitations, we propose GLFS, a Gaussian light field splatting-based Vision Transformer that integrates continuous physical illumination modeling from Gaussian splatting into the Transformer architecture. In GLFS, scene illumination is represented by a superposition of anisotropic Gaussian basis functions. Physics-guided biases are introduced into self-attention to adaptively infer a spatial gain field, enabling accurate and uniform restoration under complex illumination. To reduce color bias and structural degradation during enhancement, a color-vector angular loss and a luminance-edge loss are further developed. These losses enforce hue consistency and improve the structural fidelity of local details. Extensive ablation studies and quantitative evaluations show that GLFS provides clear advantages in illumination correction and detail preservation. It achieves state-of-the-art performance and offers a new representation paradigm for low-light image enhancement.

</details>


### [122] [Recover Semantics First, Generate Better: Improved Latent Modeling for 3D MRI Reconstruction and Cross-Contrast Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17989)
*Yonghao Chen,Sicheng Yang,Rui Tang,Lei Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种语义优先的潜在空间建模框架（RSF），用于3D MRI重建和跨对比度合成。该框架包含潜在谐波编码器（LHE）捕获全局解剖依赖、语义恢复模块（SRB）注入高层语义先验，以及解剖感知频率损失（AFL）保留诊断相关的高频结构，有效解决了现有压缩架构中长程解剖连贯性不足、语义信息丢失和重建过度平滑的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D MRI跨对比度合成方法先压缩体积到潜在空间再训练生成模型，但现有压缩架构存在三个关键问题：未能保留长程解剖连贯性、丢失临床有意义的语义信息、优化目标导致重建过度平滑，这些缺陷最终损害了后续生成模型的性能。

Method: 提出语义优先的潜在建模框架（RSF），包含三个核心组件：1) 潜在谐波编码器（LHE），用于捕获全局解剖依赖关系，确保体素表示的一致性；2) 语义恢复模块（SRB），从自监督语义教师注入高层先验，增强潜在空间中对比度感知的可分离性；3) 解剖感知频率损失（AFL），自适应保留诊断相关的高频结构。

Result: 在两个公开多对比度MRI数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在重建保真度和跨对比度合成质量上均取得了一致的改进。

Conclusion: 本文提出的语义优先潜在建模框架有效解决了现有3D MRI压缩架构的关键缺陷，显著提升了跨对比度合成质量，代码已开源。

Abstract: Multi-contrast magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) provides complementary information for clinical diagnosis. However, acquiring all MRI sequences is often time-consuming and costly. Recent generative models perform cross-contrast synthesis to address this issue by inferring absent contrasts from the available ones. Nevertheless, synthesizing 3D MRI presents significant challenges. Due to the massive volume sizes, operating directly in the pixel space is computationally prohibitive; therefore, a common approach is to first compress the 3D volumes into a latent space and subsequently train generative models in that space. We observe that existing compression architectures face several critical issues: they under-preserve long-range anatomical coherence, discard clinically meaningful semantics, and rely on optimization objectives that lead to over-smoothed reconstructions. Ultimately, these shortcomings compromise the performance of subsequent generative models. In this work, we propose a semantics-first latent modeling framework for 3D MRI reconstruction and cross-contrast synthesis. Specifically, we introduce a Latent Harmonization Encoder (LHE) to capture global anatomical dependencies, ensuring coherent volumetric representations. To mitigate semantic degradation during latent compression, we further design a Semantic Recovery Block (SRB) that injects high-level priors from a self-supervised semantic teacher, enhancing contrast-aware separability in the latent space. Additionally, we propose an Anatomy-aware Frequency Loss (AFL) to adaptively preserve diagnostically relevant high-frequency structures. Extensive experiments on two public multi-contrast MRI datasets demonstrate consistent improvements in reconstruction fidelity and cross-contrast synthesis quality. Our code is available at https://github.com/script-Yang/RSF.

</details>


### [123] [AIGS-Net: Compact Illumination Field Modeling via 2D Gaussian Splatting for Fast Low-Light Image Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17998)
*Yuhan Chen,Kunyang Huang,Fuchen Li,Zhuohan Qin,Guofa Li,Wenbo Chu,Keqiang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AIGS-Net，一种超轻量级自适应照明高斯泼溅网络，仅需约40个可训练参数即可实现快速低光图像增强，在保持高效推理的同时提升细节恢复和色彩保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低光图像增强方法在光照场建模的表征能力与计算复杂度之间存在瓶颈，亟需一种能兼顾增强质量与极端推理效率的轻量级解决方案。

Method: 构建输入自适应的2D高斯泼溅光照场，通过相对亮度统计动态调制高斯基函数的不透明度，并采用有序alpha混合渲染空间变化的光照补偿；引入零参数非线性多尺度上下文编码模块提取低频结构和局部对比度线索；集成噪声掩码估计、锁定单通道Gamma映射、跨通道一致性正则化和目标颜色对齐约束来抑制噪声和色偏。

Result: 在LOL和LSRW基准测试上，AIGS-Net仅需约40个可学习参数，在细节恢复和色彩保真度方面优于现有方法，实现了增强质量与推理效率的有效权衡。

Conclusion: AIGS-Net通过自适应高斯泼溅光照场和零参数特征提取模块，以极低的参数成本实现了高质量的低光图像增强，为资源受限场景下的实时增强提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Existing low-light image enhancement methods often face a bottleneck between the representation capacity of illumination-field modeling and computational complexity. To address this issue, this paper proposes an Adaptive Illumination Gaussian Splatting Network (AIGS-Net), an ultra-lightweight architecture for fast low-light enhancement. Unlike conventional static priors, AIGS-Net constructs an input-adaptive 2D Gaussian Splatting illumination field. The opacity of Gaussian basis functions is dynamically modulated by relative luminance statistics of the input image, and spatially varying illumination compensation is rendered through ordered alpha compositing. To guide adaptive illumination compensation efficiently, a zero-parameter nonlinear multiscale contextual encoding module is introduced to extract low-frequency structures and local contrast cues without additional convolutional weights. To suppress noise amplification and sensor-induced color bias, AIGS-Net integrates noise-mask estimation, locked single-channel Gamma mapping, cross-channel consistency regularization, and target color-alignment constraints. Experiments on LOL and LSRW benchmarks show that AIGS-Net improves detail recovery and color fidelity while requiring only approximately 40 learnable parameters, achieving an effective trade-off between enhancement quality and extreme inference efficiency.

</details>


### [124] [PhaseWin: An Efficient Search Algorithm for Faithful Visual Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18008)
*Zihan Gu,Ruoyu Chen,Junchi Zhang,Li Liu,Xiaochun Cao,Hua Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PhaseWin，一种用于忠实视觉归因的高效子集搜索算法。PhaseWin将贪心区域选择重组织为分阶段窗口搜索过程，通过交替进行全局候选筛选、自适应剪枝和局部窗口精炼，将计算复杂度从O(n²)降至O(n)，同时保持接近贪心搜索的忠实性保证。在图像分类、目标检测、视觉定位和图像描述等任务上的实验表明，PhaseWin在达到高忠实度的同时使用了最少的前向传播次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉归因旨在解释模型决策如何依赖于输入图像的局部区域。将忠实归因建模为有序子集搜索问题时，穷举搜索代价指数级，贪心搜索虽广泛使用但仍需二次数量的模型评估（每步需重新评分所有剩余候选）。现有方法面临计算效率与归因忠实性之间的权衡，亟需一种更高效的算法。

Method: PhaseWin将贪心区域选择重组织为分阶段窗口搜索过程：交替执行全局候选筛选、自适应剪枝和局部窗口精炼，避免每步重新评估全部候选集，同时保留贪心搜索的关键区域排序行为。在单调证据累积条件下，利用特征级结构假设，实现了可控的线性评估复杂度。

Result: 在图像分类、目标检测、视觉定位和图像描述等任务上的大量实验表明，PhaseWin在所有对比的归因方法中，以最少的前向传播次数达到了最高的忠实度，经验性地实现了从O(n²)到O(n)的复杂度降低。

Conclusion: PhaseWin通过分阶段窗口搜索机制有效解决了视觉归因中忠实性与计算效率的权衡问题，将复杂度从二次降至线性，并在多个视觉任务上验证了其优越性，为大规模模型的忠实归因提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Visual attribution is a fundamental tool for interpreting modern vision and vision-language models, particularly when their decisions must be inspected, diagnosed, or audited. Its goal is to explain how a model's decision depends on local regions of the visual input, typically by assigning an importance ordering over candidate image regions. Given an image partitioned into $n$ regions, faithful attribution can be cast as an ordered subset-search problem, in which progressively inserting the selected regions should recover the target model response as early as possible. Exhaustive search over region subsets incurs exponential cost, while the widely used greedy search still requires a quadratic number of model evaluations, because every selection step rescores all remaining candidates. We propose PhaseWin, an efficient subset-search algorithm for faithful visual attribution. PhaseWin reorganizes greedy region selection into a phased window-search procedure: rather than re-evaluating the full candidate set at every step, it alternates between global candidate screening, adaptive pruning, and localized window refinement, while preserving the essential region-ranking behavior of greedy search. We analyze PhaseWin under monotone evidence-accumulation conditions and show that, under feature-level structural assumptions, it attains controllable linear evaluation complexity together with near-greedy faithfulness guarantees. Extensive experiments on image classification, object detection, visual grounding, and image captioning show that, among all compared attribution methods, PhaseWin reaches high faithfulness with the fewest forward passes, empirically realizing the predicted reduction from $O(n^2)$ to $O(n)$. The code is available at https://github.com/Qihuai27/phasewin-va.

</details>


### [125] [SkillMoV: Mixture-of-View Routing with Prototype-Conditioned Gating for Unified Multi-View Proficiency Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17615)
*Edoardo Bianchi,Antonio Liotta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SkillMoV提出了一种基于混合视图投影器（MoVP）的统一、参数高效的框架，用于从同步多视角视频中进行多场景技能水平评估，在EgoExo4D数据集上Exos设置下达到50.17%准确率，超过最强基线3.57个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法往往针对单一场景或依赖共享的多视图聚合，难以适应异构摄像头视角和活动领域，因此亟需一种统一的、可跨场景和视角泛化的技能评估方法。

Method: SkillMoV的核心是Mixture-of-View Projector (MoVP)，包含四阶段：(i) 混合视图软路由器（12个专家MLP）学习视图依赖的专家偏好；(ii) 跨视图注意力对齐同步摄像头；(iii) 可学习原型锚定，以类级参考向量条件化表示；(iv) 原型条件门控投影生成最终技能嵌入。采用LoRA进行参数高效微调。

Result: 在EgoExo4D六个技能域上，Exos设置下整体准确率50.17%，超过最强基线3.57个百分点；Ego+Exos设置下47.63%（最佳为48.20%）。消融实验显示：MoV路由贡献+6.61pp，跨视图注意力+4.92pp，原型锚定+4.07pp，随机视图丢弃+3.90pp。仅训练23.32%参数。

Conclusion: SkillMoV通过MoVP有效实现了多视角、多场景下的技能水平统一评估，在参数高效的同时取得了优异的性能，验证了混合视图专家范式在技能评估任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Estimating human proficiency from video is a key challenge for automated skill assessment, with applications in sports coaching, music pedagogy, surgical training, and workplace learning. Existing approaches often focus on individual scenarios or rely on shared multi-view aggregation, limiting their ability to adapt to heterogeneous camera viewpoints and activity domains. We introduce SkillMoV, a unified, parameter-efficient framework for multi-scenario proficiency estimation from synchronized multi-view video. At its core, SkillMoV introduces a Mixture-of-View Projector (MoVP), which adapts the mixture-of-experts paradigm to camera-specific view features. MoVP is composed of four stages: (i) a Mixture-of-View soft router with twelve expert MLPs that learns view-dependent expert preferences without camera-identity supervision; (ii) cross-view attention to align synchronized cameras; (iii) learnable prototype anchoring to condition the representation on class-level reference vectors; and (iv) a prototype-conditioned gated projection that produces the final skill embedding. We evaluate SkillMoV on EgoExo4D across six skill domains and three separately trained view configurations: Ego, Exos, and Ego+Exos. SkillMoV reaches 50.17% overall accuracy in the Exos setting with a single model trained jointly across all scenarios, surpassing the strongest reported Exos result among the compared methods by 3.57 percentage points. In Ego+Exos, SkillMoV remains close to the best reported result in that setting (47.63% versus 48.20%). Ablations on the selected Exos configuration validate each component: MoV routing contributes +6.61 pp over attentive aggregation, cross-view attention +4.92 pp, prototype anchoring +4.07 pp, and stochastic view dropout +3.90 pp. Through LoRA adaptation, SkillMoV trains only 23.32% of its parameters and adds limited measured overhead relative to a LoRA-only baseline.

</details>


### [126] [Divide, Deliberate, Decide: A Multi-Agent Framework for Fine-Grained Egocentric Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17627)
*Alessandro Sottovia,Alessandro Torcinovich,Oswald Lanz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Divide, Deliberate, Decide框架，一种全本地、零样本的多智能体方法，通过异构VLM专家协作辩论和Borda计数聚合，在无需微调的情况下提升细粒度第一人称视频动作识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一人称视频中的细粒度动作识别对视觉语言模型具有挑战性：不同动作往往仅存在微小的视觉线索差异，而单一模型容易对某一部分线索产生偏差。

Method: (i) VLM编排器将视频分块并为每段提出top-k候选标签列表；(ii) 来自不同开源模型家族的异构VLM专家进行结构化辩论，包括一轮同行咨询问答；(iii) 使用Borda计数聚合专家排名，编排器根据专家证据重新排序自身预测。整个流程本地运行，无需微调。

Result: 该方法在零样本动作识别性能上相比基线有正向提升，证明了异构辩论步骤的有效性，并且性能增益源于去相关的模型先验而非额外的计算量。

Conclusion: 通过多智能体协作辩论和聚合策略，可以有效利用异构模型的互补优势，在无需微调的条件下提升细粒度动作识别的准确性。

Abstract: Fine-grained action recognition in egocentric video is challenging for Vision-Language Models (VLMs): actions often differ only in small visual cues, and a single model tends to be biased toward a subset of these cues. We propose Divide, Deliberate, Decide, a fully-local, zero-shot multi-agent framework in which (i) a VLM orchestrator chunks the video and proposes a top-k candidate label list per segment, (ii) an ensemble of heterogeneous VLM specialists, drawn from different open model families, engages in a structured deliberation that includes a peer-consultation round of questions, and (iii) agent rankings are aggregated with a Borda count and the orchestrator re-ranks its own prediction in light of the specialists' evidence. The entire pipeline runs locally with no fine-tuning. Experiments show that our method positively improves zero-shot action recognition performance over the baseline, highlighting the influence of a heterogeneous deliberation step, showing that the gain stems from decorrelated model priors rather than from additional compute.

</details>


### [127] [HLS-GPT: A Generative Pretrained Transformer (GPT) for Continental-Scale NASA Harmonized Landsat and Sentinel-2 (HLS) Reflectance Reconstruction Across All Bands on Arbitrary Dates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18115)
*Junjie Li,Hankui K. Zhang,David P. Roy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HLS-GPT，一个大规模生成式预训练Transformer模型，用于重建NASA Harmonized Landsat Sentinel-2 30米地表反射率数据，涵盖所有波段、任意日期和任意像素位置。模型采用层次化Transformer架构处理不同光谱波段配置，基于单像素12个月时间序列，使用美国本土超过25万训练像素的9年HLS数据进行训练，通过随机裁剪和掩蔽策略实现鲁棒重建。在超过6.2万独立测试像素上，所有HLS光谱波段的重建RMSE低于0.026，且优于两种传统方法和NASA-IBM Prithvi模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习方法在Landsat和Sentinel-2反射率时间序列重建方面存在光谱覆盖受限、地理可扩展性有限或基于patch的短时间上下文设计等问题，缺乏能够覆盖所有波段、任意日期和任意像素位置的大规模重建模型。

Method: 提出HLS-GPT模型，采用层次化Transformer架构处理Landsat和Sentinel-2的不同光谱波段配置；基于单像素12个月时间序列；使用美国本土超过25万像素的9年HLS数据训练；采用随机裁剪和掩蔽策略（掩蔽50%的有效观测）训练模型重建掩蔽值。

Result: 在超过6.2万独立测试像素上，留一观测值评估中所有HLS光谱波段重建RMSE低于0.026；可见波段相对RMSE低于35%，其他波段低于13%；红边波段误差与红波段和近红外波段相当；掩蔽10%至50%时所有波段RMSE低于0.028；在9个独立CONUS HLS图幅上优于两种传统方法和Prithvi模型。

Conclusion: HLS-GPT能够在大规模地理范围内实现高精度、鲁棒的HLS地表反射率重建，尤其在复杂作物物候和稀疏不规则观测条件下表现良好，为遥感时间序列重建提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent deep learning methods for Landsat and Sentinel-2 reflectance time series reconstruction remain limited by restricted spectral coverage, limited geographic scalability, or patch-based designs with short temporal contexts. We present HLS-GPT, a large-scale generative pretrained Transformer model for reconstructing NASA Harmonized Landsat Sentinel-2 30 m surface reflectance for all bands, any date, and any pixel location. HLS-GPT uses a hierarchical Transformer architecture to handle the different spectral band configurations of Landsat and Sentinel-2 and operates on single-pixel 12-month time series. To capture geographic and seasonal variability, the model was trained with nine years of HLS time series from more than 0.25 million training pixels across the conterminous United States. A random cropping and masking strategy extracts 12-month periods with varying start dates across epochs, masks 50% of valid observations, and trains the model to reconstruct the masked reflectance values from the remaining observations. Evaluation using more than 62,000 independent test pixels shows robust reconstruction under diverse land surface conditions, including complex crop phenology and sparse, irregular observations. Leave-one-observation-out evaluation achieved reconstruction RMSE below 0.026 for all HLS spectral bands, with relative RMSE below 35% for visible bands and below 13% for other bands. Red-edge band errors were comparable to red and near-infrared errors despite the absence of red-edge bands on Landsat. Sensitivity analyses that randomly masked 10% to 90% of test observations showed only modest degradation when 10% to 50% of observations were masked, with all-band RMSE below 0.028. Image reconstruction over nine independent 109 by 109 km CONUS HLS tiles further demonstrates that HLS-GPT outperforms two conventional methods and the NASA-IBM Prithvi model.

</details>


### [128] [Predicting Immune Biomarkers with MultiModal Mixture-of-Expert Pathology Foundation Models Empowers Precision Oncology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18123)
*Tianyu Liu,Ziqing Wang,Zhaokang Liang,Tong Ding,Peter Humphrey,Lorraine Colón-Cartagena,Emily Ling-Lin Pai,Kenneth Tou En Chang,Mohamed Kahila,Jonathan Chong Kai Liew,Tinglin Huang,Rex Ying,Kaize Ding,Faisal Mahmood,Wengong Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MixTIME是一个多模态基础模型，采用混合专家（MoE）架构整合不同模态的病理基础模型（图像、图像-文本、图像-转录组），能够从HE全切片图像中预测多重免疫荧光（mIF）蛋白表达，并在17个蛋白标志物上取得最优性能，同时增强空间域识别、生存预测和病理报告生成等下游任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测肿瘤免疫微环境（TIME）免疫生物标志物的方法主要局限于单图像模态，存在分辨率不足、临床和生物学信息利用不完整的问题，亟需一个能够整合多模态信息的可扩展框架。

Method: MixTIME采用混合专家（MoE）架构，动态集成三种病理基础模型（仅图像UNIv2、图像文本CONCHv1.5、图像转录组STPath）；使用可学习路由器动态加权专家贡献；采用分布和趋势感知的损失函数进行训练，实现像素级和切片级的mIF蛋白表达预测。

Result: 在两个不同规模的数据集上，MixTIME在17个蛋白标志物上达到了最先进的相关性指标。预测的mIF谱显著提升了空间域识别、生存预测和AI辅助病理报告生成等下游任务，并实现了临床时间点的蛋白表达纵向追踪，揭示了与药物耐药和免疫抑制相关的蛋白-基因相互作用模式。

Conclusion: MixTIME为计算病理学中的多模态生物标志物发现和临床转化提供了一个可扩展的框架。

Abstract: Predicting immune biomarkers associated with the tumor immune microenvironment (TIME) is critical for advancing precision oncology, yet existing approaches are largely limited to single image modalities and suffer from insufficient resolution and incomplete utilization of complementary clinical and biological information. Here we introduce MixTIME, a multimodal foundation model that leverages a mixture-of-experts (MoE) architecture to integrate pathology foundation models trained across distinct modalities: image only (UNIv2), image text (CONCHv1.5), and image transcriptomic (STPath) representations for pixel-level and slide-level prediction of multiplex immunofluorescence (mIF) protein expression from hematoxylin and eosin (HE) whole-slide images. MixTIME employs a learnable router to dynamically weight expert contributions and is trained with a distribution- and tendency-aware loss function. Benchmarked on two datasets of different scales, MixTIME achieves state-of-the-art performance across 17 protein markers as measured by correlation metrics. The predicted mIF profiles substantially enhance downstream tasks, including spatial domain identification, survival prediction, and AI-assisted pathology report generation validated by expert pathologists from multiple institutes across the world. Furthermore, MixTIME enables longitudinal tracking of protein expression dynamics across clinical time points and reveals protein gene interaction patterns linked to drug resistance and immune suppression in tumor microenvironments. Collectively, MixTIME provides a scalable framework for multimodal biomarker discovery and clinical translation in computational pathology.

</details>


### [129] [Adaptive Volumetric Mechanical Property Fields Invariant to Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18231)
*Rishit Dagli,Donglai Xiang,Vismay Modi,Xuning Yang,Gavriel State,David I. W. Levin,Maria Shugrina*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AdaVoMP通过稀疏自适应体素结构和自回归Transformer，将3D物体的材料属性（杨氏模量、泊松比、密度）预测分辨率提升至先前方法的16³倍，同时提高精度和内存效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数3D资产缺乏准确的机械属性（杨氏模量E、泊松比ν、密度ρ），而这些属性对数字世界的物理仿真至关重要。现有方法在分辨率、精度和内存效率上存在不足。

Method: 提出稀疏自适应体素结构SAV，用于高效表示输入3D形状和材料场；用稀疏Transformer编码器-解码器模型替代先前VoMP方法的固定体素模型，通过自回归方式为每个输入形状生成唯一的SAV来表示其材料属性。

Result: 分辨率达到先前方法的16³倍；在测试时计算量更少的情况下，估计出更准确的体积属性。

Conclusion: AdaVoMP能将高分辨率复杂3D物体转化为可直接用于仿真的资产，实现逼真的可变形仿真。

Abstract: Accurate mechanical properties (or materials) Young's modulus ($E$), Poisson's ratio ($ν$) and density ($ρ$) are essential for reliable physics simulation of digital worlds, but most 3D assets lack this information. We propose AdaVoMP, a method for predicting accurate dense spatially-varying ($E$, $ν$, $ρ$) for input 3D objects across representations, improving the resolution, accuracy, and memory efficiency over the state-of-the-art. The foundation of our technique is a sparse and adaptive voxel structure SAV that efficiently represents both the input 3D shape and the material field output. We replace the fixed-voxel model of the most accurate prior method, VoMP, with a novel sparse transformer encoder-decoder model that learns to generate a unique SAV autoregressively for every input shape to represent its materials, achieving a resolution $16^3\times$ higher than prior art. Experiments show that AdaVoMP estimates more accurate volumetric properties, even with lesser test-time compute than all prior art. This allows us to convert high-resolution complex 3D objects into simulation-ready assets, resulting in realistic deformable simulations.

</details>


### [130] [Neural Tree Reconstruction for the Open Forest Observatory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18153)
*Marissa Ramirez de Chanlatte,Arjun Rewari,Trevor Darrell,Derek J. N. Young*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Open Forest Observatory (OFO) 是一个跨大学合作项目，旨在为生态学家、土地管理者和公众提供低成本森林测绘工具。当前使用经典的运动恢复结构（SfM）技术生成3D树图，但该方法存在伪影、细节不足等问题。本文探索将神经辐射场（NeRF）等先进3D重建方法纳入OFO数据集，以提升重建质量，并讨论高质量3D重建对林业应用（如森林恢复、野火减灾、碳封存监测）的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OFO使用的经典SfM技术生成的3D树图存在伪影、细节不足，尤其在林下区域重建困难，这些误差可能传播到下游科学任务（如野火模拟），因此需要改进3D重建方法。

Method: 探索将NeRF等先进3D重建方法纳入OFO数据集，以替代经典SfM技术，并概述支持更前沿3D视觉模型的未来工作方向。

Result: NeRF相比SfM能生成更高质量、对稀疏视图更鲁棒、支持数据驱动先验的重建结果。

Conclusion: 高质量3D重建对于森林应用（如优先恢复、野火减灾、碳封存监测）至关重要，将NeRF等先进方法集成到OFO是提升数据质量的关键方向。

Abstract: The Open Forest Observatory (OFO) is a collaboration across universities and other partners to make low-cost forest mapping accessible to ecologists, land managers, and the general public. The OFO is building both a database of geospatial forest data as well as open-source methods and tools for forest mapping by uncrewed aerial vehicle. Such data are useful for a variety of climate applications including prioritizing reforestation efforts, informing wildfire hazard reduction, and monitoring carbon sequestration. In the current iteration of the OFO's forest map database, 3D tree maps are created using classical structure-from-motion techniques. This approach is prone to artifacts, lacks detail, and has particular difficulty on the forest floor where the input data (overhead imagery) has limited visibility. These reconstruction errors can potentially propagate to the downstream scientific tasks (e.g. a wildfire simulation.) Advances in 3D reconstruction, including methods like Neural Radiance Fields (NeRF), produce higher quality results that are more robust to sparse views and support data-driven priors. We explore ways to incorporate NeRFs into the OFO dataset, outline future work to support even more state-of-the-art 3D vision models, and describe the importance of high-quality 3D reconstructions for forestry applications.

</details>


### [131] [ReAge3D: Re-Aging 3D Faces with View Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18156)
*Libing Zeng,Li Ma,Mingming He,Ning Yu,Paul Debevec,Nima Khademi Kalantari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为DiffReaging的3D人脸年龄编辑框架，通过2D扩散模型和中心向外编辑传播策略，实现多视角一致性的人脸年龄变换，在保留身份特征的同时生成高细节的年龄编辑结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D编辑方法适用于粗粒度语义变化，但在年龄编辑任务中表现不佳，因为不同视角间的微小不一致会导致重要的年龄相关细节被过度平滑，难以生成生动、身份保留的年龄变换效果。

Method: 1) 提出DiffReaging，一个在合成图像对上训练的2D扩散重龄模型；2) 提出中心向外编辑传播策略，先编辑正面基准视角，再通过warping和Masked-DiffReaging过程重建其余视角；3) Masked-DiffReaging通过在扩散每一步注入已有内容，确保重建区域与现有像素保持一致性；4) 用一致的多视角重龄图像监督优化3D表示。

Result: 在视觉质量和定量指标上均优于现有3D编辑技术，实现了对人脸年龄变换的平滑、细粒度控制，同时保持身份特征和高频细节。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了3D人脸重龄化中的多视角一致性和细节保留难题，为可控的3D人脸年龄编辑提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: We present a novel framework for realistic and controllable 3D face re-aging which produces highly detailed, identity-preserving results. Existing 3D editing methods, while effective for coarse semantic changes, are not well suited for re-aging, as even small inconsistencies across re-aged 2D views can lead to over-smoothing of subtle but perceptually important age-related details. To address this challenge, we first introduce a 2D diffusion-based re-aging model, DiffReaging, trained on synthetically generated image pairs. We further propose a center-out editing propagation strategy that leverages this re-aging model to reconstruct multi-view-consistent re-aged images. Specifically, starting from a re-aged frontal pivot view, we reconstruct the remaining views through warping and our proposed Masked-DiffReaging process. By injecting existing content at every step of the diffusion process, Masked-DiffReaging ensures that the reconstructed regions remain coherent with existing pixels. The resulting consistent set of re-aged views supervises the optimization of the re-aged 3D representation. Our method outperforms existing 3D editing techniques both visually and quantitatively, enabling smooth, fine-grained control over age transformations in 3D face models.

</details>


### [132] [EgoCS-400K: An Egocentric Gameplay Dataset for World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18180)
*Rongjin Guo,Dong Liang,Yuhao Liu,Fang Liu,Tianyu Huang,Gerhard P. Hancke,Rynson W. H. Lau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了EgoCS-400K，一个基于反恐精英游戏回放的大规模第一人称数据集，包含40万+视频、1万小时游戏时长、1000+场比赛，为世界模型提供时间对齐的动作-语言-视频轨迹，支持动作条件未来预测、状态事件感知场景推演等多种交互式视觉建模任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从视频生成到交互式世界建模的转变需要时间对齐的视频-动作-语言轨迹数据，但现有网络视频缺乏可执行动作和可靠状态，机器人数据集成本高且场景多样性有限，模拟器缺乏大规模人类交互轨迹。

Method: 从公开的职业CS和CS2比赛回放文件中解析玩家状态、视角方向、移动、键盘/按钮输入、视角变化、武器使用、游戏事件和回合上下文，并从相同轨迹渲染干净的第一人称视频，构建了EgoCS-400K数据集。

Result: 数据集包含超过40万第一人称视频、1万小时游戏时长、来自1000+场比赛和4万+回合的数据，覆盖13张地图，每回合10个玩家视角。

Conclusion: EgoCS-400K通过大规模连接视觉观察与人类动作、相机运动、游戏状态和事件，在被动网络视频、可控游戏模拟和昂贵真实世界具身数据之间架起了实用桥梁。

Abstract: The shift from video generation to interactive world modeling places new demands on data: beyond captioned videos, world models require temporally aligned video-action-language trajectories grounded in the actions, camera motion, states, and events that drive future scene changes. However, such data is difficult to obtain at scale. Web video datasets offer broad visual coverage but lack executable actions and reliable states; robotic datasets provide action and state supervision but are costly and limited in scene diversity; and existing simulators often lack large-scale human-driven interaction trajectories. In this paper, we introduce EgoCS-400K, a large-scale replay-grounded egocentric Counter-Strike dataset for world models, built from public professional CS and CS2 match demos that preserve human gameplay trajectories and enable parsing, replaying, rendering, and temporal alignment. We extract player states, view directions, movements, keyboard/button inputs, view-angle changes, weapon usage, game events, and round-level context, and render clean first-person videos from the same trajectories. EgoCS-400K contains over 400,000 first-person videos and 10,000 hours of gameplay from more than 1,000 matches and 40,000 rounds, covering 13 maps and 10 player viewpoints per round. It supports a range of interactive visual modeling tasks, including action-conditioned future prediction, state- and event-aware scene rollout, replay-grounded captioning, and agent egocentric action understanding. By connecting visual observations with human actions, camera motion, game states, and events at scale, EgoCS-400K serves as a practical bridge between passive web videos, controllable game simulation, and costly real-world embodied data.

</details>


### [133] [EventDrive: Event Cameras for Vision-Language Driving Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18242)
*Dongyue Lu,Rong Li,Ao Liang,Lingdong Kong,Wei Yin,Lai Xing Ng,Benoit R. Cottereau,Camille Simon Chane,Wei Tsang Ooi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EventDrive是一个统一事件流、RGB帧和语言监督的大规模基准与模型套件，覆盖自动驾驶的感知、理解、预测和规划四个核心维度。其提出的EventDrive-VLM通过多水平事件金字塔和时间水平混合专家模块，自适应编码融合异步和帧基信息，实验证明事件流在时间精度、运动感知和鲁棒性方面带来显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件感知视觉语言模型仅限于通用感知任务，未能揭示事件传感如何在整个驾驶循环中贡献于推理和决策。

Method: 构建EventDrive基准套件，统一事件流、RGB帧和语言监督，覆盖描述、结构化QA、定位、运动状态识别、轨迹预测和规划任务；提出EventDrive-VLM，包含多水平事件金字塔和时间水平混合专家模块，用于自适应编码和融合异步与帧基信息。

Result: 全面评估表明事件流在时间精度、运动感知和鲁棒性方面带来显著增益。

Conclusion: 事件传感应被置于驾驶智能的中心位置。

Abstract: Event cameras sense the world through asynchronous brightness changes with microsecond latency and high dynamic range, offering motion fidelity far beyond frame-based sensors and capturing temporal structure that conventional exposures often miss. These properties make events a powerful complement to RGB in autonomous driving, especially under blur, glare, and rapid motion, where frame-based perception can become unreliable. However, existing event-aware vision-language models remain limited to generic perception and do not reveal how event sensing contributes to reasoning and decision-making across the full driving loop. We present EventDrive, a large-scale benchmark and model suite that unifies event streams, RGB frames, and language supervision across four core dimensions: Perception, Understanding, Prediction, and Planning, covering captions, structured QA, grounding, motion-state recognition, trajectory forecasting, and planning tasks. Building on this foundation, EventDrive-VLM introduces a multi-horizon event pyramid and a temporal-horizon mixture-of-experts module to adaptively encode and fuse asynchronous and frame-based information for downstream reasoning. Comprehensive evaluation across diverse tasks shows that event streams provide substantial gains in temporal precision, motion awareness, and robustness, bringing event sensing into the center of driving intelligence.

</details>


### [134] [MOCHI: Motion Enhancement of Collaborative Human-object Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18243)
*Jiye Lee,Yonghun Choi,Jungdam Won*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了MOCHI，一个两阶段框架，用于增强多人类-物体交互(MHOI)数据质量。第一阶段通过优化生成物理上合理且语义一致的手部抓取，并扩展为完整序列；第二阶段使用基于扩散的噪声优化框架，结合单人体运动先验以及人-物和人-人交互信息，细化全身运动。实验证明该方法对多种MHOI数据有效，并展示了关键帧MHOI创建和数据增强等应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多人类-物体交互(MHOI)数据采集因同时存在人-人和人-物交互而充满挑战，现有捕获数据常伴有手物接触错位、运动抖动、时间不一致以及手指级细节缺失等噪声问题，亟需一种能够增强此类噪声数据质量的方法。

Method: 两阶段框架：(1) 通过优化从噪声人体输入生成物理合理的手部抓取，语义上与身体姿态一致，并扩展为完整交互序列；(2) 基于扩散的噪声优化框架，利用单人运动先验，并在优化过程中引入人-物和人-人交互约束，对全身运动进行细化。

Result: 在多种MHOI数据（包括现有捕获方法和生成模型合成）上验证了有效性，展示了系统对不同参与者数量和交互类型的鲁棒性，以及关键帧MHOI创建和通过物体几何变化进行数据增强等应用。

Conclusion: MOCHI能有效提升MHOI数据质量，解决接触错位、运动抖动和手指细节缺失等问题，为协同人类-物体交互研究提供了可靠的数据增强方案。

Abstract: Collaborative human-object interaction shows dynamic and complex movements that require mutual anticipation and continuous adjustment between participants and the shared object. Modeling such collaborative multi-human object interaction (MHOI) scenarios requires high-quality data acquisition as a foundational step; however, this is challenging due to the inherent complexity of MHOI where human-human and human-object interactions occur simultaneously. Such complexity leads to noisy MHOI captures characterized by several artifacts: contact misalignment between hands and objects, motion jitter and temporal inconsistencies in the captured sequences, and missing or incomplete finger-level articulation details. To address these challenges, we present MOCHI (MOtion Enhancement of Collaborative Human-object Interactions), a two-stage framework for enhancing noisy MHOI data. Our approach first generates physically plausible hand grasps through optimization from noisy body input, producing grasps that are both physically plausible and semantically consistent with the body pose, where these optimized grasps are extended into complete hand-object interaction sequences. Consequently, the full-body motion for all participants are refined through a diffusion-based noise optimization framework that uses single-person motion priors. During the optimization process, we introduce optimization objectives to encode human-object and human-human interaction information within these single-person priors. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of our pipeline across diverse MHOI data, either acquired by existing capture methods or synthesized by generative models. We further show robustness of our system across varying numbers of participants and types of interactions, and demonstrate various applications including keyframe-based MHOI creation and data augmentation through varying object geometries.

</details>


### [135] [Unified Multimodal Autoregressive Modeling with Shared Context-Visual Tokenizer is Key to Unification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18249)
*Wujian Peng,Lingchen Meng,Yuxuan Cai,Xianwei Zhuang,Yuhuan Yang,Rongyao Fang,Chenfei Wu,Junyang Lin,Zuxuan Wu,Shuai Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniAR提出统一自回归框架，使用单一离散视觉分词器连接视觉理解与生成，通过多级特征融合、无查找比特量化、并行比特预测和扩散解码器，在图像生成和编辑上达到SOTA，同时保持多模态理解竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型依赖两个不同的视觉分词器，导致表示空间分裂，阻碍真正的统一建模。UniAR旨在通过单一离散视觉分词器桥接视觉理解与生成，使模型能直接解读自身生成的视觉token而无需额外重编码。

Method: 1) 采用单个离散视觉分词器，适配预训练视觉编码器进行多级特征融合，并使用无查找比特量化方案，在保留高层语义和底层细节的同时以最小代价扩展有效视觉词汇量；2) 统一自回归模型采用并行比特预测，联合预测空间分组的多级视觉编码，大幅减少视觉序列长度并加速生成；3) 基于扩散的视觉解码器对离散视觉token进行高保真图像解码。

Result: 通过大规模预训练、监督微调和强化学习，UniAR在图像生成和图像编辑任务上达到最先进性能，同时在多模态理解基准上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 单一离散视觉分词器结合并行比特预测和扩散解码的统一自回归框架能够有效统一视觉理解与生成，在两项生成任务上取得最优表现，且不牺牲理解能力。

Abstract: Unified Multimodal Modeling aims to integrate visual understanding and generation within a single system. However, existing approaches typically rely on two disparate visual tokenizers, which splits the representation space and hinders truly unified modeling. We propose UniAR, a unified autoregressive framework where a single discrete visual tokenizer serves as the key bridge between understanding and generation, enabling a shared context in which the model can directly interpret its own generated visual tokens without additional re-encoding. UniAR adapts a pretrained vision encoder with multi-level feature fusion and a lookup-free bitwise quantization scheme, preserving both high-level semantics and low-level details while scaling the effective visual vocabulary at minimal cost. Building on this, the unified autoregressive model adopts parallel-bitwise-prediction to jointly predict spatially grouped, multi-level visual codes, substantially reducing visual sequence length and accelerating generation. Finally, a diffusion-based visual decoder operates on discrete visual tokens to decode high-fidelity images. Through large-scale pre-training, followed by supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning, UniAR achieves state-of-the-art performance on image generation and image editing while remaining competitive on multimodal understanding benchmarks. The project page is available at https://sharelab-sii.github.io/uniar-web.

</details>


### [136] [Future Dynamic 3D Reconstruction: A 3D World Model with Disentangled Ego-Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18250)
*Nils Morbitzer,Jonathan Evers,Artem Savkin,Thomas Stauner,Nassir Navab,Federico Tombari,Stefano Gasperini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FR3D是一种预测持久3D潜在表示的世界模型，通过解耦场景3D演化与智能体轨迹，并引入师生蒸馏策略，实现了从单目观测到未来动态3D重建（最远2秒）的强泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式世界模型在2D视频合成中因将自我运动与环境动态混合在图像平面内，导致物理不一致性（如物体变形或消失），尤其在长时间跨度下问题突出。

Method: 1) 显式解耦场景3D演化与智能体轨迹，将推断的自我运动作为动作的潜在代理；2) 引入教师-学生蒸馏策略，利用现成基础模型的空间"常识"实现零样本泛化。

Result: 在多个数据集上，FR3D能够从单目观测中实现未来动态3D重建，即使预测未来2秒仍保持强性能和几何一致性。

Conclusion: 通过解耦自运动与世界运动、结合基础模型蒸馏，FR3D有效解决了世界模型中的物理不一致性问题，为长期动态3D场景预测提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Forecasting the evolution of dynamic environments is crucial for autonomous agents. While generative world models have recently achieved high photorealism in 2D video synthesis by mixing ego-motion and environmental dynamics within the image plane, they exhibit physical inconsistencies, such as morphing or vanishing objects, especially over long time horizons. In this paper, we propose FR3D, a world model that predicts a persistent 3D latent representation for future dynamic 3D reconstruction. Unlike prior works that treat the world as a sequence of image-based features, FR3D explicitly decouples the 3D evolution of the scene from the agent's trajectory, treating the inferred ego-motion as a latent proxy for action. This disentanglement resolves the ambiguities between self-motion and world-motion, ensuring geometric consistency into the future. Furthermore, we introduce a teacher-student distillation strategy that leverages the spatial "common sense" of off-the-shelf foundation models, leading to robust zero-shot generalization. Extensive experiments demonstrate FR3D's strong performance for future dynamic 3D reconstruction from monocular observations across multiple datasets, even 2 seconds into the future. Project page: https://fr3d-wm.github.io.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [137] [Security and Human-Centered Assessment of BACnet-Controlled DALI Infrastructure in an Educational Building Automation Testbed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17089)
*Ariton Verush*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过2026年在瑞士图恩举办的楼宇自动化网络安全黑客马拉松中的实践案例，结合Yabe和BACteria工具，对BACnet/IP楼宇自动化测试平台及DALI照明基础设施进行了网络枚举、对象级检查、物理机架分析和人机交互反思分析，重建了对象层级结构并识别了房间级照明控制路径，强调楼宇自动化系统评估不仅是技术协议任务，还需可用工具界面、物理可观测性、可解释命名约定和安全心理模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 楼宇自动化与控制系统通过专用通信协议集成了暖通、照明、传感和管理功能，这种集成在实现灵活楼宇运行的同时，也造成了难以检查、安全加固和向新分析师解释的复杂信息物理环境。现有研究缺乏对BACnet/DALI联合环境的安全评估实践案例，特别是在教育性黑客马拉松场景下结合人机交互视角的系统性探索。

Method: 在2026年4月瑞士图恩的楼宇自动化网络安全黑客马拉松中，以BACnet/IP楼宇自动化测试平台和DALI照明基础设施为对象，综合采用网络枚举、对象级检查、物理机架分析和反思性人机交互分析四种方法。使用Yabe和BACteria工具，观测BACnet服务，重构对象层级结构，识别房间级照明控制路径，并将BACnet对象映射到DALI组级基础设施。

Result: 成功记录了可观测的BACnet服务，重建了结构化对象层级，识别了房间级照明控制路径，并建立了BACnet对象与DALI组级基础设施之间的映射关系。研究发现楼宇自动化系统评估不仅是技术协议任务，还需要可用工具界面、物理可观测性、可解释命名约定和安全命令优先级心理模型。

Conclusion: 本文贡献了一个BACnet/DALI探索的教育测试平台案例研究，并讨论了其对网络安全教育、以人为中心的安全工具设计以及信息物理楼宇环境中负责任实验的启示。

Abstract: Building automation and control systems integrate heating, ventilation, air conditioning, lighting, sensing, and management functions through specialized communication protocols. While this integration enables flexible building operation, it also creates complex cyber-physical environments that are difficult to inspect, secure, and explain to new analysts. This paper presents a practical security and human-centered case study of a BACnet/IP building automation testbed with DALI lighting infrastructure, investigated during a domotics-oriented cybersecurity hackathon in Thun, Switzerland in April 2026. The study combines network-oriented enumeration, object-level inspection, physical rack analysis, and reflective HCI analysis of tool-supported learning. Using Yabe and BACteria, the work documents observable BACnet services, reconstructs structured object hierarchies, identifies room-level lighting-control paths, and maps BACnet objects to DALI group-level infrastructure. The analysis emphasizes that BACS assessment is not only a technical protocol task: it also requires usable tool interfaces, physical observability, interpretable naming conventions, and safe mental models for command priorities. The paper contributes a compact case study of BACnet/DALI exploration in an educational testbed and discusses implications for cybersecurity education, human-centered security tooling, and responsible experimentation in cyber-physical building environments.

</details>


### [138] [Securing Multi-Agent GIS Systems: Risk Evaluation and Prompt Hardening Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17092)
*Kyle Gao,Pranavi Kotta,Linlin Xu,Jonathan Li,David A. Clausi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向多智能体GIS系统的安全框架，包括风险识别、评估和缓解，通过模块化状态机编排、红队攻击测试和提示优化，在不影响任务性能的前提下提升系统鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统与地理信息系统（GIS）的集成带来了复杂对话和空间分析能力，但也引入了安全风险，现有研究缺乏针对此类系统的系统性安全评估与防护框架。

Method: 提出模块化状态机编排框架抽象智能体行为；构建红队测试框架（自适应攻击LLM + 确定性评判器）；设计提示优化框架将提示视为结构化签名并注入对抗样本。

Result: 提示优化框架能在不降低任务性能的前提下系统性提升安全性；红队框架可生成多轮攻击的二元结果及支持性理由。

Conclusion: 本文框架有效提升了多智能体GIS系统的安全鲁棒性，且具有良好的可迁移性，可适配更广泛的智能体架构。

Abstract: Agentic systems are increasingly integrated with geographic information systems (GIS), where multi-agent coordination enables complex conversational and spatial analysis but introduces security risks. This work presents a security-oriented framework for risk identification, evaluation, and mitigation in a multi-agent GIS system while maintaining adaptability to broader agentic architectures. We test the agentic system of a commercial geospatial partner while developing a modular state-machine-based orchestration framework that abstracts agent behavior into reusable components. We evaluate robustness using a red-teaming framework with an adaptive attacker LLM and a deterministic judge that produces binary outcomes with supporting rationales across multi-turn attacks. We further improve resilience with a prompt optimization framework that treats prompts as structured signatures and injects adversarial demonstrations, enabling systematic security improvements without degrading task performance.

</details>


### [139] [Timestamp-Aware Spatio-Temporal Graph Contrastive Learning for Network Intrusion Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17109)
*Jianli Dai,Guangwei Wu,Jiacheng Li,Weiping Wang,An He,Xinjun Xiao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于自监督图神经网络（GNN）的网络入侵检测框架，利用真实时间戳构建时序图，结合E-GraphSAGE和LSTM编码器提取时空依赖，并通过多视图图对比学习（时间、空间、特征对比）和梯度范数自适应加权策略，在四个真实数据集上达到与监督方法可比性能，且计算高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN-based NIDS方法主要关注流量间的 relational structure，将流量视为时间独立的，难以应对演化的攻击行为；此外，监督或半监督学习限制了其对未知攻击的泛化能力。

Method: 1) 根据时间戳构建时序图序列；2) 使用E-GraphSAGE+LSTM编码器提取时空依赖（避免注意力机制的高计算成本）；3) 多视图图对比学习（时间对比、空间对比、特征对比）；4) 梯度范数自适应加权策略优化对比损失权重。

Result: 在四个具有真实时间戳的NIDS数据集上，显著优于现有自监督方法，性能与监督SOTA GNN方法相当，且保持高计算效率。

Conclusion: 自监督GNN框架通过显式利用时间戳和多视图对比学习，有效提升了网络入侵检测对演化攻击的泛化能力和计算效率。

Abstract: Given their effectiveness in modeling the relational structure among network traffic flows, graph neural networks (GNNs) have been widely adopted in network intrusion detection systems (NIDSs). However, most existing GNN-based NIDS approaches focus on the relational structure of traffic flows, and treat them as temporally independent, which limits their ability to cope with evolving attack behaviors. Moreover, their reliance on supervised or semi-supervised learning often restricts generalization to unseen attacks. To address these limitations, we propose a novel self-supervised GNN-based framework. To the best of our knowledge, the proposed model is among the first self-supervised GNN-based NIDS models to explicitly leverage real timestamps, which provides faithful temporal dependencies for representation learning. We first construct a series of temporal graphs from network traffic flows according to their timestamps, and then employ an E-GraphSAGE and LSTM based encoder to fully extract temporal information and spatial dependencies of network traffic, without introducing time-costly attention mechanisms. A multi-view graph contrastive learning (GCL) scheme is introduced, where temporal, spatial, and feature contrasts are jointly performed to capture temporal continuity, preserve structural consistency, and improve the generalization and robustness of the learned representations, respectively. In addition, a gradient-norm-based adaptive weighting strategy is designed to optimize the contrastive loss weights. Experimental results on four representative NIDS datasets with real timestamps demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms existing self-supervised approaches and achieves performance comparable to the supervised state-of-the-art GNN method, while maintaining high computational efficiency.

</details>


### [140] [Loss Landscape Poisoning: Targeted Extraction of Unseen Training Data from LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17110)
*Md Abdullah Al Mamun,Ngoc Phu Doan,Pedram Zaree,Ihsen Alouani,Nael Abu-Ghazaleh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种数据投毒攻击，通过操纵训练数据重塑模型损失景观，迫使模型记忆目标数据，从而在集中式和联邦学习场景中实现高成功率的隐私泄露（语言模型达100%，多模态模型达90%），并发现了一种能绕过差分隐私防御的新型攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型训练常涉及敏感数据（医疗、金融、用户对话），隐私泄露风险日益严峻。现有攻击通常假设攻击者能直接访问目标数据或进行推理，本文探究攻击者能否通过投毒部分训练数据，间接泄露其无法访问的独立目标记录。

Method: 攻击者投毒部分训练数据，在目标完成位置附近重塑模型的局部损失景观：在目标处制造尖锐的损失最小值，同时抬高附近替代输出的损失值，迫使模型将目标记忆为邻域内唯一的低损失解。该方法无需修改模型架构，可适用于集中式和联邦学习。

Result: 该攻击在语言模型上实现高达100%的成功提取，在视觉-语言模型上实现高达90%的成功提取。差分隐私训练可抵御该攻击，但作者进一步提出一种直接探测损失景观的新型攻击，能够绕过差分隐私防御。

Conclusion: 数据投毒可有效放大隐私泄露风险，且现有差分隐私防御存在被绕过可能，需警惕更复杂的隐私攻击手段。

Abstract: Large Language Models are increasingly trained on proprietary or sensitive data, from private healthcare and financial records to user conversations containing secrets. Ensuring the privacy of such data against extraction attacks has become a central concern. In this paper, we ask whether an attacker who can poison a portion of the training data can facilitate the leakage of a separate target record they have no access to. We answer in the affirmative and show that such leakage can be induced by a poisoning mechanism that reshapes the model's local loss landscape around the target completion. Our key insight is that poisoning to create a sharp loss minimum at the target, surrounded by elevated loss on nearby alternatives, forces the model to memorize the target as the unique low-loss solution in its neighborhood. The attack requires no architectural changes, and generalizes across centralized and federated learning settings. We demonstrate that the attack amplifies privacy leakage across language (up to 100% successful extraction), and vision-language models (up 90% successful extraction). We show that the attack is thwarted when the model is trained to be differentially private. However, we introduce a new attack that directly probes the loss landscape bypassing even differential privacy defenses.

</details>


### [141] [Fractional Verkle Trees: A Hypertree Decomposition and Verified Proof Serialization Architecture for High-Performance Blockchain State Accumulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17111)
*Ekleen Kaur,Everton Fraga*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出分数维克尔树(FVT)，通过超树分解将全局状态划分为N个独立子累加器，解决以太坊Verkle树实现中的四项关键低效问题，在Apple M1 Pro上实现57%堆分配减少、并行插入2,433 ns/op，全网每年消除4.85 PB冗余数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代区块链状态管理面临可扩展性瓶颈：维护数亿条目的密码学承诺计算开销巨大。以太坊转向Verkle树以实现无状态操作，但当前参考实现(go-verkle)存在病理特征，加重了家庭验证者负担。

Method: 提出分数维克尔树(FVT)，采用超树分解将全局状态划分为N个独立子累加器，由Merkle承诺树协调。针对四项低效问题分别采用：存在性检查、32字节SHA256节点引用、零拷贝引用计数缓冲区、基于HashMap的字典序去重。

Result: Apple M1 Pro基准测试：堆分配减少57%（每10K证明从566,760降至242,004字节）、并行插入2,433 ns/op、根重计算91μs（vs ~500ms）、全网6,000全节点每年消除4.85 PB冗余数据。

Conclusion: FVT通过超树分解和针对性优化，显著提升了Verkle树实现的效率，推进了以太坊无状态路线图。

Abstract: Modern blockchain state management faces a critical scalability bottleneck: maintaining cryptographic commitments over hundreds of millions of entries becomes computationally prohibitive. Ethereum's transition to Verkle Trees: polynomial commitment accumulators reducing proof sizes from O(width * depth) to O(depth) via constant-size IPA vector commitments, is a critical step toward stateless operation. Yet, current implementations exhibit pathological characteristics that burden home validators.
  We identify four inefficiencies in the reference go-verkle implementation \cite{kaur2025goverkle, kaur2025goethereum}: (1) phantom node creation during non-existent account deletion; (2) 64-byte database keys triggering excessive LSM-tree compaction; (3) redundant memory copying in proof deserialization; (4) a Proof of Absence wire format incompatibility causing non-deterministic serialization.
  We present Fractional Verkle Trees (FVT), a hypertree decomposition partitioning global state into N independent sub-accumulators coordinated by a Merkle commitment tree, achieving improved cache locality, zero-lock-contention goroutine-parallel commitment computation, and faster root recomputation (91 $μ$s vs $\sim$500 ms). We address each inefficiency via existence checks, 32-byte SHA256 node references, zero-copy reference-counted buffers, and HashMap-based lexicographic deduplication.
  Benchmarks on Apple M1 Pro show 57\% heap allocation reduction (566,760 to 242,004 bytes per 10K proofs), parallel insertion at 2,433 ns/op, and network-wide elimination of 4.85 PB/year across 6,000 full nodes, advancing the Ethereum stateless roadmap.

</details>


### [142] [An Evaluation of Data Leakage Risks in Tool-Using LLM Agents in Realistic Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17114)
*Hankyul Baek,Jaewon Noh,Sang Seo,Yongsu Kim,Gabriel Waikin Loh Matienzo,Young Il Kim,Ee Wei Seah,Akriti Vij*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文由新加坡AI安全研究所和韩国AI安全研究所联合评估了AI agent在非对抗性场景下的数据泄露风险。在12个真实任务（客服、DevOps、网页自动化、生产力等）中测试了5种风险类型（数据意识、受众意识、政策合规、数据最小化、访问边界意识），结果显示所有三种agent均未能在所有场景中同时实现正确且安全的执行，成功完成任务常伴随数据处理失败，表明能力与数据安全应分别评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注对抗性数据泄露（如提示注入和越狱攻击），但敏感信息也可能在非对抗性正常使用中泄露。我们旨在系统评估AI agent在日常操作中的数据泄漏风险。

Method: 联合评估：12个非对抗性真实任务（客服、DevOps、网页自动化、企业/个人生产力），覆盖5类风险（数据意识、受众意识、政策合规、数据最小化、访问边界意识）。两个机构使用独立测试环境和任务特定LLM评判标准进行测试。

Result: 所有三种agent均未在所有场景中实现完全正确且完全安全的执行。任务成功完成常伴随数据处理失败（如访问不必要信息、向不适当接收者披露信息）。定性审查还揭示了声明-行动不匹配、模拟感知行为、用户-模拟器角色反转以及自动评判中的解释差距。

Conclusion: 操作性数据泄露是与对抗性数据泄露不同的首要agent安全问题，能力和数据处理安全性应分开评估。本文提供了未来agent数据处理安全评估的方法论。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly being adopted in enterprise and personal settings with access to emails, databases, documents, and other tools where they can read, update, and disseminate sensitive information. Much of prior research on data leakage risks in agents has focused on adversarial data exfiltration through prompt injections and jailbreaks. However, sensitive information may also be exposed during non-adversarial use, creating leakage risks even when users issue benign requests.
  We report a joint evaluation by the Singapore AI Safety Institute and the Korea AI Safety Institute examining agent data leakage in 12 realistic, non-adversarial tasks spanning customer support, DevOps, web automation, and enterprise and personal productivity. The evaluation covers five risk types: lack of data awareness, audience awareness, policy compliance, data minimization, and access-boundary awareness. Both institutes tested a common set of scenarios mirroring real-world deployments using independent testing environments and task-specific LLM-judge rubrics.
  Across the three tested agents, none achieved fully correct and fully safe execution across all scenarios. Successful task completion often coincided with data-handling failures such as accessing unnecessary information or disclosing information to inappropriate recipients, indicating that capability and data-handling safety should be evaluated separately. Qualitative review also revealed claim-action mismatches, simulation-aware behavior, user-simulator role reversal, and interpretation gaps in automated judging. Overall, the results indicate that operational data leakage is a first-order agent-safety concern distinct from adversarial exfiltration and provide a methodology for future evaluations of agent data-handling safety.

</details>


### [143] [Quantifying quantum risk: a measure of crypto agility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17116)
*Coryan Wilson-Shah*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出"轮换时间"概念作为密码敏捷性的度量，并推导出轮换时间容忍度与安全风险容忍度之间的近似关系。基于历史CVE数据计算表明，轮换时间容忍度在数小时到数天量级，说明结合密码敏捷性与混合加密是设计量子弹性系统的有效方法，但可能面临严格的技术与操作挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
