Conclusion: 本文填补了lugsail估计量带宽选择的理论空白，为稳健长程方差估计提供了更优的实践指导。

Abstract: For datasets with unknown but stationary serial dependence, a robust long run variance estimator is essential to handle diverse scenarios. Spectral variance estimators are commonly used but tend to exhibit significant negative bias in the presence of positive correlation. To overcome this, zero lugsail estimators have been introduced, offering zero asymptotic bias regardless of the correlation structure. However, there are currently no guidelines for selecting the optimal bandwidth for lugsail estimators, a critical component in the estimation process. We propose an inference optimal bandwidth rule for lugsail estimators, based on nonstandard fixed-smoothing limiting distributions developed in our study. This approach significantly improves bias correction, accounts for variability, and provides an estimator optimized for robust inference. Our theoretical findings are supported by a simulation study.

</details>


### [179] [Non-asymptotic Tail Bounds for the Kostlan--Shub--Smale Field: Tensor PCA and Spherical $k$-Spin Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17665)
*Jean-Marc Azaïs,Federico Dalmao,Yohann De Castro*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文构建了Kostlan–Shub–Smale (KSS)随机场在球面上的显式非渐近尾界层次结构，并将其应用于Spiked Tensor PCA和球面k-自旋模型景观两个问题。通过Tube Method和秩约化将估计误差归结为KSS场的上确界，再利用Kac–Rice公式转化为Gaussian积分，最终由四种显式尾界控制。在Tensor PCA中得到了有限样本误差界，恢复了Perry–Wein–Bandeira的渐近最优率√(d log k)；在球面k-自旋模型中得到了退火复杂度的非渐近括号界，恢复了Auffinger–Ben Arous–Černý复杂度函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为KSS随机场的上确界建立显式的非渐近尾界层次结构，并利用此工具解决两个重要统计问题：Spiked Tensor PCA的有限样本估计误差分析和球面k-自旋模型能量景观的复杂度分析。

Method: 1) 确定性几何不等式（Tube Method）结合秩约化步骤，将估计误差归约为KSS随机场的上确界；2) 利用Kac–Rice公式将上确界转化为Gaussian积分，即期望的移位GOE特征多项式绝对值；3) 通过四种显式尾界（三种基于Mehta–Fyodorov表示，一种基于Ben Arous–Dembo–Guionnet大偏差）控制该积分。

Result: 1) 对Tensor PCA，得到了依赖秩R和相干性κ的有限样本误差界，恢复了渐近最优率√(d log k)；2) 对球面k-自旋模型，得到了退火复杂度的两侧非渐近括号界，在高维极限下恢复Auffinger–Ben Arous–Černý复杂度函数。

Conclusion: 展示了KSS场尾界层次的通用性，统一了Tensor PCA和球面自旋玻璃模型的分析框架，提供了两个问题的显式常数和有限样本保证。

Abstract: This paper builds a hierarchy of explicit, non-asymptotic tail bounds for the supremum of the Kostlan--Shub--Smale (KSS) random field on the sphere, and applies it to two problems: Spiked Tensor PCA and the landscape of the spherical $k$-spin model. For Tensor PCA, we study the non-asymptotic statistical limits of estimating a rank-$R$ symmetric signal tensor of order~$k\ge 3$ and dimension~$d\ge 3$ from a single Gaussian observation at signal-to-noise ratio~$λ$, through the \emph{profile maximum likelihood estimator}, the MLE restricted to normalized rank-$R$ tensors of coherence at least~$κ$. Our analysis uses a single reduction: a deterministic geometric inequality (the Tube Method) and a rank-reduction step bound the estimation error by the supremum of the canonical KSS field, which the Kac--Rice formula turns into a Gaussian integral against the expected absolute characteristic polynomial of a shifted Gaussian Orthogonal Ensemble, controlled in turn by the four explicit tail bounds of our hierarchy (three from a Mehta--Fyodorov representation, one from a Ben Arous--Dembo--Guionnet large deviation). The same reduction yields two results, each with explicit constants. For estimation, a finite-$(k,d)$ error bound recovers the asymptotically optimal rate~$\sqrt{d\log k}$ of Perry, Wein and Bandeira, with explicit dependence on the rank~$R$ and the coherence~$κ$. For the landscape, a two-sided non-asymptotic bracketing of the annealed complexity of the spherical $k$-spin Hamiltonian recovers the Auffinger--Ben Arous--Černý complexity function in the high-dimensional limit.

</details>


### [180] [Conformal Prediction Intervals with Tail-Specific Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18199)
*Simone Cuonzo,Nina Deliu*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文扩展了经典共形预测区间框架，通过分别构造上、下单侧共形区间并取交集，得到具有尾部特异性覆盖保证的双侧预测区间。在可交换数据下具有有限样本保证，在非可交换数据下具有渐近保证。模拟和金融应用表明，该方法在偏态数据中显著改善了方向性校准，尤其适用于对左尾风险有严格控制的场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统共形预测区间只保证全局边际覆盖 $1-α$，无法对上下尾提供分别的校准保证，在偏态数据中可能导致某一尾部的实际覆盖严重偏离名义水平。本文旨在构建具有显式尾部校准保证的共形预测区间。

Method: 基于分割共形预测框架，首先分别构造具有边际有效性的上侧和下侧单侧共形区间，然后通过取交集得到双侧区间。理论推导了该区间在可交换数据下的有限样本尾部覆盖保证和非可交换数据下的渐近保证。

Result: 模拟研究表明，所提方法在偏态数据中相比经典双侧共形区间显著改善了方向性校准；金融应用展示了该方法在最大化收益的同时严格控制左尾风险的实际价值。

Conclusion: 本文提出的尾部特异性共形预测框架为偏态分布下的预测不确定性量化提供了更精细的控制，弥补了经典全局覆盖方法的不足，在风险管理等场景具有重要应用前景。

Abstract: This paper extends classical conformal frameworks for constructing prediction intervals with global marginal coverage $1-α$ to intervals that provide explicitly calibrated guarantees for the upper and lower tails separately. Focusing on split conformal prediction, we first construct lower and upper one-sided conformal intervals that achieve marginal validity, and then derive the induced two-sided interval by intersection. Theoretical results prove both tail-specific and global marginal coverage of the induced two-sided interval. Results are presented first for the exchangeable setting, where coverage has finite-sample guarantees, and then for non-exchangeable data, where guarantees are asymptotic. Simulation studies show that the proposed approach achieves improved directional calibration relative to classical two-sided intervals, especially relevant in skewed data. Finally, the benefit of the proposed framework is showcased in a financial application, where one aims for return maximization while seeking strict control on the left tail.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [181] [Correct When Paired, Wrong When Split: Decoupling and Editing Modality-Specific Neurons in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17057)
*Tingchao Fu,Wenkai Wang,Fanxiao Li,Huadong Zhang,Jinhong Zhang,Dayang Li,Yunyun Dong,Renyang Liu,Wei Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多模态大语言模型知识编辑中的"编辑解耦失败"问题（多模态输入更新知识后，单模态输入仍恢复旧知识），提出DECODE方法，通过显式解耦和定位模态特定神经元组来实现跨模态一致的知识更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态大语言模型知识编辑存在一个被忽视的重要问题：编辑解耦失败——当通过多模态输入（文本-图像对）更新实体相关知识后，若将输入拆分为单模态形式，模型仍会回退到编辑前的旧知识。这一问题的根源尚未被充分探索和解决。

Method: 首先通过深入实证分析发现MLLM中的实体知识并非以统一表示存储，而是分散在解耦的模态特定通路中。基于此，提出DECODE方法，显式地解耦并定位不同模态特定的神经元组，从而针对性地进行知识更新。

Result: 大量实验表明，DECODE能够在不同模态触发条件下 consistently 实现有效的知识更新，从而缓解编辑解耦失败问题。

Conclusion: MLLM中的知识编辑必须考虑模态特异性问题，DECODE通过显式解耦模态通路实现了跨模态一致的知识更新，为多模态知识编辑提供了新范式。

Abstract: Although Knowledge Editing provides an efficient mechanism for updating the knowledge of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), we find that current paradigms still suffer from an important yet remain underexplored issue : editing decoupling failure, where entity-related knowledge can be updated when the model is triggered by multimodal inputs (text--image query pairs), however, it often reverts to outdated pre-edit facts when the paired inputs are split into unimodal ones. Our in-depth empirical analysis reveals that the entity knowledge in MLLMs is not stored as a unified representation, but is instead distributed across disentangled modality-specific pathways. As a result, updates biased toward multimodal queries fail to propagate effectively to unimodal circuits. To bridge this gap, we propose DECODE, which explicitly disentangles and localizes modality-specific neuron groups for targeted knowledge. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DECODE consistently achieves effective knowledge updates under different modality triggers, thereby mitigating editing decoupling failures.

</details>


### [182] [Diagnosing and Repairing Shape-Prior Shortcuts in Long-Range Single-Shot Fringe Projection Profilometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17093)
*Adam Haroon,Anush Lakshman,Cody Fleming,Beiwen Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对远距离（1.5-2.1米）单次条纹投影轮廓测量问题，通过机械可解释性和保形不确定性量化诊断发现基线UNet依赖物体边界形状先验而非条纹相位解码。提出PhiCalNet，通过输出包裹相位并应用固定可微标定层架构性地移除形状先验解，将物体MAE降低3.3倍至4.46mm，残差主要集中于±π包裹不连续处。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远距离单次FPP面临三个问题：逆平方强度衰减降低条纹信噪比并破坏物理真值、单次图像缺乏条纹阶次信息导致病态问题、现有架构缺乏机理性研究。

Method: 采用"诊断-修复-验证"框架：(1)用线性探测、Grad-CAM、平面外分布测试三种探针诊断基线UNet；(2)提出PhiCalNet输出包裹相位而非深度，用固定可微标定层将相位映射到深度，从假设空间中架构性地移除形状先验解；(3)用保形不确定性量化进行收敛验证。

Result: 基线UNet物体MAE为14.54mm；PhiCalNet降低3.3倍至4.46mm；残差仅0.103%像素位于±π包裹不连续处；剔除前5%高不确定度像素后PhiCalNet RMSE降低64%（20.6→7.4mm），基线仅降低3.5%。

Conclusion: MI和UQ两种收敛诊断工具一致定位故障源为物理上的形状先验依赖；架构修复（而非损失惩罚）是消除该幻觉的有效手段；PhiCalNet在远距离单次FPP任务中显著优于基线。

Abstract: Learning-based single-shot fringe projection profilometry (FPP) has been studied mostly at close range. The long-range regime (standoff beyond 1 m) remains largely unaddressed: inverse-square intensity falloff lowers fringe signal-to-noise ratio and degrades physical ground truth, the single-shot problem is ill-posed because fringe-order information is absent from one image, and these architectures have not been studied mechanistically. We present a diagnose-repair-verify study using mechanistic interpretability (MI) and conformal uncertainty quantification (UQ) as convergent diagnostics: they agree on one physical failure locus, driving and verifying an architectural repair. On a photorealistic synthetic benchmark (15,600 fringe images, 50 objects at 1.5-2.1 m), a best UNet baseline reaches 14.54 mm object mean absolute error (MAE). Three probes (linear probing, Grad-CAM, flat-plane out-of-distribution test) converge: the baseline solves the task via object-boundary shape priors rather than fringe-phase decoding. We repair this with PhiCalNet, which outputs wrapped phase rather than depth and applies a fixed differentiable calibration layer mapping phase to depth, removing the shape-prior solution from the hypothesis space architecturally rather than by a loss penalty. A physics-informed loss that enforces the same physics as a soft penalty on a depth-regressing network yields no measurable gain, isolating the architecture as the operative factor. PhiCalNet reduces object MAE 3.3x to 4.46 mm; the residual is carried by 0.103% of pixels at the +/-pi wrap discontinuity. Pixel-wise conformal UQ confirms the diagnosis: rejecting the top 5% of object pixels by snapshot disagreement cuts PhiCalNet RMSE by 64% (20.6->7.4 mm) versus 3.5% for the baseline. MI and UQ converge on the same failure locus.

</details>


### [183] [Informative Missingness to Generate Irregular Clinical Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17106)
*Hadi Mehdizavareh,Gabriele Santangelo,Giovanna Nicora,Simon Lebech Cichosz,Arianna Dagliati,Arijit Khan,Riccardo Bellazzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于扩散模型的临床时间序列生成方法，联合建模实验室检验值及其缺失模式。该方法将数据对齐为4小时间隔、7天窗口的轨迹，扩展TimeDiff框架通过互补扩散目标学习连续值和离散缺失模式。实验表明生成数据能捕捉患者生理与临床检测行为之间的依赖关系，可作为临床基础模型的前期组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录中的实验室检验数据采集不规律，检验缺失本身蕴含临床决策和患者生理状态信息，传统预处理方式会丢失这种信息。现有方法多将缺失视为预处理伪影而非直接建模的对象，因此需要一种能联合建模观测值和缺失模式的方法。

Method: 基于扩散模型，扩展TimeDiff框架：(1) 将MIMIC-III数据中的检验时间对齐到4小时间隔，住院记录分割为7天窗口；(2) 每个实验室值配对对应的观测指示符；(3) 通过互补扩散目标（连续值的扩散损失 + 离散缺失模式的扩散损失）联合学习；(4) 应用标准变换和归一化稳定训练。

Result: 生成数据在单个实验室分布和联合值-缺失嵌入空间上与真实患者轨迹高度吻合，表明扩散模型可以捕捉患者生理与临床检测行为之间在MNAR-like缺失模式下的依赖关系。

Conclusion: 该方法可作为临床基础模型的初始组件，通过生成保留关键生理-缺失关系的合成先验数据，为后续训练Prior-Data Fitted Networks（利用信息性缺失）奠定基础。

Abstract: Laboratory tests in electronic health records are collected irregularly, and the absence of a test order can be as informative as the measurement itself. Such missingness reflects clinicians' decisions and patient physiology, making it important to model it directly rather than treat it as a preprocessing artifact. Here we present a diffusion-based approach for generating clinical time series that jointly models laboratory values and their observation patterns using the public Data Analytics Challenge on Missing Data Imputation (DACMI) benchmark derived from MIMIC-III. To preserve realistic sampling, we align chart times into 4-hour intervals and segment admissions into 7-day windows, producing trajectories that pair each lab value with a corresponding observation indicator. Standard transformations and normalization are applied to stabilize training. Our method extends the TimeDiff framework to learn continuous lab values and discrete missingness patterns through complementary diffusion objectives. Experiments show that the generated data closely match real patient trajectories across individual lab distributions and joint value-missingness embeddings, demonstrating that diffusion models can capture clinically meaningful dependencies between patient physiology and clinicians' testing behavior under MNAR-like (missing-not-at-random) missingness. These preliminary results indicate that our model can serve as an initial component toward developing clinical foundation models. By producing synthetic priors that preserve key physiology-missingness relationships, this work motivates the subsequent training of Prior-Data Fitted Networks capable of leveraging informative missingness, which we will investigate in the extended work.

</details>


### [184] [Models Take Notes at Prefill: KV Cache Can Be Editable and Composable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17107)
*Bojie Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现前缀缓存中，前缀共享失效的根本原因是模型在预填充阶段已将字段条件结论写入下游“笔记”，而字段自身的key/value对决策贡献不足1%。基于此观察，提出可编辑和可组合的KV缓存机制：通过追加纠错来修改笔记，且笔记位置可移植，支持预编译技能的拼接，最终实现与完全重计算决策一致的编辑+组合智能体，延迟降低最高14.9倍，并在vLLM基准测试中保持98.5%缓存命中率，p90首token延迟降低53-398倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前缀缓存在共享前缀中仅复用精确匹配的预填充结果，一旦某个字段发生变化，整个下游缓存失效。但仅覆盖该字段自身的key/value向量而重用其余部分，模型仍基于旧值做出决策。需要从因果角度理解这一现象的本质，并据此设计更高效的KV缓存编辑与组合方法。

Method: 通过跨四个模型家族的因果分析，发现模型在预填充阶段已将“字段条件性结论”写入下游“笔记”（即注意力层中的隐藏状态），而字段自身的key/value对决策贡献极小（低于1%）。基于此“笔记化结论”视角，提出：(1) 可编辑性：通过追加纠错（erratum）修改笔记，结合思维链（CoT）可在仅编辑字段的情况下恢复决策，计算量约为原始1%；(2) 可组合性：笔记具有位置可移植性，预编译技能可通过RoPE重定位并拼接到任意上下文中，在O(L)而非O(L^2)时间内达到与完全重计算不可区分的程度（logit余弦相似度0.90-0.999）。最终构建统一的编辑+组合智能体。

Result: 编辑方式在8B模型上恢复决策准确率1.00，计算量约1%；组合方式在12个模型上logit余弦相似度0.90-0.999，时间复杂度从O(L²)降至O(L)；统一智能体与完全重计算决策一致，延迟降低最高14.9倍；在vLLM在线基准测试中保持98.5%前缀缓存命中率，p90首token延迟降低53-398倍。方法适用于各种规模、量化、MoE、多模态缓存及多种注意力变体。

Conclusion: 论文揭示了前缀缓存失效的根本原因——模型将结论写入下游笔记而非字段自身key/value中，并据此提出了可编辑、可组合的KV缓存机制。该机制仅通过追加操作即可实现高效编辑与组合，与生产级前缀缓存兼容，显著降低了首token延迟，且适用范围广泛。

Abstract: Prefix caching reuses prefill only across an exactly shared prefix, so one changed field invalidates the entire downstream cache. Yet overwriting the field's own key/value vectors and reusing the rest leaves the model acting on the old value. The reason, established causally across four model families: at prefill the model has already written the field-conditioned conclusion onto downstream notes; the field's own key/value drives under 1% of the decision. Read as a notebook of memoized conclusions, two capabilities follow. (1) It is editable. A salient erratum amends the notes; and with chain-of-thought, editing the field alone recovers the decision (1.00 at 8B, ~1% compute), while without CoT it is ignored. (2) It is composable. The notes are position-portable, so a precompiled skill can be RoPE-repositioned and spliced into any context, indistinguishable from full recompute (logit cosine 0.90-0.999, twelve models) at O(L) rather than O(L^2) time-to-first-token. A unified edit+compose agent stays decision-identical to recompute at up to 14.9x lower latency. The approach applies to any per-token attention KV cache, validated across scale, quantization, Mixture-of-Experts, and multimodal caches, and extends to several attention variants through small adapters. Because the erratum is append-only, it composes with production prefix caching: in an online vLLM benchmark it keeps the prefix cache-aligned (98.5% hit-rate), cutting p90 time-to-first-token by 53-398x.

</details>


### [185] [The Critical Role of Model Selection in Causal Inference: A Comparative Analysis of Classification Models within the InferBERT Framework for Pharmacovigilance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17113)
*Csaba Kiss,Roland Molontay,Gabriele Pergola*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究比较了InferBERT框架中不同模型（XGBoost、ALBERT、BioBERT、Med-LLaMA）在因果药物不良事件检测中的效果，发现领域特异性预训练的BioBERT在准确率和因果一致性上最优，而大型医学LLM（Med-LLaMA）表现不佳，表明领域专业模型比单纯扩大模型规模更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物警戒中区分因果性药物不良事件（ADE）与虚假相关性是一大核心挑战。InferBERT框架将Transformer模型与Do-calculus结合，但其成功依赖于底层分类模型的选择。本研究旨在系统评估模型选择对InferBERT性能的影响，探究简单模型是否足够、领域特异性预训练是否有帮助、扩展到LLM是否能提升因果检测效果，以及事后校准的影响。

Method: 在两个基准数据集（镇痛药诱导的急性肝衰竭AILF和曲马多相关死亡TRAM）上进行比较研究，评估四种模型：XGBoost（基线）、ALBERT（原始InferBERT）、BioBERT（生物医学Transformer）和Med-LLaMA（医学LLM）。采用5折交叉验证重复20次，测量准确率、校准误差（ECE，等温回归前后）、因果术语与PRR/ROR/EBGM的Jaccard一致性，并使用配对t检验检验显著性。

Result: BioBERT在两个数据集上均取得最高准确率；Med-LLaMA尽管规模大且采用参数高效微调，表现却不佳；领域特异性预训练是决定性因素；校准改善了ECE但对准确率和因果发现效果不一；BioBERT与传统药物警戒信号的一致性最强。

Conclusion: 领域特异性预训练相比简单基线和大型LLM具有明显优势。在计算药物警戒中，投资于可管理的、领域感知的模型比单纯扩大模型规模更有效。

Abstract: Distinguishing causal adverse drug events (ADEs) from spurious correlations remains a central challenge in pharmacovigilance. The InferBERT framework integrates transformer models with Do-calculus, but its success hinges on the underlying classification model. This study evaluates the impact of model choice in InferBERT, assessing whether simpler models suffice, if domain-specific pre-training helps, whether scaling to LLMs improves causal detection, and the effect of post-hoc calibration. We performed a comparative study on two benchmarks: Analgesics-induced Acute Liver Failure (AILF) and Tramadol-related Mortalities (TRAM). Four models were evaluated-XGBoost (baseline), ALBERT (original InferBERT), BioBERT (biomedical transformer), and Med-LLaMA (medical LLM)-using 5-fold cross-validation repeated over 20 runs. We measured accuracy, Expected Calibration Error (ECE) pre- and post-isotonic regression, and Jaccard concordance of causal terms with PRR, ROR, and EBGM; significance was tested with paired t-tests. BioBERT achieved the highest accuracy on both datasets, while Med-LLaMA underperformed despite its size and parameter-efficient fine-tuning. Domain-specific pre-training was decisive. Calibration improved ECE but had mixed effects on accuracy and causal discovery. BioBERT's superiority also yielded the strongest concordance with traditional pharmacovigilance signals. These results show that domain-specific pre-training provides a clear advantage over simpler baselines and larger LLMs. Investing in manageable, domain-aware models is more effective for computational pharmacovigilance than simply scaling model size.

</details>


### [186] [Probing, Fusion, and Trustworthiness: A Systematic Evaluation of Foundation Model Representations for Multimodal Cancer Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17115)
*Jingyu Hu,Giuseppe Tripodi,Reed Naidoo,Sarah F. McGough,Tapabrata Chakraborti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文系统评估了基础模型（FM）在计算病理学任务中对分布外数据的泛化能力，利用两个真实世界商业队列（IH-BC和IH-NSCLC）的全切片图像和转录组数据，通过单模态和多模态融合实验发现：FM表征在分布外数据上表现优异，多模态融合仅在单一模态信号不占主导时有效，而共形预测表明大多数预测失败时真实诊断仍可被预测集覆盖，验证了不确定性感知推理的临床价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型在医学数据中已展示出强大的表征提取能力，但其对分布偏移数据集的泛化性尚未被充分探索，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 使用两个真实世界商业队列（IH-BC和IH-NSCLC）中的全切片图像和转录组数据，首先在8个下游分类任务上对5个基础模型进行单模态探测性能基准测试，然后比较三种图像-组学融合策略，最后通过共形预测评估模型的可信度。

Result: FM表征在分布外数据上取得竞争性性能；多模态融合仅在单一模态不占主导时带来额外增益；共形预测表明大部分点预测失败时真实诊断仍可在预测集中找回。

Conclusion: FM表征对分布外数据具有良好泛化性，多模态融合需谨慎应用，不确定性感知推理对于临床支持具有重要价值。

Abstract: Foundation models (FMs) have emerged as powerful representation extractors for medical data, yet their generalizability to datasets under distribution shift remains underexplored. This work systematically evaluates FM-based representations on a suite of computational pathology tasks across two real-world commercial cohorts, IH-BC and IH-NSCLC, drawn from the licensed in-house (IH) oncology dataset. The analysis focuses on two modalities, whole-slide images and transcriptomic profiles, drawn from the IH multimodal data. We first benchmark unimodal probing performance across five FMs on eight downstream classification tasks, and find that image and omics representations carry complementary predictive signals. Then we investigate whether multimodal fusion can yield additional gains over unimodal baselines by comparing three image-omics fusion strategies built on paired representations. The trustworthiness of selected unimodal and multimodal pipelines is further assessed through conformal prediction. Our results show that FM representations achieve competitive performance on out-of-distribution data and that multimodal fusion helps mainly when no single modality dominates the signal. Conformal prediction reveals that in the majority of cases where a point prediction fails, the true diagnosis remains recoverable within the prediction set, reinforcing the value of uncertainty-aware inference for clinical support.

</details>


### [187] [MODE: Modality-Decomposed Expert-Level Mixed-Precision Quantization for MoE Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17118)
*Yuanteng Chen,Peisong Wang,Zhilei Liu,Nanxin Zeng,Yuantian Shao,Shiqiang Lang,Tao Liu,Chuangyi Li,Qinghao Hu,Gang Li,Jing Liu,Jian Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MODE提出了一种模态分解的专家级混合精度量化框架，通过分解跨模态和视觉内两个层面的专家选择频率偏差，结合ILP优化给MoE-MLLM分配每专家位宽，在W3A16下性能损失仅2.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE-MLLM虽然性能优越但GPU显存开销巨大，现有专家级混合精度量化方法在迁移到MoE-MLLM时表现不佳，原因是忽视了两种专家重要性估计偏差：跨模态层面视觉token数量优势掩盖了文本关键专家，视觉内层面冗余视觉token进一步扭曲频率统计。

Method: (1) 按模态分解专家选择频率；(2) 过滤冗余视觉token得到去噪视觉频率；(3) 按模态评估量化敏感性作为频率估计的互补信号；(4) 将上述信号整合为整数线性规划（ILP）问题，在给定预算下为每个专家分配位宽。

Result: MODE在W3A16设置下平均性能损失控制在2.9%以内，在极端2-bit设置下取得更大增益，特别适合MoE-MLLM。

Conclusion: 通过模态分解策略有效解决了MoE-MLLM中专家重要性估计的偏差问题，实现了更优的混合精度量化压缩效果。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts Multimodal Large Language Models (MoE-MLLMs) offer remarkable performance but incur prohibitive GPU memory costs, making compression essential. Among PTQ methods, expert-level mixed-precision quantization has proven effective for MoE-LLMs, yet suffers notable degradation on MoE-MLLMs due to two overlooked biases in expert importance estimation. (1) At the cross-modal level, the numerical dominance of vision tokens causes expert selection frequency to be dominated by vision tokens, masking experts that are critical to the text modality; (2) at the intra-vision level, the large proportion of redundant vision tokens further skew frequency statistics, obscuring experts critical for informative visual content. To bridge gaps, we propose MODE, a modality-decomposed expert-level mixed-precision quantization framework for MoE-MLLMs that decomposes expert selection frequency by modality, filters redundant vision tokens to obtain denoised visual frequency, and further evaluates quantization sensitivity per modality as a complementary signal to frequency-based estimation. These signals are integrated into an Integer Linear Programming formulation to assign per-expert bit-widths under a given budget. Extensive experiments show that MODE is particularly well-suited for MoE-MLLMs, limiting average performance loss to within 2.9% at W3A16, with larger gains at the extreme 2-bit setting.

</details>


### [188] [Noise-Driven Escape from Metastable Phases explains Grokking in Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17120)
*Ibrahim Talha Ersoy,Karoline Wiesner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 深度神经网络在L2正则化强度变化下表现出第一阶相变，每个相变标志着新可学习特征的出现。研究发现，grokking（突然延迟的泛化现象）与第一阶L2相变中的迟滞效应一致：模型被困在低精度的亚稳态中，只有SGD噪声驱动其跨越能量壁垒才能逃逸，逃逸时间遵循阿伦尼乌斯标度。亚稳态的数量等于可学习特征的数量（每个数据协方差奇异值对应一个），为理解深度学习中的泛化延迟现象提供了新的理论解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络具有良好的泛化能力，但grokking现象（长时间过拟合后突然泛化）的起源尚不明确。本文旨在揭示grokking现象背后的物理机制，探索其与L2正则化下第一阶相变中迟滞效应的关系。

Method: 使用线性深度神经网络作为研究对象，通过L2正则化工程化地制造陷阱，将模型刻意困在低精度亚稳态中，观察SGD噪声驱动其跨越能量壁垒的行为，验证逃逸时间是否符合阿伦尼乌斯标度。采用稀疏子采样方法复现经典grokking曲线。

Result: 1. Grokking与第一阶L2相变中的迟滞效应一致；2. 被困模型逃逸时间遵循阿伦尼乌斯标度，在逃逸时间两个数量级范围内复现了grokking式的延迟收敛；3. 通过稀疏子采样复现了测试误差最终趋近训练误差的经典grokking曲线；4. 亚稳态数量等于可学习特征数量（每个数据协方差奇异值对应一个），表明迟滞潜力随任务复杂度自然增长；5. 提供证据表明相同机制可能适用于非线性DNNs。

Conclusion: Grokking现象可由L2正则化下第一阶相变中的迟滞效应解释，这为更高效的学习策略设计提供了理论依据和可行路径。

Abstract: Deep neural networks (DNNs) exhibit first order phase transitions under variations of the L2 regularization strength, with each transition marking the onset of a new learnable feature. Below a critical regularization strength, all features are in principle learnable, but coexisting metastable states, separated by energy barriers, can trap the network and impede convergence. A strength of DNNs is their ability to generalize. But many open questions remain, among them the origin of so called grokking: the abrupt, delayed onset of generalization after prolonged apparent overfitting. We show for linear DNNs that grokking is consistent with hysteresis in first-order L2 phase transitions: using L2 regularization to engineer deliberate trapping, we demonstrate that a model in a low-accuracy metastable state escapes only when SGD noise drives it across an energy barrier, with escape times following Arrhenius scaling. We reproduce grokking-like delayed convergence across two orders of magnitude in escape time by deliberately trapping models in metastable phases. Using sparse sub-sampling we also reproduce the canonical grokking curve where test error eventually approaches the final training error. Our work suggests that the number of metastable states equals the number of learnable features -- one per singular value of the data covariance -- the potential for hysteresis grows naturally with task complexity. We provide evidence that the same mechanism likely operates in general nonlinear DNNs. Our results provide routes toward more efficient learning schemes.

</details>


### [189] [Towards Fast GNN Surrogates for CO2 Migration in Complex Geological Formations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17180)
*Rodrigo S. Luna,Thiago H. N. Coelho,Luiz S. L. Neto,Roberto M. Velho,Adriano M. A. Cortes,Renato N. Elias,Alexandre G. Evsukoff,Fernando A. Rochinha,Mauricio Araya-Polo,Herve Gross,Alvaro L. G. A. Coutinho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图神经网络的端到端代理模型，用于预测地质封存中CO₂羽流的迁移，在SPE11A基准测试中展示了竞争性的气体饱和度与液相密度预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数值模拟CO₂地质封存中的多相流行为计算成本高昂，亟需高效的替代模型来加速预测与监测。

Method: 将计算网格构建为图，节点代表计算单元，边编码基于传导率的交互及几何属性；采用各向异性消息传递机制，通过几何条件化的边嵌入计算交互权重；在潜空间中使用自回归残差公式进行多步监督训练的时间演化建模。

Result: 该模型在SPE11A基准任务上对气体饱和度和液相密度产生了具有竞争力的预测，在扩展预测范围内累积误差保持适度。

Conclusion: 数据驱动的图神经网络方法能够有效重现多相流在复杂地质中的关键物理行为，为CO₂地质封存监测提供了一种高效的代理模型方案。

Abstract: This chapter discusses how a data-driven machine learning approach can reproduce key aspects of the physical behavior of multiphase flows in complex geological formations. We propose an end-to-end graph neural surrogate tailored to CO$_2$ plume migration forecasting in geological storage. The method is evaluated on the SPE11A benchmark, a well-known industry test case designed to assess CO$_2$ storage scenarios and characterized by sharp gas-water interfaces, strong advective transport, and rapid convective mixing with fingering development. The benchmark is reformulated as a graph in which nodes represent computational cells and edges encode transmissibility-based interactions enriched with geometric attributes. Directional transport arising from grid geometry, permeability contrasts, and geological heterogeneity is captured through an anisotropic message-passing mechanism, where interaction weights are computed via geometry-conditioned edge embeddings, biasing message aggregation toward physically relevant transport directions. Temporal evolution is modeled in latent space using an autoregressive residual formulation trained with multi-step supervision. The proposed model produces competitive forecasts of gas saturation and liquid-phase density, which are key indicators for CO$_2$ storage monitoring, with cumulative errors that remain moderate over extended forecasting horizons.

</details>


### [190] [Verified Detection and Prevention of Concurrency Anomalies in Multi-Agent Large Language Model Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17182)
*Sajjad Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对多智能体LLM系统中的共享状态并发问题进行了形式化建模，定义了四种并发异常（stale-generation, phantom-tool, causal-cascade, tool-effect reordering），构建了一个经机械验证的一致性层级L0⊊...⊊L4，并通过274个Verus验证义务在三个部署的Rust运行时中实现了该层级，同时复现并修复了ByteDance's deer-flow和LangGraph中的实际问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统通过记忆存储、向量索引和工具注册表共享状态，但这种共享缺乏形式化的并发一致性保证，导致可能出现数据异常。现有系统（如ByteDance的deer-flow和LangGraph）已暴露出实际的并发问题，亟需形式化的理论和验证方法。

Method: 在TLA+中形式化了四种并发异常，建立了它们在确定性重放语义下的排除格（exclusion lattice），并从中提取了一条最大链L0⊊...⊊L4。使用Verus验证工具开发了274个验证义务（零assume/零admit），证明检测器对规范的完备性和正确性，以及每个运行时对其避免集的满足性。在三个部署的Rust运行时中实现了L0-L4的不同层级。

Result: 三个Rust运行时分别实现了L0-L1（悲观锁、可串行化快照隔离、默认SI）；L2-L4通过执行模式验证，使用无依赖预防双胞胎（A3、A6、A2实现了0/1000 vs 1000/1000）；L2在三个模型族上实机运行（A3在全部120次回撤会话中被预防）。复现了deer-flow中的静默丢失更新并形式化修复为L0→L1精化，展示了LangGraph中因L3提交顺序导致的tool-effect重排序并通过sequencer消除。

Conclusion: 贡献在于机械验证的检测器、精化证明和可实现性工件；并发现象和格结构本身是经典的。这是首个针对此类运行时的机器检验一致性层级。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems share state through memory stores, vector indices, and tool registries. We model such sharing as long-running read-generate-write operations under deterministic-generation semantics -- the regime durable-execution engines enforce by deterministic replay -- and formalize four concurrency anomalies in TLA+: stale-generation, phantom-tool, causal-cascade, and tool-effect reordering, structural analogues of classical isolation anomalies, each with a TLC counter-example. The exclusion lattice over these anomalies is trivial; the contribution is the mechanically verified realizability and strict separation of one maximal chain within it, $L_0 \subsetneq \cdots \subsetneq L_4$, to our knowledge the first machine-checked consistency hierarchy for such runtimes. A development of 274 Verus obligations (zero assume, zero admit; trust base: two structural axioms and a mutex correspondence) proves the detectors sound and complete against the specifications and each runtime its avoidance set. Three deployed Rust runtimes realize L0-L1 (pessimistic locking, serializable snapshot isolation, default-SI), each verified against stale-generation and refined to its state machine; L2-L4 are exec-mode-verified with dependency-free prevention twins (A3, A6, A2: 0/1000 versus 1000/1000), and L2 is run live across three model families (A3 prevented in all 120 retracted sessions). We reproduce a silent lost update in ByteDance's deer-flow, formalizing its fix as a verified $L_0 \to L_1$ refinement, and exhibit tool-effect reordering in LangGraph's ToolNode on unmodified output, removed by an L3 commit-order sequencer. The verified detector, refinements, and realizability artifacts are the contribution; the phenomena and lattice are classical.

</details>


### [191] [Finsler Geometry, Graph Neural Networks, and You](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17185)
*T. Mitchell Roddenberry,Richard G. Baraniuk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于芬斯勒拉普拉斯算子的图神经网络架构，证明了离散估计在点云采样中的收敛性，并展示了该网络能够恢复非线性扩散方程背后的几何结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于图拉普拉斯的GNN只能近似各向同性的拉普拉斯-贝尔特拉米算子，限制了其对非线性各向异性算子的表达能力。本文旨在引入芬斯勒拉普拉斯算子作为非线性替代，扩展GNN的表达能力。

Method: 在流形点云上估计芬斯勒拉普拉斯算子，证明离散估计收敛到真实算子；将该算子表达为图神经网络层，构建受芬斯勒几何约束的GNN家族。

Result: 理论上证明了离散芬斯勒拉普拉斯估计在点云采样增长时收敛到流形上的真实算子；实验上展示了芬斯勒GNN能够恢复非线性扩散方程的底层几何结构。

Conclusion: 芬斯勒图神经网络能够有效表达各向异性非线性扩散过程，为图神经网络在非线性几何问题上的应用提供了理论基础和实践方法。

Abstract: Graph neural network architectures based on the graph Laplacian approximate the Laplace-Beltrami operator, thus limiting their application to isotropic operators. As a nonlinear alternative to the Laplace-Beltrami operator, we consider estimates of the Finsler Laplacian on point clouds sampled from a manifold. We prove that these discrete estimates converge to the true operator on the manifold as the number of point samples grows. Moreover, we show that this operator can be expressed as a graph neural network layer, which we use to define a family of Finslerian graph neural networks constrained to express Finsler geometry. We show that Finslerian graph neural networks recover the geometry underlying nonlinear diffusion equations in practice.

</details>


### [192] [Constrained Diffusion Models with Primal-Dual Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17192)
*Samar Hadou,Yigit Berkay Uslu,Alejandro Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出原始-对偶推理（PDI）方法，通过联合推断最优对偶变量和原始分布，实现带平均约束的熵正则化优化问题的约束扩散采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有约束扩散采样方法通常先估计对偶乘子再冻结它进行生成，无法在采样过程中适应性地调整，导致约束满足不佳。

Method: PDI在逆向扩散中联合推断最优原始分布及其参数化对偶变量：每个去噪步骤使用当前乘子对应的条件分数场去噪，然后通过估计约束违反度进行对偶上升更新乘子；训练单一的对偶条件分数网络覆盖推理中遇到的对偶变量族。

Result: 证明了对偶变量的时间平均收敛到对偶最优值的一个邻域，并通过调度相关的稳定性因子约束了对偶残差对终端分布的影响。在高斯混合、无线资源分配和投资组合管理任务上验证有效。

Conclusion: PDI通过联合推断原始分布和对偶变量，实现了对平均约束的精确满足，克服了传统"先估计再冻结"范式的局限。

Abstract: This paper develops constrained diffusion models with primal-dual inference (PDI) to sample from optimal distributions of entropy-regularized optimization problems with \emph{average} constraints. We formalize constrained sampling in the Lagrangian dual domain, where the optimal distribution takes the form of a Gibbs distribution indexed by the optimal dual variable. Rather than estimating this dual multiplier before sampling and freezing it throughout generation, PDI jointly infers the optimal primal distribution and its parametrizing dual variable. Each reverse diffusion step denoises using the score field associated with the current multiplier and then updates the multiplier through dual ascent using the estimated constraint violation of the denoised samples. To enable this conditional score field, we train a single dual-conditioned score network over the family of Gibbs distributions induced by the dual variables encountered during inference. We prove that the time average of the dual variables generated along the inference trajectory converges to a neighborhood of the dual optimum and bound the effect of residual dual mismatch on the terminal distribution through schedule-dependent stability factors. We evaluate PDI on constrained sampling from a mixture of Gaussians, wireless resource allocation, and portfolio management.

</details>


### [193] [PowerOPD: Stabilizing On-Policy Distillation with Bounded Power Transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17199)
*Anhao Zhao,Junlong Tong,Yingqi Fan,Ping Nie,Wenjie Li,Xiaoyu Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PowerOPD通过Box-Cox幂变换构造原生有界且符号一致的奖励函数，解决了标准在线蒸馏（OPD）中log-ratio奖励无界导致的高方差梯度、训练不稳定等问题，在数学推理基准上取得显著提升，同时降低59.2%的时间开销和23.1%的GPU内存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准在线蒸馏（OPD）使用学生采样token的单样本蒙特卡洛估计近似反向KL散度，但该估计器存在样本效率低、生成动态不稳定、与全词表OPD性能差距大等问题。诊断发现根本原因是log-ratio奖励无界，导致梯度方差极高且集中在早期位置。

Method: 提出PowerOPD，对奖励函数应用Box-Cox幂变换（参数α>0），构造原生有界且符号一致的奖励族。log-ratio是α→0的退化极限情况，而α>0时奖励自然有界。

Result: 在6个数学推理基准和4组Qwen3师生对上，PowerOPD相比原始OPD的Avg@8/Pass@8提升最高+6.37/+5.71；相比事后稳定化提升+3.01/+3.54；相比全词表OPD提升+2.59/+8.90。同时缩短59.2%运行时间和23.1%峰值GPU内存，梯度范数比原始OPD小3000倍以上。

Conclusion: PowerOPD通过简单的幂变换从根本上解决了在线蒸馏中奖励无界导致的训练病理问题，在性能、效率和稳定性上全面优于现有方法，且更大的α值通常带来更好的精度和更短的响应长度。

Abstract: Standard on-policy distillation (OPD) for large language models estimates the reverse-KL objective using student-sampled tokens, yielding an unbiased single-sample Monte Carlo estimator that avoids vocabulary-wide computation. However, we show that this estimator suffers from severe training pathologies in practice: sample inefficiency, unstable generation dynamics, and a substantial performance gap compared to exact full-vocabulary OPD. Reward-level diagnosis traces these pathologies to the log-ratio reward, which is unbounded by construction, producing extremely high-variance gradients concentrated at early positions and persisting throughout training; standard post-hoc scaling fail as they operate only after this distortion occurs. To solve this problem, we propose PowerOPD: a family of natively bounded, sign-consistent rewards from the Box-Cox power transformation, parameterized by alpha > 0, of which the log-ratio is the degenerate alpha -> 0 limit. Across six mathematical reasoning benchmarks and four Qwen3 teacher-student pairs, PowerOPD achieves benchmark-averaged Avg@8/Pass@8 gains of up to +6.37/+5.71 over vanilla OPD, +3.01/+3.54 over post-hoc stabilization, and +2.59/+8.90 over full-vocabulary OPD, while reducing wall-clock time by 59.2% and peak GPU memory by 23.1%. Larger alpha generally improves accuracy, consistently shortens responses, and keeps gradient norms more than 3,000x smaller than vanilla OPD.

</details>


### [194] [Sum-of-Squares Degree Barriers for the Reweighted-Hinge Method in Robust Halfspace Learning: A Christoffel-Function Characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17215)
*Xiaoyu Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示异常值移除证书的盲区大小恰好由干净分布的Christoffel函数决定，并将其作为重加权铰链方法的核心组织原则，推导出γ-间隔半空间在恶意噪声下鲁棒学习的三个结论：间隔-度数权衡、度数-2异常值屏障以及度数-2t算法的最优前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解有界度数异常值移除证书的固有限制——即证书只能通过低阶矩"看到"数据，而攻击者利用这一盲区隐藏异常值。作者试图量化这一盲区的大小，并将其作为鲁棒学习的组织原则。

Method: 将Christoffel函数从异常检测视角翻转，从攻击者角度量化可隐藏的异常值质量；采用Sum-of-Squares (SoS) 度数作为证书资源度量；使用加权-Chebyshev归约建立间隔-度数的紧致阈值关系；构造显式实例展示度数-2与度数-4的能力差异。

Result: (1) 间隔-度数权衡：达到ε误差所需SoS度数为Ω(log(1/ε))，或间隔为Ω(√log(1/ε)/√d)；(2) 度数-2异常值屏障：度数-2被卡在η^{1/2}，而度数-4可突破；(3) 度数-2t算法实现前沿η^{1-1/2t}，且常数最优，不可被度数-2改进。

Conclusion: Christoffel函数精确刻画了SoS证书的盲区大小，为鲁棒学习中的度数-精度权衡提供了紧致下界，解释了Shen (2025)中log(1/ε)间隔的必然性，并定位了方法的小故障率在证书度数而非分析本身。

Abstract: A certificate that removes outliers sees the data only through its low-degree moments, and an adversary exploits exactly this, hiding corruption where the clean data already looks typical, in the blind spot no bounded-degree test resolves. That blind spot turns out to have an exact size: the Christoffel function of the clean marginal, the very quantity modern data analysis thresholds to detect outliers, here read from the adversary's side as the corruption a bounded-degree certificate cannot remove. We turn this inversion into the organizing principle of the reweighted-hinge approach to robustly learning $γ$-margin halfspaces under malicious noise (Shen, 2025; Zeng and Shen, 2025): the governing resource is the Sum-of-Squares degree of the outlier-removal certificate, and the resolution principle states that the maximal corruption mass which can hide at a center $c$ from a degree-$2t$ certificate is exactly the Christoffel function $λ_{t+1}(c)$ of the clean marginal. Three consequences follow, all against the certificate method (not information-theoretic). A margin-degree tradeoff: certifying the dense pancake to error $ε$ costs SoS degree $Ω(\log(1/ε))$ or margin $Ω(\sqrt{\log(1/ε)}/\sqrt{d})$, explaining why the $\log(1/ε)$ margin Shen (2025) records is forced, with a weighted-Chebyshev reduction making the threshold $2t=Θ((|c|/s)^2)$ tight modulo one classical weighted-extremal estimate. A degree-$2$ outlier barrier: the resolution principle realized as an explicit instance on which degree $2$ is stuck at $η^{1/2}$ while degree $4$ escapes, locating the method's small breakdown rate in the degree, not the analysis. And a degree-$2t$ algorithm tracing the frontier $η^{1-1/2t}$ (recovering Shen (2025) at $t=1$), whose gain is an explicit constant, capped by the pancake density and shown unimprovable by the degree-$2$ barrier.

</details>


### [195] [Rift: A Conflict Signature for Deception in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17229)
*Petr Nyoma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现，知道真相却故意说谎的模型在其前向传播中会留下一种可检测的“冲突签名”（残差秩显著升高），该签名能100%准确区分谎言与诚实错误，且跨模型架构、语言和欺骗策略泛化，但无法被注入（只读）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决ELK（Eliciting Latent Knowledge）的核心难题：行为评估无法区分“知道真相但撒谎”的模型与“诚实地给出错误答案”的模型。需要找到一种内部表征层面的方法，能在输出完全相同的情况下，识别出模型是否在知情撒谎。

Method: 构造两类模型对比：睡梦特工（知真相，触发时撒谎） vs 天真的说谎者（微调输出相同错误答案但无诚实训练）。通过分析前向传播中的残差秩（residual rank）差异来捕获“知识冲突”信号。实验覆盖GPT-2小/中、Qwen2.5-1.5B/7B、Phi-3-mini等多种模型，并测试了自我构建谎言、主动隐藏、长度控制等变体。

Result: 欺骗性前向传播的残差秩是天真的说谎者的2.1-2.3倍；可100%准确识别谎言（无标签）；跨模型家族零样本迁移平均AUC 0.933；跨五种语言AUC 1.000；该签名是只读的，无法被注入（8/8双向均失败）；通过了全部六个负面实验的检验。

Conclusion: 模型内部的知情撒谎存在一种通用的、可检测且可迁移的内部签名，这为ELK问题提供了一种超越行为评估的解决方案，同时也揭示了当前模型在欺骗检测方面的内在脆弱性（签名只读，不可伪造）。

Abstract: A model that lies while knowing the truth is the central case ELK cannot handle with behavioral evaluation alone. We ask whether such deception leaves an internal signature distinguishing it from honest error. Our key move is a control for wrongness: we contrast a sleeper agent (knows the truth, lies on trigger) against a naive liar (fine-tuned to emit the same wrong answers with no honest training). Both produce identical wrong outputs; any difference is about knowledge conflict, not incorrectness. We find deceptive forward passes carry a conflict signature - 2.1-2.3x higher residual rank than naive-liar passes on the same wrong answer - strong enough to identify which of two responses is the lie with 100% accuracy and no labels, across GPT-2 small/medium (three seeds) and three instruct models. Across Qwen2.5-1.5B/7B and Phi-3-mini, instructed deception raises residual rank on every tested fact (18/18, 40/40, 34/34); on Phi-3, lies separate perfectly from both honest answers and hallucinations (AUC 1.0, Wilcoxon p~6e-11). The signature survives strategic self-constructed deception (model invents its own lie, AUC 1.0), active concealment attempts (AUC 1.0), and length-controlled replication (20/20, AUC 1.0, p~1e-6). Using basis-free relative representations, a probe trained on one model family detects deception in two other families zero-shot (mean AUC 0.933), surviving simultaneous architecture and format change (AUC 0.821), and transfers across five languages (AUC 1.000, length-controlled). The signature is read-only: detectable but not injectable (0/8 both directions). Honest limitations and six negative experiments are documented in full.

</details>


### [196] [Uncertainty Quantification of Engineering Structures by Polynomial Chaos Expansion and Multivariate Active Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17233)
*Qitian Lu,Jafar Jafari-Asl,Panagiotis Spyridis,Lukas Novak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对多输出工程问题，提出了一种面向向量值感兴趣量的自适应序贯采样方法，通过平衡局部方差贡献与空间探索，改进多项式混沌展开代理模型的精度和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多输出工程模型中，单一实验设计难以同时充分表示所有输出，而分别采样会增加计算成本并忽略输出间的相关性，因此需要一种兼顾所有输出的自适应采样策略。

Method: 将自适应序贯采样方法推广至向量值感兴趣量，基于候选点对各输出方差的局部贡献进行选择，同时平衡输入空间的距离探索（exploration）与跨输出聚合方差信息的利用（exploitation），构建多项式混沌展开代理模型。

Result: 通过多个工程数值算例与拉丁超立方采样对比，所提策略在代理模型精度和稳定性上均有提升，且能更可靠地估计二阶统计量。

Conclusion: 该方法有效地解决了多输出场景下单一实验设计代表性不足的问题，在保持数据一致性的同时降低了计算成本。

Abstract: In many engineering applications, a single high-fidelity model produces multiple quantities of interest (QoIs) under the same input parameters, e.g. finite element models of complex physical systems. To alleviate the high computational cost of direct model evaluations, surrogate models are widely used to construct efficient approximations of model responses. Naturally, the accuracy of surrogates strongly depends on the quality of the experimental design (ED). However, a single ED may not provide an adequate representation for all outputs simultaneously, especially when different outputs exhibit varying sensitivities to the input variables. A straightforward solution is to perform separate sampling for each output, but this results in increased sampling complexity and computational cost. From a statistical perspective, such an approach also ignores potential correlations among all outputs and may compromise data consistency. To address this issue, an adaptive sequential sampling method for constructing polynomial chaos expansion surrogate models is generalized for vector valued QoIs. The method sequentially selects new samples from a candidate pool based on their local contribution to the output variance, while balancing distance-based exploration of the input space and exploitation of aggregated variance information across all outputs. Its performance is compared with non-sequential Latin Hypercube Sampling through several numerical examples from engineering problems. Numerical results demonstrate that the proposed strategy improves both surrogate accuracy and stability, and provides a more reliable estimation of second-order statistics.

</details>


### [197] [Rethinking Groups in Critic-Free RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17250)
*Yihong Wu,Liheng Ma,Lingfeng Xiao,Muzhi Li,Xinyu Wang,Yingxue Zhang,Jian-Yun Nie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出负令牌过滤策略，通过防止对负样本的错误惩罚，实现单次 rollout 的稳定训练，在推理和智能体任务上达到或超越组级 RL 方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无评论家 RL 方法依赖同问题多组 rollout 来估计价值基线，存在数据效率低、组同步瓶颈、与结构化 rollout 不灵活等问题。本文旨在揭示“组”的底层功能并设计更高效的训练策略。

Method: 重新审视“组”的作用，指出其核心功能并非估计基线而是防止对负样本的错误惩罚；提出负令牌过滤（negative token filtering），一种支持单次 rollout 训练的简单策略，并应用于两种批级优势方法。

Result: 在推理任务上与组级 RL 方法性能相当，在智能体任务上表现更强。

Conclusion: 负令牌过滤是一种有效替代组级基线估计的策略，可提升数据效率并降低组同步开销。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a central paradigm for post-training large language models. Existing critic-free RL methods typically generate a group of rollouts for the same question to estimate value baselines for advantage computation. However, this design suffers from data inefficiency, group synchronization barriers, and inflexibility with structured rollouts. In this work, we revisit the role of the ``group'' and show that its underlying function is not merely to estimate baselines but to prevent false penalties on negative samples. Building on this insight, we propose negative token filtering, a simple and effective strategy that enables stable single-rollout training. We apply it to two batch-level advantage methods, achieving comparable performance on reasoning tasks and stronger performance on agentic tasks relative to group-based RL techniques.

</details>


### [198] [ProCUA-SFT Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17321)
*Jaehun Jung,Ximing Lu,Brandon Cui,Muhammad Khalifa,Shaokun Zhang,Hao Zhang,Jin Xu,Amala Sanjay Deshmukh,Karan Sapra,Andrew Tao,Yejin Choi,Jan Kautz,Mingjie Liu,Yi Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ProCUA-SFT，一个包含310万步级SFT样本的数据集，来自93K合成轨迹（覆盖2,484个应用组合），通过全自动流水线生成。在UI-TARS 7B上微调1个epoch后，OSWorld成功率从26.3%提升至45.0%，对比AgentNet训练的模型（8-10%）提升超35个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最大规模CUA轨迹数据集AgentNet（22.5K条）在SFT时会导致负迁移（OSWorld成功率从26.3%降至8-10%），亟需高质量、大规模、多样化的SFT数据来有效训练计算机使用智能体。

Method: 构建全自动流水线：(i) 以真实世界内容（SpreadsheetBench的912个表格、Zenodo10K的约10K个演示文稿、OSWorld多应用配置）为种子，在真实桌面上合成具身任务；(ii) 通过二元前置条件检查验证任务可行性；(iii) 单一VLM（Kimi-K2.5）同时承担目标生成器、前置条件判断器和轨迹执行器，消除规划-执行能力差距；(iv) 每条轨迹扩展为步级前缀样本，精确复现推理时的上下文布局。

Result: UI-TARS 7B在ProCUA-SFT上微调1个epoch后，OSWorld成功率达45.0%，相比基础模型提升18.7个百分点，相比AgentNet训练的模型（8-10%）提升超35个百分点。ProCUA子集还被用于Nemotron 3 Nano Omni模型的训练。

Conclusion: 大规模、多样性、经可行性验证的合成SFT数据（而非人类轨迹）能够有效训练通用计算机使用智能体，避免负迁移问题。

Abstract: Training computer-use agents (CUAs) -- models that interact with graphical desktops through screenshots and keyboard/mouse actions -- requires large-scale, diverse trajectory data collected in full desktop environments. The largest public resource, AgentNet (22.5K human trajectories), leads to negative transfer when used for supervised fine-tuning (SFT): continuing training UI-TARS 7B on AgentNet causes OSWorld success rate to fall from 26.3% to 8-10%. We present ProCUA-SFT, a dataset of 3.1M step-level SFT samples distilled from 93K synthetic trajectories across 2,484 application combinations. The dataset is produced by a fully automated pipeline that (i) synthesizes grounded tasks on live desktops seeded with real-world content -- 912 spreadsheets from SpreadsheetBench, approximately 10K permissively-licensed presentations from Zenodo10K, and multi-application OSWorld configs -- and (ii) verifies each task's feasibility through binary precondition checking before rollout. A single VLM (Kimi-K2.5) serves as goal generator, precondition judge, and trajectory executor, eliminating planner-actor capability gaps. Each trajectory is expanded into step-prefix samples that exactly reproduce the context layout seen at inference time. Fine-tuning UI-TARS 7B on ProCUA-SFT for one epoch yields 45.0% on OSWorld -- an 18.7 percentage-point improvement over the base model and over 35% above AgentNet-trained counterparts. A subset of ProCUA was incorporated into the training data for the Nemotron 3 Nano Omni model, contributing to its computer-use capabilities.

</details>


### [199] [Decision-Driven Geosteering Under Uncertainty: A Unified Framework for Sequential Decision Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17331)
*Hibat Errahmen Djecta,Sergey Alyaev,Kristian Fossum,Reidar B. Bratvold,Ressi Bonti Muhammad,Apoorv Srivastava*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将粒子滤波（用于概率性地下解释）与基于价值的强化学习（用于序贯决策）相结合的不确定性感知地质导向框架，在相同不确定性表征下比较了ADP、深度Q学习和双深度强化学习三种决策策略，并通过工业模拟器进行了高保真验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地质导向需要在不完全了解地质构造的情况下进行井轨迹导航，并基于随钻获取的间接测量数据序贯更新决策。现有方法未能在统一的概率框架下整合不确定性表征与序贯决策。

Method: 采用粒子滤波显式表征钻头前方地质不确定性，将信念更新与信念知情的决策策略耦合；比较了三种策略：可解释的近似动态规划、深度Q学习基线、以及采用目标Q网络和决斗架构的双深度强化学习，并使用稳定性导向指标评估策略行为。

Result: 通过工业地质导向模拟器的API进行验证，在相同地质实现、操作限制和奖励定义下进行了受控高保真度实验，评估了不同决策策略在整个钻井过程中的行为表现。

Conclusion: 该框架将不确定性表征与序贯决策紧密集成，提供了超越最终轨迹性能的、基于稳定性指标的决策行为评估，为实际地质导向提供了更具操作性的洞察。

Abstract: Geosteering requires navigating a well trajectory through an unknown geological configuration, while sequentially updating decisions based on indirect measurements acquired during drilling. This work presents an uncertainty-aware geosteering framework that tightly integrates particle filtering for probabilistic subsurface interpretation with value-based reinforcement learning for sequential decision-making. Geological uncertainty ahead of the drill bit is represented explicitly through a particle filter (PF), enabling belief-informed control rather than deterministic trajectory correction.
  The framework couples PF belief updates with belief-informed decision policies and evaluates three decision-making options that operate under identical uncertainty representations: an interpretable Approximate Dynamic Programming (ADP) scheme, a Deep Q-learning baseline, and a Dual Deep Reinforcement Learning (Dual DRL) architecture trained with a target Q-network scheme for stability, using a dueling (value/advantage) decomposition for Q-value parameterization. Beyond final placement performance, we assess policy behavior using stability-oriented metrics that quantify steering smoothness over time, providing additional operational insight into how decision policies respond as uncertainty evolves.
  The framework is integrated with an API for validation within an industrial geosteering simulator under realistic measurement noise and drilling constraints. Using identical geological realizations, operational limits, and reward definitions across methods, the experiments provide a controlled and high-fidelity evaluation of how alternative decision policies behave throughout the drilling process, rather than evaluating performance solely from the final well trajectory.

</details>


### [200] [Counterfactual Optimization of Baseball Pitch Sequences and Estimation of Its Impact on Season-Level Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17345)
*Ryota Takamido,Hiroki Nakamoto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究使用Transformer模型和反事实分析方法，利用MLB Statcast数据证明了投球序列中最终球和设置球的选择优化对投手赛季级表现（如K/9提升超过1.0）有显著影响，并提供了速度区间有效位置、投球命令重要性等实践见解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究主要关注单个打席中最终投球的优化，忽视了前置设置球的作用及其对长期赛季级表现的影响。

Method: 使用Transformer机器学习模型预测目标投球是否会导致击入界内或挥空；通过反事实分析替换最终球或前置设置球的球种和位置，固定其他上下文信息；选择最小化预测击入概率的反事实方案；使用回归模型将模型输出与投手赛季统计数据关联，估计优化方案的赛季级效果。

Result: 最终球和设置球的优化可能显著影响赛季级表现，包括K/9提升超过1.0；提供了速度区间有效位置、投球命令重要性、中速球扩展投球选择等实践见解。

Conclusion: 量化支持了投球序列在棒球中的战略重要性。

Abstract: Although pitch sequencing is a central topic in baseball analytics, previous studies have primarily focused on optimizing the final pitch within a single plate appearance, leaving the role of preceding setup pitches and their impact on long-term season-level performance insufficiently examined. To address these issues, this study conducted counterfactual analyses using MLB Statcast data. A Transformer-based machine-learning model was trained to predict whether a target pitch would result in an in-play outcome or swing-out. Counterfactual pitch sequences were then generated by replacing either the final pitch or the preceding setup pitch with alternative pitch types and locations while keeping the surrounding contextual information fixed. Optimal counterfactual selections were defined as those that minimized the predicted in-play probability, and their expected effects on pitchers' seasonal statistics were estimated using regression models linking model outputs to season statistics. The results suggest that the optimization of both final and setup pitches may substantially influence season-level performance, including improvements of more than 1.0 in K/9. The analyses also provided several practical insights, including velocity-band-specific effective locations, the importance of pitch commands, and the expansion of pitch-selection options through middle-velocity pitches. These findings quantitatively support the strategic importance of pitch sequencing in baseball.

</details>


### [201] [MM++: Unsupervised Scale-Invariant Multilayer OOD Detection via Top-K Gated Feature Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17352)
*Rahim Hossain,Md Tawheedul Islam Bhuian,Md Farhan Shadiq,Kyoung-Don Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MM++提出了一种全无监督、严格事后且尺度不变的多层Mahalanobis框架用于分布外检测，通过熵密度下降识别判别性中间层，融合跨层特征并引入Ledoit-Wolf正则化协方差矩阵，在无需辅助OOD数据、微调或架构修改的情况下实现鲁棒的远近OOD检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决OOD检测中尺度不变性与层次表达性之间的权衡问题，现有方法依赖辅助数据、微调或架构修改，缺乏无监督且事后可用的统一框架。

Method: 1) 通过测量熵密度下降识别判别性中间层，标记语义压缩边界；2) 融合选定中间层与终端表示，捕获跨层相关性并抑制早期层噪声；3) 采用Ledoit-Wolf正则化绑定协方差矩阵稳定统一特征空间，实现可靠距离估计。

Result: 在不同架构下对近OOD和远OOD检测均表现出稳健性能。

Conclusion: MM++在完全无监督、事后处理的设定下，无需辅助OOD数据、分类器微调或架构修改，即可有效实现OOD检测。

Abstract: We introduce MM++ (Multilayer Mahalanobis++), a fully unsupervised, strictly post-hoc, and scale-invariant framework for Out-of-Distribution (OOD) detection. To address the trade-off between scale invariance and hierarchical expressivity, MM++ constructs a principled joint feature space. It first identifies discriminative intermediate layers by measuring entropy density drops, which mark the boundaries of sharp semantic compression. By fusing these selected layers with the terminal representation, the framework captures latent cross-layer correlations while mitigating early-layer noise. Crucially, a Ledoit-Wolf regularized tied covariance matrix stabilizes this unified space, enabling reliable distance estimation. Requiring no auxiliary OOD data, classifier fine-tuning, or architectural modifications, MM++ delivers robust performance across distinct architectures for both near- and far-OOD detection.

</details>


### [202] [Performance-Driven Environment Abstraction with Multi-Timescale Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17377)
*Yue Guan,Dipankar Maity,Panagiotis Tsiotras*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种面向决策性能的大规模MDP环境抽象方法，通过状态聚合与动作共享建立受控近似，并开发多时间尺度强化学习框架联合优化策略与树结构抽象，实现状态压缩与样本效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有环境抽象方法多关注保留几何或拓扑结构，而非直接优化决策质量，缺乏从决策性能角度进行状态聚合的理论指导。

Method: 将抽象建模为通过状态聚合和动作共享的受控近似；推导固定分区下的性能保证（分离值函数近似误差与动作共享损失）；基于此分析开发多时间尺度RL框架，根据Q值差异自适应细化或粗化状态空间区域。

Result: 与actor-critic基线相比，实现了显著的状态压缩、更好的样本效率和更快的重规划能力。

Conclusion: 以决策质量为导向的环境抽象能够有效平衡性能与抽象复杂度，多时间尺度联合优化策略与抽象结构是一种可行且高效的方法。

Abstract: We study performance-driven environment abstraction for decision-making in large Markov decision processes. Rather than preserving geometric or topological structure, we seek abstractions that directly optimize decision quality. We model abstraction as a controlled approximation obtained by aggregating the state space and enforcing a shared action distribution within each aggregated state. For a fixed partition, we establish a performance guarantee that separates value-function approximation error from the loss introduced by action sharing. Guided by this analysis, we develop a multi-timescale reinforcement learning framework that jointly adapts the policy and a tree-structured environment abstraction. The resulting algorithm refines and coarsens regions of the state space based on Q-value discrepancies, balancing performance against abstraction size and complexity. Empirical results demonstrate substantial state compression, improved sample efficiency, and faster replanning compared to actor-critic baselines.

</details>


### [203] [The Discrete-Log Clock: How a Transformer Learns Modular Multiplication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17399)
*Huu Danh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现，在小规模Transformer学会模乘法后，其嵌入的密集傅里叶谱是分析基（加法DFT）不匹配任务代数结构（乘法群）造成的伪像。改用乘法特征变换（基于乘法群不可约表示），谱变得高度稀疏（基尼系数从0.07升至0.58），仅4个关键频率携带主要能量；96.9%的MLP神经元被单一乘法频率干净地调谐，且按离散对数重排后的神经元激活热图呈现二维周期结构。这表明Transformer实现了离散对数空间中的加法，即“离散对数时钟”算法。方法论上，将分析基与任务的代数结构匹配，能揭示被标准工具掩盖的可解释结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作发现Transformer在模乘法上学习到的嵌入具有“密集”傅里叶谱（需要所有频率），这与模加法仅需稀疏关键频率的情况形成鲜明对比。作者认为这种“密度”是使用错误基（标准加法DFT）进行分析的伪像，实际任务（乘法群）的代数结构要求使用乘法特征变换。

Method: 对在a·b mod 113上训练的groked Transformer，将标准加法DFT替换为乘法特征变换（基于乘法群(Z/pZ)^*的不可约表示）进行分析。计算嵌入谱的基尼系数，统计MLP神经元对单一乘法频率的调谐比例，并按离散对数重排神经元激活热图以观察周期性结构。

Result: 乘法特征变换下嵌入谱高度稀疏：基尼系数从加法基的0.07提升至0.58；仅4个关键频率携带主要能量；96.9%的MLP神经元被单一乘法频率干净调谐；重排后的激活热图呈现二维周期结构。

Conclusion: Transformer将模乘法约简为离散对数空间中的加法，实现了类似Nanda等人加法Clock算法的“离散对数时钟”算法。方法论启示：匹配分析基与任务的代数结构，能揭示标准工具视为噪声的可解释结构，该思路可泛化至其他代数结构任务。

Abstract: When small transformers grok modular multiplication, prior work reports that the learned embedding has a "dense" Fourier spectrum requiring all frequencies. This contrasts with modular addition, where only a sparse set of key frequencies suffices. We show this density is an artifact of analyzing in the wrong basis. The natural Fourier transform for multiplication is not the standard additive DFT but the multiplicative character transform, which decomposes functions on the multiplicative group $(\mathbb{Z}/p\mathbb{Z})^*$ into its irreducible representations. Applying this transform to a grokked transformer trained on $a \cdot b \bmod 113$, we find the embedding spectrum becomes highly sparse (Gini coefficient 0.58 vs. 0.07 in the additive basis) with only 4 key frequencies carrying significant energy. Furthermore, 96.9% of MLP neurons are cleanly tuned to a single multiplicative frequency, and neuron activation heatmaps reveal 2D-periodic structure when reordered by the discrete logarithm. These results demonstrate the transformer reduces multiplication to addition in discrete-log space, implementing a "Discrete-Log Clock" algorithm analogous to Nanda et al.'s Clock algorithm for addition. The methodology generalizes: matching the analysis basis to the algebraic structure of the task reveals interpretable structure where standard tools see noise.

</details>


### [204] [Discrete Autoregressive Transformer for Generative Mechanism Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17409)
*Anar Nurizada,Anurag Purwar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于条件自回归序列建模的平面路径综合方法，利用解码器仅含Transformer和VAE潜变量，在超过一百万个机构的数据集上实现从目标曲线到机构参数的生成，取得了优异的几何误差指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平面路径综合需要机构连杆曲线匹配给定轨迹，而曲线到连杆机构的映射本质上是一对多的，涵盖四杆、六杆和八杆拓扑结构。现有方法难以高效处理这种一对多映射并生成多样化、准确的机构方案。

Method: 将综合问题形式化为条件自回归序列建模：关节坐标均匀量化后作为token，由解码器仅含Transformer模型生成，模型以目标曲线的VAE潜变量和显式机构类型token为条件。训练采用token交叉熵损失和高斯平滑分箱辅助损失。推理时，通过有界潜变量噪声调度在所有噪声水平下解码各类机构，取几何误差最小的前五个候选。

Result: 在保留测试集上，聚合平均Chamfer距离为0.0132，平均动态时间规整为0.153；基于训练集邻居潜变量的k近邻基线方法在相同解码器下达到匹配拓扑的平均Chamfer距离0.0071和平均动态时间规整0.117。

Conclusion: 该方法无需数据集查找即可生成多样且准确的机构系列，为平面路径综合提供了一种有效的生成式解决方案。

Abstract: Planar path synthesis requires mechanisms whose coupler curves match a prescribed trajectory; the mapping from curve to linkage is inherently one-to-many across four-, six-, and eight-bar topologies. We address this design problem with simulation-grounded evaluation on a curated corpus of over one million mechanisms, reporting Chamfer distance and dynamic time warping after forward kinematics and geometric alignment. We formulate synthesis as conditional autoregressive sequence modeling: joint coordinates are uniformly quantized to tokens and generated by a decoder-only transformer with a variational-autoencoder (VAE) latent of the target curve and an explicit mechanism-type token. Training combines token cross-entropy with a Gaussian-smoothed bin auxiliary loss that respects ordinal structure among bins. At inference, a bounded latent-noise schedule decodes all mechanism types at each noise level; we retain the top five candidates by geometric error, yielding diverse accurate families without dataset lookup. On held-out tests, aggregate mean Chamfer distance is $0.0132$ and mean dynamic time warping is $0.153$; a latent $k$-nearest-neighbor baseline that conditions on training-set neighbor latents in VAE space achieves matched-topology mean Chamfer distance $0.0071$ and mean dynamic time warping $0.117$ using the same decoder.

</details>


### [205] [Amortized Probabilistic Retrieval of Atmospheric CO2 from OCO-2 Spectra Using Deep Learning with Laplace Approximations and Normalizing Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17413)
*Alejandro Calle-Saldarriaga,Felix Jimenez,Jack Grosskreuz,Jiazheng Wang,Jonathan Hobbs,Matthias Katzfuss*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度学习的框架，利用多分支神经网络和高保真模拟数据集，通过拉普拉斯近似和归一化流两种可扩展的不确定性量化方法，实现对OCO-2卫星XCO2的快速、准确反演，相比传统全物理求解器在计算速度、模型误差鲁棒性、预测精度、不确定性校准和非高斯后验建模方面具有显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前OCO-2卫星的CO2柱浓度反演算法计算成本高昂且无法正确量化不确定性，亟需一种高效且能够提供可靠不确定性估计的替代方法。

Method: 构建高保真模拟数据集（包含前向模型误差），采用多分支神经网络编码光谱波段，并使用拉普拉斯近似和归一化流两种可扩展方法估计CO2柱浓度的后验分布或目标统计量。

Result: 该方法在五个方面优于传统全物理求解器：推理速度提升数个数量级、对模型误差具有鲁棒性、点估计精度更高、不确定性校准更优、能够建模非高斯后验分布。

Conclusion: 基于模拟的深度学习是实现下一代业务化卫星CO2反演处理系统的可行途径。

Abstract: Space-based monitoring of atmospheric carbon dioxide (CO2) is essential for constraining the global carbon budget. NASA's Orbiting Carbon Observatory-2 (OCO-2) estimates column-averaged dry-air mole fractions of CO2 (XCO2) using high-resolution spectra. However, current operational retrieval algorithms are computationally expensive and do not properly quantify uncertainties. We present a novel deep learning framework that addresses these challenges. Due to the difficulties of ground-truth data for real satellite observations, we develop and validate our approach using a high-fidelity simulation dataset. This dataset, created to support OCO-2 uncertainty quantification (UQ), incorporates realistic forward model errors. Our architecture encodes spectral bands using a multi-branch neural network and estimates posteriors of the full CO2 column or desired summaries thereof using two scalable UQ methods: Laplace approximations and normalizing flows. Our approach has five key advantages relative to operational "full-physics" solvers: (1) Amortization: Inference is orders of magnitude faster, enabling real-time processing of massive data streams; (2) Model error robustness: By training on simulations that explicitly include model discrepancies, our method accounts for systematic errors often neglected by standard inversions; (3) Point estimate accuracy: We achieve superior predictive accuracy compared to baseline methods; (4) Improved UQ: The probabilistic outputs yield better-calibrated uncertainty estimates; and (5) Non-Gaussian posteriors: When utilizing normalizing flows, our framework successfully models complex, asymmetric posterior distributions, overcoming the limitations of the Gaussian assumption. These results suggest that simulation-based deep learning is a viable path toward next-generation operational processing systems.

</details>


### [206] [Memory-Efficient Meta-Reinforcement Learning for Adaptive Safety-Critical Control in Adversarial Spacecraft Proximity Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17414)
*Alejandro Posadas-Nava,Richard Linares,Minduli Wijayatunga*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究利用元强化学习（meta-RL）优化输入约束控制屏障函数（ICCBF）中的class-𝒦函数，以解决自主航天器交会与近距离操作（RPO）中的安全关键控制问题。实验比较了三种递归网络架构（LSTM、GRU、Mamba）和两种训练算法（PPO、SAC），结果表明Mamba结合PPO在合作与非合作场景下均实现最优的任务完成率、安全性和燃料效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在航天器自主交会与近距离操作中，需设计在推力约束下保证安全且最小化燃料消耗的控制器。现有ICCBF方法依赖人工设计的class-𝒦函数，难以适应复杂动态环境。因此，需要一种自适应、鲁棒的方法来学习最优的class-𝒦函数参数。

Method: 将ICCBF中的class-𝒦函数参数化，通过元强化学习（meta-RL）进行优化。系统比较了三种递归网络架构（LSTM、GRU、Mamba）和两种训练算法（PPO、SAC）的组合性能，并在合作与非合作（目标航天器对抗性行为）场景下进行评估。

Result: 状态空间模型Mamba结合PPO算法在所有合作与非合作测试场景中均取得了最优的任务完成率、安全性和燃料节省效果，优于LSTM和GRU架构。

Conclusion: 基于状态空间模型的Mamba架构与PPO算法的组合是ICCBF中class-𝒦函数元学习的最佳方案，能够有效应对航天器交会中的安全关键控制挑战，特别是在存在对抗性行为的情况下。

Abstract: Autonomous spacecraft rendezvous and proximity operations (RPO) require controllers that guarantee safety under thrust constraints while minimizing fuel expenditure. Input-constrained control barrier functions (ICCBFs) provide a control method for nonlinear systems with actuation constraints that construct a forward-invariant safe set. Previous work has shown that learning class-$\mathcal{K}$ functions defining the ICCBF recursion via meta reinforcement learning (meta-RL) yields a robust, non-greedy approach to safety-critical control in RPO. This paper extends that framework further by investigating the performance of three recurrent network architectures (Long Short Term Memory (LSTM), Gated Recurrent Unit (GRU), Selective State Space Model (Mamba)) and two training algorithms (Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) and Soft Actor Critic (SAC)) to identify the best setup for tuning ICCBF class-K functions via meta-RL. In addition to cooperative test cases, performance is evaluated in the presence of adversarial behavior where the target spacecraft behaves in a way that worsens the safety of the chaser spacecraft. Results indicate that state space models such as Mamba when used with PPO achieve superior task completion, safety, and fuel-savings compared to other architectures, across all cooperative and uncooperative scenarios tested.

</details>


### [207] [Generalization Guarantees for Multi-Input Neural Operator Learning in Sobolev Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17419)
*Yahong Yang,Zecheng Zhang,Wei Zhu,Wenjing Liao,Hao Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了多输入神经算子在Sobolev范数下的逼近与泛化误差估计，建立了多输入函数（可定义在不同域、不同维度和不同Sobolev正则性下）的误差边界理论，揭示了输入维度、正则性与Sobolev阶数对误差率的交互影响，并保留了模型复杂度上的log log/log型依赖结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经算子理论主要针对单输入函数设定，缺乏对多输入场景（输入函数来自不同定义域、不同维度和不同正则性空间）的理论分析，无法支撑PDE及科学计算中常见的多输入算子学习问题。

Method: 基于Sobolev范数作为输出误差度量，结合多输入函数空间的正则性假设与神经算子的逼近能力，推导出每个输入空间对最终误差边界的显式贡献，并通过平衡机制分析输入维度、正则性和Sobolev阶数之间的相互作用。

Result: 在平衡机制下，逼近与泛化速率由输入维度、正则性和Sobolev阶数的交互决定；模型复杂度的影响呈log log/log型结构；所得误差边界显式量化了每个输入空间的贡献。

Conclusion: 该工作为多输入算子学习（包括Sobolev训练）提供了通用理论框架，可应用于PDE及科学计算中的算子学习问题。

Abstract: We develop approximation and generalization error estimates for multi-input neural operators, with the output error measured in Sobolev norms. In contrast to standard operator-learning settings with a single input function, our framework allows multiple input functions defined on possibly different domains, with different dimensions and Sobolev regularities. The derived rates explicitly quantify the contribution of each input space to the final error bound. In particular, in the balanced regime, the approximation and generalization rates are governed by the interaction between the input dimensions, regularities, and Sobolev orders, while the dependence on the model complexity retains a \(\log\log/\log\)-type structure. Our analysis provides a general theoretical framework for multi-input operator learning, including Sobolev training, and is applicable to operator learning problems arising from partial differential equations and scientific computing.

</details>


### [208] [MorphStrata: Layer-Specific Perturbations for Generating Morphence Students in Time-Series Moving Target Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17435)
*Abhishek Bhardwaj,Arnav Doshi,Anusri Nagarajan,Thanh Quynh Nhu Ta,Mohammad Masum,Robert Chun,Jaydip Sen,Saptarshi Sengupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MorphStrata是一种针对时间序列预测模型的学生生成策略，通过对教师模型（Transformer骨干）的特定层注入随机噪声来构建结构化异构学生模型集成，作为移动目标防御（MTD）方法。实验表明，该方法在FGSM、BIM和PGD攻击下保持了对抗鲁棒性，在AEP数据集上相比静态基线RMSE降低最高达97.97%，且训练时间增加不超过1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列预测模型易受梯度对抗攻击，而防御机制（特别是移动目标防御MTD）在维持有限响应和计算成本的前提下牺牲鲁棒性，且维护多个随机模型实例会大幅增加训练开销。MorphStrata旨在解决这一鲁棒性与效率的权衡问题。

Method: 以Transformer骨干网络作为教师模型，对学生模型随机选择的架构层注入特定层级的随机噪声（selective, layer-specific stochastic noise injection），生成结构化异构学生模型集成，扩展了传统的Morphence防御范式。

Result: 在Jena Climate、Electricity Load Diagrams和Appliances Energy Prediction三个基准上，针对FGSM、BIM、PGD攻击进行评估。AEP数据集上，MorphStrata在epsilon=0.5时相比静态基线RMSE降低24.11%（FGSM）和97.97%（BIM）。训练时间增加小于1%，同时观察到学生模型间L2距离与防御效果正相关。

Conclusion: MorphStrata作为一种MTD防御方法，在边际成本增量下维持了对抗鲁棒性，相比现有基线具有显著优势。

Abstract: Time-series forecasting models remain vulnerable to gradient-based adversarial attacks while existing defense mechanisms typically incur a trade-off in robustness for bounded response and compute cost. The problem is pronounced in Moving Target Defense where maintaining multiple randomized model instances substantially exacerbates the training overhead. In this work, we introduce MorphStrata, a student generation strategy with selective, layer-specific stochastic noise injection that extends the traditional Morphence defense. MorphStrata uses a Transformer backbone as the teacher and perturbs randomly selected architectural blocks to create structured heterogeneity across student models in response to varied data distributions and threat models. We evaluate against vanilla Transformer and Morphence backbones on a suite of benchmarks including the Jena Climate, Electricity Load Diagrams, and Appliances Energy Prediction using FGSM, BIM and PGD attacks across multiple attack strengths. Across datasets and attack regimes, the proposed ensemble maintains comparable adversarial RMSE. Specifically, for high entropy, periodic datasets as in the case of the AEP data, MorphStrata achieves the lowest RMSE across all attacks and perturbation budgets, improving over the static baseline by up to 24.11% and 97.97% under FGSM and BIM respectively at an epsilon value of 0.5 over 30 randomized trials. Targeting the layers to generate MorphStrata students accounts for less than 1% increase in train-times over the Morphence MTD baseline for most of the experiments, while accounting for double digit gains in adversarial RMSE reduction. We also observe a positive correlation between higher pairwise L2 distance (among generated students) and overall defense effectiveness. In summary, MorphStrata maintains adversarial robustness as an MTD defense at marginal cost deltas when compared to existing baselines.

</details>


### [209] [Toward Controllable Catalyst Inverse Design via Large-Scale Autoregressive Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17445)
*Dong Hyeon Mok,Jonggeol Na,Seoin Back*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于GPT架构的条件催化剂生成模型，在1.33亿个催化剂结构上预训练，并通过约46万个优化结构微调，实现了98%的结构有效性和95%的优化有效性，结合显式属性条件控制，可将筛选效率提升1.5-4倍，为非均相催化剂的反向设计提供了实用路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非均相催化剂表面存在结构复杂性与耦合表面-吸附质相互作用，传统筛选方法难以高效探索广阔化学空间；机器学习高通量筛选效率随搜索空间增大而下降，亟需能够直接生成具有目标属性催化剂的生成模型。

Method: 基于GPT架构的条件催化剂生成模型，采用数值嵌入层实现分类属性和连续属性的统一自回归条件生成；在1.33亿催化剂结构上预训练，随后在约46万个优化结构上微调，结合分类属性和结合能进行条件生成。

Result: 模型实现98%结构有效性、95%优化有效性、93%吸附质类型和成分联合匹配率；结合能条件匹配率约20%（较基线提升4倍），生成分布系统性地向目标值偏移，无需额外微调即可实现1.5-4倍筛选效率提升。

Conclusion: 大规模自回归预训练结合显式属性条件控制，为可控催化剂生成和加速催化剂发现提供了实用途径。

Abstract: Inverse design of heterogeneous catalysts remains challenging because catalyst surfaces exhibit substantial structural complexity with coupled surface-adsorbate interactions across a vast chemical space that is difficult to explore efficiently through conventional screening alone. Although machine learning-based high-throughput screening has accelerated catalyst discovery, its efficiency inevitably declines as the search space grows, motivating the development of generative models that can directly construct catalysts with target properties. Here, we present a conditional catalyst generative model based on the Generative Pretrained Transformer architecture with a numerical embedding layer that enables the generation of catalyst structures conditioned on both categorical and continuous properties within a single autoregressive framework. The model was pretrained on 133 million catalyst structures and subsequently fine-tuned on approximately 460,000 optimized structures with associated categorical properties and binding energies for conditional generation. The resulting model achieved 98% structural validity, 95% optimization validity, and high categorical condition fidelity, with a 93 % joint match rate for adsorbate type and composition. For binding energy conditioning, the match rate of approximately 20% represents a four-fold improvement over the baseline training distribution, and the generated distributions shift systematically toward the target values, enabling a 1.5 to 4-fold improvement in screening efficiency for reaction-targeted catalyst discovery without additional fine-tuning. These results show that large-scale autoregressive pre-training, combined with explicit property conditioning, provides a practical route toward controllable catalyst generation and accelerated catalysts discovery.

</details>


### [210] [Credibility-Weighted Pricing of Autonomous Vehicle Liability Under Operational Design Domain Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17451)
*Doyeon Jang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对自动驾驶系统部署中的费率制定难题（稀疏经验、不断变化的ODD、跨软件版本的非平稳风险），提出了一种分层贝叶斯可信度框架，通过学习的ODD相似性核函数在城市、软件版本和区域间进行部分池化，将Buhlmann-Straub作为极限情况。基于Waymo在美国四个大都市的648起已验证碰撞事件及1.16亿匹配英里数据的验证表明，城市聚合可信度权重适中（0.12-0.46），部分池化显著优于无池化，功效分析显示学习核函数优势在约12个部署城市时可被检测到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶系统部署面临三个核心费率制定挑战：稀疏经验数据、运营设计域（ODD）的动态变化、以及跨软件版本的非平稳风险，亟需一种能够有效整合有限信息并进行跨维度信息借用的统计方法。

Method: 提出分层贝叶斯可信度框架，通过学习的ODD相似性核函数在城市、软件版本和区域间进行部分信息池化，该框架将经典Buhlmann-Straub模型作为极限情况嵌套其中。

Result: 基于NHTSA Standing General Order数据库中Waymo在四个美国大都市的648起已验证碰撞事件和1.16亿匹配英里数据，城市聚合可信度权重范围为0.12-0.46；部分池化方法显著优于无池化方法；功效分析表明学习核函数的统计优势在约12个部署城市时可被可靠检测。

Conclusion: 分层贝叶斯可信度框架能有效应对自动驾驶费率制定中的数据稀疏性和非平稳性挑战，部分池化策略显著提升估计精度，且ODD相似性核函数在足够多的部署城市（约12个）下能提供可检测的额外增益。

Abstract: Automated Driving System deployments create a foundational ratemaking challenge: sparse experience, shifting operational design domains, and non-stationary risk across software releases. We propose a hierarchical Bayesian credibility framework pooling across cities, software versions, and territories via a learned ODD-similarity kernel, nesting Buhlmann-Straub as a limiting case. Demonstrated on 648 verified-engaged Waymo crashes across four U.S. metros from the NHTSA Standing General Order database against 116 million matched miles, city-aggregate credibility weights are moderate (0.12-0.46), partial pooling decisively outperforms no pooling, and a power analysis shows the learned kernel's advantage becomes detectable at approximately twelve deployed cities.

</details>


### [211] [Operator Boosting Produces Pareto-Efficient PDE Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17460)
*Lennon J. Shikhman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Operator Boosting框架，通过分阶段残差学习训练小型神经算子代理模型，在多个PDE基准上实现了72-95%的参数减少，同时多数情况下保持或提升了预测精度，改善了经验精度-参数帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全尺寸神经算子模型在存储、部署和多次查询场景中成本高昂，现有方法通常先训练大模型再进行压缩，效率较低。本文旨在直接构建紧凑型神经算子代理模型，而非后压缩。

Method: 提出Operator Boosting框架：从归一化输出坐标的经验均值预测器出发，按阶段对残差场序列训练多个同族小型神经算子（FNO、DeepONet、CNO），并通过验证集选择的收缩因子整合每次修正，最终堆叠形成紧凑代理模型。

Result: 在30个数据集-架构组合中，21个显示出正向平均精度提升，17个具有正向置信区间；所有堆叠模型参数量减少约72-95%；在10个PDE基准中7个实现了帕累托改进，涵盖2D Navier-Stokes、浅水动力学、Darcy流、1D输运反应系统及3D可压缩Navier-Stokes等。

Conclusion: Operator Boosting通常能改善神经PDE代理模型的精度-参数帕累托前沿，但在某些PDE和架构依赖的场景中残差提升未能抵消压缩带来的损失，暴露了方法的局限性。

Abstract: Neural operators are widely used as surrogate solution maps for partial differential equations (PDEs), but full-size models can be costly to store, deploy, and evaluate in many-query scientific workflows. This work introduces Operator Boosting, a stagewise residual-learning framework for constructing compact neural-operator surrogates directly, rather than training a large model and compressing it afterward. Starting from the empirical mean predictor in normalized output coordinates, the method trains a sequence of tiny same-family neural operators on residual fields and incorporates each correction through validation-selected shrinkage. We instantiate the framework with Fourier neural operators (FNOs), DeepONets, and convolutional neural operators (CNOs), and compare boosted tiny stacks against full-size monolithic baselines across one-, two-, and three-dimensional PDE benchmarks from PDEBench, APEBench, and The Well. Across 30 dataset-architecture pairs, 21 show positive mean accuracy gains and 17 have positive confidence intervals, while all boosted stacks reduce trainable parameter count by approximately 72-95%. Best-model comparisons show empirical Pareto improvements on 7 of 10 completed PDE benchmarks, including two-dimensional Navier-Stokes, shallow-water dynamics, Darcy flow, one-dimensional transport and reaction systems, and three-dimensional compressible Navier-Stokes. These results show that Operator Boosting often improves the empirical accuracy-parameter Pareto frontier of neural PDE surrogates, while also exposing PDE- and architecture-dependent regimes where residual boosting fails to offset compression.

</details>


### [212] [ResAware: Cross-Environment Website Fingerprinting via Resource-Privileged Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17462)
*Chongru Fan,Wei Wang,Wentao Huang,Zhenquan Ding,Jinqiao Shi,Lei Cui,Zhiyu Hao,Xiaochun Yun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ResAware，一种跨环境资源感知蒸馏框架，通过教师模型学习资源级特征并将知识蒸馏给学生模型，使仅依赖加密流量的学生模型在现实环境中抵抗时空漂移、浏览器异构和代理混淆等扰动，显著提升网站指纹攻击的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网站指纹攻击在实验室环境准确率高，但因仅依赖低层流量特征（易受环境扰动影响），在真实环境中因时空漂移、浏览器异构、代理混淆等因素性能严重下降，亟需提升跨环境鲁棒性。

Method: ResAware采用训练丰富/推理稀缺的不对称设定：教师模型在资源级特征上训练，通过异构知识蒸馏将特权知识传递给学生模型；部署时学生模型仅用加密流量推理，零额外开销。

Result: 在5个月、6个全球分布 vantage points、超16万配对样本的大规模数据集上，ResAware显著增强多种WF基线的跨环境鲁棒性：150天时间漂移下，Var-CNN的F1分数从72.77%提升至81.49%，开放世界TPR@1%FPR从22.40%提升至27.20%。

Conclusion: 资源级监督能够在不扩大在线观测能力的前提下提升WF鲁棒性，为现实部署中应对环境变化提供了有效方案。

Abstract: While Website Fingerprinting (WF) attacks achieve high accuracy in controlled laboratory settings, they often degrade substantially in real-world environments due to spatio-temporal drift, browser heterogeneity, proxy obfuscation and etc. This limitation stems from their sole reliance on low-level traffic features that are noisy and highly sensitive to environmental perturbations. To address this problem, we propose \textbf{ResAware}, a cross-environment resource-aware distillation framework under a \textit{training-rich/inference-poor} asymmetric setting. Specifically, ResAware trains a teacher model on resource-level features, and then distills the resulting privileged knowledge into a student model through heterogeneous knowledge distillation. At deployment time, the student model performs inference using only encrypted traffic, incurring zero additional cost. We evaluate ResAware on a large-scale dataset collected over five months from six globally distributed vantage points, comprising more than $160{,}000$ paired samples. The results show that ResAware significantly enhances the cross-environment robustness of diverse WF baselines. Under a 150-day temporal drift, for example, ResAware improves the F1-score of Var-CNN from $72.77\%$ to $81.49\%$ and the open-world $TPR@1\%FPR$ from $22.40\%$ to $27.20\%$. Our results demonstrate that resource-level supervision improves WF robustness without expanding online observation capabilities.

</details>


### [213] [CheckMIABench: Firm Foundations For Membership Inference Attacks on Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17464)
*Jeffrey G. Wang,Jason Wang,Marvin Li,Seth Neel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于中间检查点的基准测试方法，用于对大型语言模型进行原则性的成员推理攻击评估，解决了以往研究中由于成员与非成员数据分布差异导致的统计有效性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM成员推理攻击评估存在成员与非成员数据分布偏移问题，导致"盲攻击"表现异常出色，亟需构建统计有效的评估基准。

Method: 利用训练过程中固定时间点前后的数据来自同一分布的洞察，将所有具有中间检查点的开源模型和公开训练数据转化为成员推理攻击测试平台。
