Result: 在Pythia和OLMo系列模型（70M到7B参数）上对六种已发表攻击进行了评估，并开源了模块化攻击库。

Conclusion: 通过消除分布偏移，为LLM成员推理攻击提供了更可靠的评估框架，促进了隐私研究的可复现性。

Abstract: Membership inference attacks (MIAs) are a canonical way to assess a machine learning model's privacy properties. Although several attempts have been made to evaluate MIAs on language models, the extant literature has suffered numerous difficulties in constructing clean evaluations to test new techniques. In particular, subtle distribution shifts between member and non-member sets can undermine the statistical validity of MIAs; recent work has underscored this by showing that "blind" methods with no access to the underlying model can perform far better than published methods on the same benchmarks. This paper constructs a benchmark for principled evaluation of MIAs against LLMs, by leveraging the insight that training data before and after a fixed point during training are drawn from the same distribution. Therefore, all open-source models with intermediate checkpoints and public training data can be converted into MIA testbeds. We apply our framework to a half-dozen published attacks on the Pythia and OLMo family of models, from 70M to 7B parameters. To facilitate further privacy research, we open-source a modular library for designing and implementing attacks in this setting: https://github.com/safr-ai-lab/pandora_llm.

</details>


### [214] [Perron--Frobenius Operator Matching for Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17465)
*Shiqi Zhang,Wuwei Wu,Jaemin Oh,Jie Chen,Xiaoning Qian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Perron-Frobenius算子匹配(PFOM)框架，通过积分PF算子统一流模型、扩散模型和跳跃模型，证明Bregman散度中仅KL散度保持密度级与样本条件目标的一致性，并开发了Nesterov加速的训练与采样方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型（流、扩散、跳跃模型）在数学框架上存在割裂，缺乏统一的理论基础；同时训练和采样的数值稳定性与收敛速度有待提升。

Method: 利用Perron-Frobenius算子匹配密度演化，建立统一的生成框架；从理论上证明KL散度是唯一保证密度级与样本条件目标等价的Bregman散度；引入Nesterov加速技术优化训练与采样过程。

Result: 在高斯混合模型和双月数据上，PFOM在KL散度/Wasserstein-2距离/最大均值差异（MMD）指标上实现更快下降，并提升了实际运行效率。

Conclusion: PFOM统一了算子理论识别与现代生成建模，为自适应字典和高维应用开辟了新路径。

Abstract: We introduce Perron--Frobenius Operator Matching (PFOM), a generative framework that matches density evolution via the integral PF operator, subsuming flow, diffusion, and jump models. We prove that among Bregman divergences, only Kullback--Leibler divergence preserves equality between density-level and sample-conditioned objectives, yielding a practical loss equivalent to Koopman path matching. We further develop Nesterov-accelerated training and sampling that stabilize discretization and accelerate convergence. %On Gaussian mixtures and two-moons, PFOM achieves faster KL/$W_2$/MMD decrease and improved wall-clock efficiency with empirical validation. PFOM unifies operator-theoretic identification with modern generative modeling and opens paths to adaptive dictionaries and high-dimensional applications.

</details>


### [215] [ReRAM-aware Model Finetuning addressing I-V Non-linearity and Retention Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17471)
*Ching-Yi Lin,Shamik Kundu,Arnab Raha,Sahil Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于微调的硬件感知训练算法，通过在微调过程中应用范围收缩的sinh变换缓解I-V非线性，并将保留误差纳入正则化损失，实现低开销的ReRAM部署。在ImageNet分类和SQuAD v2问答任务上，该方法在大型模型上几无精度损失（MobileNetV3系列<2%退化，SQuAD v2仅1点F1下降）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CPU/GPU/NPU受限于冯·诺依曼瓶颈，ReRAM存内计算虽具高密度、高能效优势，但其非理想特性（如I-V非线性、保留误差）限制了实际部署；现有硬件感知训练框架需从头训练，对大规模模型计算成本过高。

Method: 提出基于微调的硬件感知训练算法：(1) 应用范围收缩的sinh变换来缓解I-V非线性；(2) 将保留误差直接建模为正则化损失项，在微调过程中联合优化，从而避免从头训练的高昂开销。

Result: 在ResNet18、DeiT-Tiny等大规模模型上达到与基线相近的精度；ImageNet上MobileNetV3系列精度退化<2%；SQuAD v2数据集上F1分数仅下降1点。

Conclusion: 所提微调方案能以极低训练开销实现DNN在ReRAM上的鲁棒部署，有效克服非理想特性影响，在分类和问答任务上均验证了有效性。

Abstract: Traditional CPU, GPU, and NPU architectures are increasingly limited by the von Neumann bottleneck. While In-Memory Computing (IMC) using ReRAM crossbar arrays offers a high-density, energy-efficient alternative, its practical deployment is constrained through their non-idealities. Existing hardware-aware training frameworks often require training from scratch, which is computationally prohibitive for modern large-scale models. In this work, we propose a finetuning-based hardware-aware training algorithm that enables robust DNN deployment on ReRAM with minimal training overhead. Our approach mitigates I-V non-linearity by applying a range-shrunk sinh transformation and incorporates retention errors directly into a regularization loss during the finetuning process. We evaluate our framework across models and tasks such as image classification and question-answering (QA). Experimental results demonstrate that our method achieves similar accuracy on large-scale models like ResNet18 and DeiT-Tiny as the base model. In-case of ImageNet for MobileNetV3 families the technique has only less than 2% accuracy degradation. Further, applying the technique on the SQuAD v2 dataset results in only 1 point degradation of F-1 score.

</details>


### [216] [Multi-Adapter PPO: A Cross-Attention Enhanced Wavelength Selection Framework for LIBS Quantitative Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17476)
*Hao Li,Man Fung Zhuo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于多适配器PPO框架的激光诱导击穿光谱波长选择方法，通过强化学习解决高维光谱数据中的特征选择问题，在钢铁和煤炭数据集上相比PSO综合得分提升28.4%，预测精度提升45.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LIBS定量分析面临波长选择难题，高维光谱数据存在预测精度与特征效率之间的根本性权衡，现有方法难以有效平衡二者。

Method: 将波长选择建模为强化学习问题，提出Multi-Adapter PPO框架，利用交叉注意力机制和多个专用适配器捕获复杂光谱关系。

Result: 在钢铁和煤炭数据集上，相比传统粒子群优化(PSO)方法，综合得分平均提升28.4%，预测精度平均提升45.2%。

Conclusion: 该方法在预测精度与特征效率之间取得了更优平衡，实现了LIBS定量分析的最佳性能，同时保持了可解释性和计算效率。

Abstract: Laser-induced breakdown spectroscopy (LIBS) quantitative analysis faces critical challenges in wavelength selection due to high-dimensional spectral data and the fundamental trade-off between prediction accuracy and feature efficiency. This paper presents a novel Multi-Adapter PPO framework that transforms wavelength selection into a reinforcement learning problem, leveraging cross-attention mechanisms and multiple specialized adapters to capture complex spectral relationships. Our approach outperforms traditional Particle Swarm Optimization (PSO) by an average of 28.4\% in comprehensive score and 45.2\% in prediction accuracy across steel and coal datasets. The proposed method demonstrates superior performance in balancing prediction accuracy with feature efficiency, achieving state-of-the-art results in LIBS quantitative analysis while maintaining interpretability and computational efficiency. We released our code and dataset here: https://github.com/Hflying/MAPPO

</details>


### [217] [Online LLM Selection via Constrained Bandits with Time-Varying Demand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17489)
*Yin Huang,Qingsong Liu,Jie Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对边缘-云推理系统中大语言模型（LLM）的在线选择问题，将其建模为带硬性（资源预算）和软性（服务质量）约束的随机赌博机学习任务，提出一种基于置信区间估计和需求预测的在线学习算法，在动态资源受限环境下实现亚线性遗憾和亚线性约束违反的理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘-云推理系统中存在异构LLM，不同任务对准确率、延迟和成本有差异化需求，而模型性能特征未知、任务需求时变，传统的静态选择策略无法适应实际部署中的硬性资源预算和软性服务质量约束。

Method: 将LLM在线选择问题形式化为带约束的随机赌博机学习问题，同时处理packing型（硬）约束和covering型（软）约束；提出利用置信区间估计和需求预测的在线学习算法，在仅依赖部分反馈（无奖励、成本或延迟分布先验）的条件下进行序贯决策。

Result: 理论分析证明了算法相对于具有完整信息的离线基准具有亚线性遗憾和亚线性覆盖约束违反；在合成负载上的实验验证了该方法在动态、资源受限环境中的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提出的在线学习方法能够有效应对LLM异构性、性能随机性以及时变任务需求，在满足多类型约束的同时实现接近最优的累积奖励，为边缘-云场景下LLM的自动化部署提供了理论支撑和实用方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in edge-cloud inference systems to handle diverse user tasks with heterogeneous accuracy, latency, and cost profiles. Selecting the appropriate LLM for each incoming task is critical for ensuring service quality and efficient resource utilization. However, model heterogeneity, stochastic and unknown performance characteristics, and time-varying task demands make static selection strategies inadequate. Real-world deployments often impose hard resource budgets such as monetary expenditure limits, along with soft service-level requirements such as latency guarantees. These constraints introduce additional challenges for online decision-making. We formulate this problem as a constrained stochastic bandit learning task, where the learner sequentially selects models under both packing-type (hard) and covering-type (soft) constraints, while adapting to time-varying task demand. The learner operates without access to the underlying reward, cost, or latency distributions and must rely on partial feedback. We develop a novel online learning algorithm that leverages confidence-bound estimates and demand predictions to balance reward maximization with long-term constraint satisfaction. We provide theoretical guarantees showing sublinear regret and sublinear covering constraint violations compared to an offline benchmark with full information. Experimental results on synthetic workloads demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of our approach in dynamic, resource-constrained environments.

</details>


### [218] [Reconfigurable Computing Challenge: Transformer for Jet Tagging on Versal AI Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17500)
*Gram Koski,Sean Lipps,Zhenghua Ma,G. Abarajithan,Ryan Kastner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在AMD Versal AI Engine上部署量化整数Transformer用于喷注标记的框架，将稠密层和多头注意力层映射到AIE瓦片上，支持从Python模型自动生成Vitis图代码，并作为开源软件发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer模型在喷注标记任务上表现优异，但难以部署在低延迟、资源受限的LHC触发系统中，因此需要高效的硬件加速方案。

Method: 将量化的整数-only Transformer中的稠密层和多头注意力层映射到AMD Versal AI Engine的AIE瓦片上；开发了可复用的软件框架，将Transformer层表示为可组合的AIE构建模块，并自动从高层Python模型描述生成对应的Vitis图代码。

Result: 实现了量化整数Transformer在AIE上的初始部署，提供了自动代码生成能力，并开源了框架代码。

Conclusion: 该框架为未来在资源受限的触发系统中部署Transformer模型的研究奠定了基础，并促进了相关领域的研究共享。

Abstract: Transformer-based models achieve strong performance for jet tagging at the CERN LHC, but deploying them in low-latency, resource-constrained trigger systems is challenging. We present an initial implementation of a quantized, integer-only transformer for jet tagging on the AMD Versal AI Engine (AIE), mapping dense and multi-head attention (MHA) layers to AIE tiles. The main contribution is a reusable software framework that represents transformer layers as composable AIE building blocks and automatically generates the corresponding Vitis graph code from a high-level Python model description. This framework provides a foundation for future research and is released as open-source software at https://github.com/KastnerRG/particle_transformer_aie.

</details>


### [219] [When the Next Step Is Not One Step: Distribution-Aware Execution Modeling for Concurrent Go Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17508)
*Kaviru Hapuarachchi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用并发程序调度非确定性作为训练信号，通过运行程序多次聚合下一事件的经验分布，使用KL散度微调7B模型。在798个真实Go生产bug预测中达到36.2%准确率（超过Gemini 3.5 Flash零样本的34.8%），分布训练相比交叉熵将期望校准误差从0.205降至0.169。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并发程序中调度器具有非确定性，同一程序从相同轨迹前缀出发可能产生多个合法的下一事件，传统单标签预测模型本质是在猜测随机过程的某一结果。作者希望扭转这一困境，将非确定性本身作为训练信号来提升模型预测能力。

Method: 对每个程序多次运行，聚合观测到的下一事件形成经验分布；使用KL散度目标对7B模型进行微调，使其输出分布匹配经验分布；同时在select阻塞的goroutine场景中形式化推导了P(GoUnblock)=0的goroutine泄漏签名。

Result: 在798个来自CockroachDB、Kubernetes、gRPC、etcd等真实Go生产bug的预测任务中，微调少于1000条轨迹达到36.2%准确率，超过Gemini 3.5 Flash零样本的34.8%和未微调模型的28.6%。分布训练与交叉熵准确率相当（35.8% vs 36.2%），但期望校准误差从0.205降至0.169。

Conclusion: 利用调度非确定性进行分布训练能有效提升并发程序下一事件预测的准确率和校准性能；形式化的goroutine泄漏签名可辅助检测特定类型的阻塞问题；数据集、适配器和工具已开源。

Abstract: Training a model to predict the next step in a concurrent program is harder than it looks: two runs of the same program from the same trace prefix can produce different next events, both valid, because the scheduler is nondeterministic. A model trained against a single label is learning to guess one outcome of a random process. We turn this around and use the nondeterminism as a training signal. We run each program many times, aggregate the observed next events into an empirical distribution, and fine-tune a 7B model to match that distribution with a KL objective. On 798 held-out predictions drawn from real production Go bugs (CockroachDB, Kubernetes, gRPC, etcd), fine-tuning on fewer than a thousand traces reaches 36.2% accuracy, ahead of Gemini 3.5 Flash used zero-shot (34.8%) and the same model without fine-tuning (28.6%). Distribution training matches cross-entropy on accuracy (35.8% vs. 36.2%) while reducing Expected Calibration Error from 0.205 to 0.169. We also derive a formal goroutine-leak signature for a class of select-blocked goroutines where P(GoUnblock)=0 holds by scheduler semantics, not by learning. We release the dataset, trained adapters, and all tooling.

</details>


### [220] [Geometry-Aware Post-Hoc Uncertainty Quantification in Operator Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17513)
*Oriol Vendrell-Gallart,Nima Negarandeh,Ramin Bostanabad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: REEF-GP提出了一种后验不确定性量化框架，通过在冻结的神经算子残差上拟合高斯过程，利用算子内部嵌入定义核特征空间，实现了几何感知的高效不确定性量化，在五个PDE基准上以远低于深度集成的成本达到了校准的不确定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子在物理模拟中提供快速替代模型，但其确定性预测限制了在需要不确定性量化（UQ）任务中的应用，尤其面对几何变化时。现有方法主要建模网络参数的不确定性，忽略了算子本身学习的几何感知表示。

Method: 提出REEF-GP框架，在冻结的神经算子残差上拟合高斯过程，算子的内部嵌入定义核特征空间；采用谱归一化投影、异方差几何感知噪声和高效的基于子集的训练，避免低秩近似约束。

Result: 在五个不同几何的PDE基准上，REEF-GP保持预测精度的同时实现了校准的不确定性估计，性能与深度集成相当但成本大幅降低；在几何分布偏移下保持鲁棒，不确定性集中在物理有意义的区域（如激波前沿）。

Conclusion: 准确且可扩展的后验UQ可以直接在神经算子的学习特征空间中实现，为以参数为中心的方法提供了实用替代方案。

Abstract: Neural operators provide fast surrogates for PDEs but their deterministic predictions limit their use in tasks requiring uncertainty quantification (UQ), especially under geometric variability. Existing approaches primarily model uncertainty in network parameters, largely overlooking the geometry-aware representations learned by the operator itself. We propose REEF-GP (Residual on Embedded Features Gaussian Process), a post-hoc UQ framework that fits a GP to the residuals of a frozen neural operator whose internal embeddings define the kernel feature space. Rather than learning a separate feature map, REEF-GP adapts the operator's intrinsic coordinate-feature representations to construct geometry-aware uncertainties. To ensure stability and scalability on unstructured domains, REEF-GP incorporates spectral-normalized projections, heteroscedastic geometry-aware noise, and efficient subset-based training that avoids restrictive low-rank approximations. Across five PDE benchmarks with varying geometries, REEF-GP preserves predictive accuracy while achieving calibrated uncertainty estimates competitive with deep ensembles but at a fraction of their cost. Our approach remains robust under geometric distribution shift, with uncertainty concentrating in physically meaningful regions (e.g., shock fronts). Our results demonstrate that accurate and scalable post-hoc UQ for neural operators can be achieved directly in their learned feature space, offering a practical alternative to parameter-centric approaches.

</details>


### [221] [FoundCause: Causal Discovery with Latent Confounders from Observational Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17516)
*Patrick Blöbaum,Krishnakumar Balasubramanian,Shiva Prasad Kasiviswanathan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FoundCause是一种基于合成数据训练的摊销因果发现模型，通过单次前向传播将数据集直接映射为因果图，显式建模隐式混杂因素，在15个真实数据集上超过11种非摊销方法和4种摊销方法，F1提升9.6%，AUROC提升1.2%，结构汉明距离降低18.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果发现从观测数据中恢复有向结构和隐式混杂因素仍然具有挑战性，现有方法需要干预实验或无法很好地处理潜在混杂。

Method: 提出FoundCause模型，采用置换不变transformer编码器，通过样本和变量间的交替注意力机制联合建模跨变量依赖和单变量分布；注入基于经典非对称度量的成对统计特征引导因果信号；采用因子化解码器分离边存在性与方向性；设计三角细化模块推理高阶因果结构（链、对撞）；使用可学习隐式标记的混杂模块显式建模隐式共同原因；通过掩码输入表示处理缺失数据。

Result: 在15个真实数据集上，相比11种经典非摊销方法（PC、GES、NOTEARS等）和4种摊销方法，F1提升9.6%，AUROC提升1.2%，结构汉明距离降低18.9%，且仅需单次前向传播完成推理。

Conclusion: FoundCause是首个显式建模隐式混杂的摊销因果发现方法，通过合成数据学习可迁移的统计模式，实现了高效且精确的因果结构推断。

Abstract: Causal discovery from observational data remains challenging due to the need to recover directed structure and latent confounding without interventions. We propose FoundCause, an amortized causal discovery model trained entirely on synthetic data that maps datasets directly to causal graphs in a single forward pass. By learning from large collections of simulated structural causal models, FoundCause captures transferable statistical patterns that generalize beyond individual datasets. The architecture incorporates several key inductive biases for causal discovery. It uses a permutation-invariant transformer encoder with alternating attention over samples and variables to jointly model cross-variable dependence and per-variable distributions. Pairwise statistical features derived from classical asymmetry measures are injected through statistics-conditioned attention, guiding the model toward known causal signals. A factorized decoder separates edge existence from direction, while a triangular refinement module enables reasoning over higher-order causal motifs such as chains and colliders. In addition, a dedicated confounder module based on learnable latent tokens explicitly models hidden common causes, and the model explicitly handles missing data via its masked input representation. To our knowledge, FoundCause is the first amortized causal discovery approach to explicitly model latent confounding. FoundCause outperforms 11 classical non-amortized methods (e.g., PC, GES, NOTEARS-style optimization) and 4 amortized causal discovery methods on 15 real-world datasets, achieving +9.6% improvement in $F_1$, +1.2% in AUROC, and an 18.9% reduction in structural Hamming distance relative to the strongest non-amortized methods, while performing inference in a single forward pass.

</details>


### [222] [Reducing Learner Redundancy in Boosting via Residual Orthogonalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17567)
*Ye Su,Jipeng Guo,Yong Liu,Xin Xu,Gangchun Zhang,Jinxin Chen,Di Wu,Longlong Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SCBoost框架，通过从残差拟合转向残差正交化来解决标准boosting中的学习器冗余问题。核心包含两个机制：谱残差投影（SRP）在训练时将残差投影到历史预测子空间的正交补上，迫使后续学习器捕捉新信息；协方差正则化加权（CRW）在聚合时通过显式协方差惩罚优化集成权重。理论证明了SRP的加性残差能量分解及其在提高有效信噪比方面的条件。实验表明SCBoost在十个基准数据集上表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准boosting框架中的顺序残差拟合会反复关注相关的误差成分，导致学习器之间产生冗余，限制了集成效率。为突破这一瓶颈，需要从几何角度重新审视boosting过程。

Method: 提出SCBoost框架，包含两个互补机制：(1) 谱残差投影（SRP）——将每个残差目标投影到历史预测子空间的正交补上；(2) 协方差正则化加权（CRW）——在验证集上使用显式协方差惩罚项优化集成权重。

Result: 在十个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，SCBoost在准确率和F1分数上均展现出强大的开箱即用性能。

Conclusion: 本文通过几何视角重新诠释了boosting，表明显式冗余控制是构建更高效集成架构的原则性且必要的步骤。

Abstract: While sequential residual fitting is the bedrock of standard boosting frameworks, it inherently breeds learner redundancy by repeatedly revisiting correlated error components. To address this bottleneck, we propose a shift from residual fitting to \textit{residual orthogonalization} and introduce SCBoost. Our framework tackles redundancy through two complementary mechanisms: Spectral Residual Projection (SRP) and Covariance-Regularized Weighting (CRW). During training, SRP projects each residual target onto the orthogonal complement of the historical prediction subspace, forcing successive learners to capture only novel empirical innovations. During aggregation, CRW optimizes ensemble weights on a validation set with an explicit covariance penalty to mitigate remaining correlations. Theoretically, we provide a finite-sample geometric characterization proving that SRP yields an exact additive residual-energy decomposition. Furthermore, under an isotropic-noise assumption, we rigorously establish the conditions under which this projection improves the effective Signal-to-Noise Ratio. Extensive experiments across ten benchmark datasets demonstrate that SCBoost delivers strong out-of-the-box performance, particularly in accuracy and F1 score. This work reinterprets boosting through a geometric lens, suggesting that explicit redundancy control is a principled and necessary step toward more efficient ensemble architectures.

</details>


### [223] [When Dynamics Models Read the Wrong Time Steps: Label-Free Event Credit Re-Anchoring for Robust Global Readouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17572)
*Yifan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出并研究了序列到全局接口中的时间信用稀释问题（temporal credit dilution），即模型在轨迹级监督下依赖平滑相关特征而非关键物理事件进行预测。作者提出了Credit-in-Event探针来量化事件信用分配，并从理论证明了池化线性读取器在小事件占比下会将信用路由到虚假背景通道。进一步提出CREST方法，通过无训练、无标签的事件核心估计与对比重锚定来恢复事件信用，显著降低分布外误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习动力模型在序列到全局的接口中存在未被充分研究的时间信用分配问题：模型在轨迹级监督下可以利用平滑的、与目标相关的特征进行准确预测，而忽略了真正决定目标的短暂物理事件。这种失败模式无法被训练损失暴露，也无法被标准物理信息残差消除，因为误差本质在于全局读取头如何分配功能性信用。

Method: 1) 提出Credit-in-Event探针，用于测量池化信用有多少落在事件步上；2) 闭式证明池化线性读取器在小事件占比下会将信用路由到虚假背景通道；3) 提出CREST方法：从学习特征中估计瞬态事件核心，通过事件-背景对比重新锚定池化表示，无需额外训练或标签。

Result: 在模拟齿轮和冲击系统、循环和注意力编码器以及公开轴承振动数据上，CREST显著降低了分布外误差，同时恢复了事件信用。消融实验表明，稳定步选择和感受野收缩均失败，证实增益来自事件核心信用重锚定而非泛化的局部性或稳定性先验。

Conclusion: 序列到全局接口存在时间信用稀释问题，传统训练和物理信息残差无法解决；Credit-in-Event可作为诊断工具，CREST作为无训练、无标签的读取头替代方案能有效缓解该问题。

Abstract: Learned dynamics models often answer global physical questions, such as fault severity or impact stiffness, by pooling a per-step feature sequence into one readout vector. This sequence-to-global interface creates an under-studied temporal credit problem: with only trajectory-level supervision, a model can predict accurately in training conditions while reading from abundant smooth correlates rather than the brief physical events that determine the target. We call this failure temporal credit dilution. It is not exposed by the training loss and is not removed by standard physics-informed residuals, because the error lies in where the global readout assigns functional credit. We introduce Credit-in-Event, an interface-level probe for measuring how much pooled credit lands on event steps, and prove in closed form that a pooled linear reader routes credit to a spurious background channel as the event fraction shrinks. We then propose CREST, a training-free and label-free readout that estimates a transient event core from learned features and re-anchors the pooled representation through event-versus-rest contrast. Across simulated gear and impact systems, recurrent and attention encoders, and public bearing vibration data, CREST reduces out-of-distribution error while restoring event credit. Ablations show that stable-step selection and receptive-field shrinking fail, confirming that the gain comes from event-core credit re-anchoring rather than a generic locality or stability prior.

</details>


### [224] [LLM Features Can Hurt GNNs: Concatenation Interference on Homophilous Graph Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17579)
*Zhongyuan Wang,Pratyusha Vemuri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现，通过纯输入拼接（而非联合训练、蒸馏或提示条件化）将LLM生成的节点特征引入GNN，在原本同质性高的基准上会系统性降低准确率。例如在PubMed上，拼接SBERT编码的GPT-4o-mini TAPE特征导致MLP骨干网络测试准确率下降17.0±0.3个百分点。作者提出一个名为Delta_sig的简单指标来预测拼接何时有帮助或有害，并观察到该指标与拼接成本的相关性（r²=0.38）强于同质性（r²=0.06）。通过维度控制消融实验和幂律关系建模，进一步揭示了这一现象的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献广泛报道LLM生成的节点特征能提升GNN准确率，但作者观察到在纯输入拼接条件下，LLM特征会系统性降低同质性基准上的性能。这一矛盾现象亟需解释，以便更可靠地决定何时以及如何使用LLM特征增强GNN。

Method: 使用MLP骨干网络、Planetoid公共划分和bag-of-words原始特征，将SBERT编码的GPT-4o-mini TAPE特征通过纯输入拼接引入。系统比较不同骨干网络（GCN/GCNII/GAT）、不同划分方式、不同编码器的影响。提出Delta_sig指标衡量LLM单独可判别性，通过9个数据集检验其与拼接成本的相关性。进行维度控制消融实验（PCA降维、高斯噪声对照），并在9种PubMed配置下拟合幂律关系。

Result: 纯输入拼接LLM特征导致PubMed测试准确率下降17.0±0.3个pp，Cora下降4.3±0.6个pp，CiteSeer变化不显著。使用GCN/GCNII/GAT骨干、随机划分、更小编码器时下降减弱。在中等同质性数据集WikiCS（+4.4pp）和ogbn-arxiv（+11.7pp）上拼接反而有帮助。Delta_sig与拼接成本的相关性（r²=0.38）强于同质性（r²=0.06）。Delta_sig≤13.8pp的规则能正确分类7/9个数据集。维度控制消融显示LLM特征下降介于同源PCA（-2.3pp）和同维高斯噪声（-37.3pp）之间。9种配置拟合幂律|Δ_concat|∝(√(d_l/n))^1.31，r²=0.97。

Conclusion: LLM特征通过纯输入拼接引入GNN时，并非总是有益；在同质性高、Delta_sig低的场景下可能显著损害性能。Delta_sig可作为预测拼接效果的有效解释性指标。拼接效果受骨干网络、数据划分、编码器选择和特征维度与样本量比值等多种因素影响，遵循可预测的幂律关系。研究者应谨慎选择LLM特征的引入方式，避免盲目使用简单拼接。

Abstract: Adding LLM-generated node features to graph neural networks (GNNs) is widely reported to improve accuracy on standard benchmarks. We document a contrasting observation: when LLM features are introduced through pure input concatenation (rather than joint training, distillation, or prompt-conditioning), they can systematically degrade accuracy on the same homophilous benchmarks where end-to-end LLM pipelines succeed. With an MLP backbone on the Planetoid public split and bag-of-words original features, concatenating SBERT-encoded GPT-4o-mini TAPE features reduces PubMed test accuracy by -17.0 +/- 0.3 pp and Cora by -4.3 +/- 0.6 pp (CiteSeer -0.6 +/- 0.8 pp, within seed noise). The drop attenuates as we relax each condition (GCN / GCNII / GAT backbones, random splits, smaller encoders) and reverses on medium-homophily WikiCS (+4.4 pp) and ogbn-arxiv (+11.7 pp). To predict when concatenation helps versus hurts, we report a simple measure of LLM-alone discriminability, Delta_sig. Across 9 datasets Delta_sig correlates with the concatenation cost more strongly than homophily at point estimate (r^2 = 0.38 vs. 0.06; N=9, bootstrap CIs overlap). The bootstrap-best change-point is tau = 13.8 pp, and the rule "Delta_sig <= tau predicts non-positive concat cost" classifies 7/9 datasets correctly; since 60% of bootstrap samples place tau in [5, 30] pp, we treat Delta_sig as an interpretive lens rather than a precision filter. A dimension-controlled ablation on PubMed places the LLM-feature drop between same-source PCA (-2.3 pp) and same-dim Gaussian noise (-37.3 pp), ruling out dimensionality and weight-decay artifacts. Nine PubMed configurations fit a power law |Delta_concat| proportional to (sqrt(d_l/n))^1.31 with r^2 = 0.97; the low-Delta_sig, small-n corner is exactly where the headline -17 pp PubMed deficit appears.

</details>


### [225] [Expanding SPHERE-JEPA: A Family of Statistical Regularizers for the Hypersphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17603)
*Léo Nicollier,Enric Meinhardt-Llopis,Max Dunitz,Marc Pic,Pablo Musé,Gabriele Facciolo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出用确定性最大均值差异（MMD）替代随机切片正则化器（如SIGReg/SUSReg）来防止自监督学习中的表示塌缩，通过旋转不变核在单位超球面上直接实现均匀分布约束，消除了蒙特卡洛采样引入的投影方差，从而稳定优化、加速收敛，并在ImageNet和Galaxy10上取得一致改进。同时揭示了不同统计检验（MMD、KSD、KL散度）对潜在空间几何结构的不同影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自监督学习框架（如LeJEPA、SPHERE-JEPA）使用基于蒙特卡洛采样的切片统计正则化器（SIGReg、SUSReg）来近似单位超球面上的均匀分布，这种随机性在训练梯度中注入投影方差，导致优化不稳定并阻碍收敛。

Method: 1) 理论证明切片正则化器的随机投影积分在解析上等价于确定性MMD；2) 在球面上直接构造全维MMD、KSD和KL散度目标函数，使用基于谱理论构建的旋转不变核（Heat核与Bandlimited核）防止空间偏差；3) 通过消除投影噪声实现更稳定的优化。

Result: 在ImageNet和Galaxy10上，消除投影噪声的确定性方法相比随机切片正则化器实现了更稳定的优化、更快的收敛和一致的性能提升。同时发现MMD/KSD倾向于局部聚类组织（适合目标中心域），而基于KDE的KL散度促进细粒度实例分离（在非聚类程序化纹理检索上表现最强）。

Conclusion: 解析积分消除随机投影方差可稳定SSL训练并提升性能；不同统计检验选择直接影响潜在空间的几何结构，应根据任务需求选择适当的正则化器。

Abstract: In Self-Supervised Learning (SSL), preventing representation collapse by explicitly enforcing a uniform distribution on the unit hypersphere has proven to be effective. However, current frameworks typically rely on sliced statistical regularizers such as SIGReg (used in LeJEPA) and SUSReg (used in SPHERE-JEPA), which approximate this continuous objective via Monte Carlo sampling along random 1D directions. This stochasticity injects projection variance into the training gradients, destabilizing optimization, and hindering convergence. In this work, we first show that analytically integrating out these random projections natively yields a deterministic Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD), bypassing the variance of sliced methods. Motivated by this equivalence, we formulate full-dimensional objectives for MMD, Kernel Stein Discrepancy (KSD), and Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence directly on the sphere to enforce a uniform distribution. To prevent spatial bias, we equip these tests with rotationally invariant kernels constructed via spectral theory, systematically evaluating two canonical families: smooth exponential decay (Heat) and strict frequency cutoff (Bandlimited) filters. Empirically, removing projection-induced noise results in more stable optimization, faster convergence, and consistent improvements over stochastic sliced regularizers on ImageNet and Galaxy10. Furthermore, we reveal that the choice of the statistical test shapes the geometry of the learned latent space: MMD and KSD favor locally clustered organization suitable for object-centric domains, whereas the continuous KDE-based KL divergence promotes fine-grained instance separation, yielding the strongest results on unclustered procedural texture retrieval.

</details>


### [226] [A Risk Decomposition Framework for Pre-Hoc Fine-Tuning Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17649)
*Yuxiang Luo,Chen Wang,Nan Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次揭示了LLM微调前性能预测的理论极限，将预测风险分解为静态数据-模型兼容性和可优化方差，证明了优化方差衰减率存在必要下界，并基于此提出预算最优探测原则和可预测性相图（将任务分为静态充足、动态临界和噪声主导三类），实验验证了理论框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调LLM的高昂成本构成了显著的经济障碍，而事前性能预测是降低这一成本的关键解决方案。然而，事前性能预测的理论极限尚未被探索，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 将事前性能预测建模为信息约束下的随机估计问题，将预测风险分解为内在极限（静态数据-模型兼容性）和可约减的优化方差两部分。证明优化方差衰减率存在必要下界，推导出预算最优探测原则，并引入可预测性相图（静态充足、动态临界、噪声主导三种任务类型）。

Result: 在合成和真实基准数据集上的大量实验验证了理论 regimes 的有效性，并证明了所提探测策略的效率。

Conclusion: 证明了无论使用何种预测器，不确定性消散的速度都受到基本约束，并据此提出了预算最优的探测策略和任务可预测性分类框架。

Abstract: The high cost of fine-tuning LLMs poses a significant economic barrier; pre-hoc performance prediction offers a critical solution to substantially reduce this expense. However, the theoretical limits of pre-hoc performance prediction remain unexplored. We formulate it as a stochastic estimation problem under information constraints, decomposing prediction risk into two components: an intrinsic limit (static data-model compatibility) and a reducible optimization variance. We prove that optimization variance admits a necessary lower bound on its decay rate, implying fundamental constraints on how quickly uncertainty dissipates, regardless of the predictor used. Based on these dynamics, we derive a budget-optimal probing principle and introduce a predictability phase diagram that organizes tasks into three distinct regimes: Static-Sufficient, Dynamic-Critical, and Noise-Dominant. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world benchmarks validate these theoretical regimes and demonstrate the efficiency of our probing strategy.

</details>


### [227] [Physics-Constrained Neural Networks for Improved Short-Term Weather Forecasting: A Case Study over the South Pacific](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17659)
*Egor Bugaev,Fedor Buzaev,Dmitry Efremenko,Denis Derkach,Fedor Ratnikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过对物理约束神经网络（PCNNs）的增强改进，提出三项创新（改进数值求解器、统一自回归混合块、集成两种先进神经骨干），在短时天气预报中相比纯神经网络方法降低8-22%的均方根误差，同时提升物理一致性和计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升物理约束神经网络（PCNNs）在短时混合天气预报模型中的精度和稳定性，解决原有模型在时间步长限制、过拟合特定预报时效等问题。

Method: 基于WeatherGFT架构，提出三项创新：(1) 改进数值求解器，结合WENO-5格式、beta平面近似和亚网格尺度粘性，将积分时间步长提升4倍至1200秒，日均MSE降低26%；(2) 统一自回归混合块替代24个专门模块，消除对特定预报时效的过拟合；(3) 物理核心集成两种先进神经骨干，形成PI-PredFormer和PI-IAM4VP混合模型。

Result: 在2000-2004年WeatherBench南太平洋子集上，混合模型在1-12小时预报时效内相比纯神经方法降低8-22%的RMSE，同时更好地保持物理一致性。

Conclusion: 混合模型各组件的增量式改进是提升短时天气预报准确性和效率的实用途径。

Abstract: This study introduces enhancements to physics-constrained neural networks (PCNNs) that improve the accuracy and stability of hybrid short-term weather forecasting models. Building on the WeatherGFT architecture, three innovations are proposed. First, an upgraded numerical solver, combining a fifth-order weighted essentially non-oscillatory scheme (WENO-5), a beta-plane approximation, and subgrid-scale viscosity, permits a fourfold increase in the integration time step to 1200 s while reducing the daily mean squared error by up to 26%. Second, a unified autoregressive hybrid block replaces the original chain of 24 specialised modules, eliminating overfitting to specific lead times. Third, the physical core is integrated with two state-of-the-art neural backbones, resulting in PI-PredFormer and PI-IAM4VP. Evaluation on the WeatherBench South Pacific subset from 2000 to 2004 shows that these hybrids reduce root mean squared error at 1-12 h lead times by 8-22% compared to purely neural counterparts, while better preserving physical consistency. These results demonstrate that incremental refinement of hybrid components offers a practical route toward more accurate and efficient short-range weather forecasting.

</details>


### [228] [TuneAhead: Predicting Fine-tuning Performance Before Full Training Begins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17660)
*Yuxiang Luo,Haonan Long,Chen Wang,Qiqi Duan,Xiaotian Lin,Yanwei Xu,Yuyu Luo,Weikai Yang,Nan Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TUNEAHEAD提出了一种轻量级框架，通过结合静态数据集描述符和短标准探测的动态探测特征构建元特征向量，在完全微调前预测大语言模型的微调性能。在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct上的1,300+次微调实验中，该框架以RMSE 1.47个百分点、95.1%预测落在真值±3%以内的精度优于Early-Stop Extrapolation和ProxyLM等基线方法，并支持实用的go/no-go筛选策略以减少不必要的全微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调大语言模型计算开销大且易出错，模型性能对数据质量和超参数选择敏感，盲目微调甚至可能降低性能。因此需要一种在提交全量训练前就能预测微调性能的方法，以避免资源浪费。

Method: TUNEAHEAD为每个候选微调运行编码一个元特征向量，该向量由静态数据集描述符和来自短标准探测过程的动态探测特征组成。然后使用一个预测器将这些特征映射到性能估计，并利用SHAP归因提供可解释的诊断，揭示哪些具体特征驱动了预测结果。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct上的1,300+次微调运行中，TUNEAHEAD持续优于Early-Stop Extrapolation和ProxyLM等强基线方法。在370次运行的留出测试集上，RMSE为1.47个百分点，95.1%的预测落在真值±3个百分点以内。

Conclusion: TUNEAHEAD的准确连续预测支持实用的go/no-go筛选策略，能够减少不必要的全微调同时保留最有希望的运行，为微调前的性能预判提供了可靠且可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: Fine-tuning large language models (LLMs) is compute-intensive and error-prone: model performance depends sensitively on data quality and hyperparameter choices, and naïve runs can even degrade model performance. This raises a practical question:can we predict fine-tuning performance before committing to a full training run? We present TUNEAHEAD, a lightweight framework for pre-hoc prediction of fine-tuning performance. TUNEAHEAD encodes each candidate run as a meta-feature vector that combines static dataset descriptors with dynamic probe features from a short standardized probe. A predictor maps these features to performance estimates, while SHAP-based attributions provide interpretable diagnostics that reveal which specific features drive the prediction. Across 1,300+ fine-tuning runs on Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, TUNEAHEAD consistently outperforms strong baselines such as Early-Stop Extrapolation and ProxyLM. On a held-out test set of 370 runs, TUNEAHEAD achieves an RMSE of 1.47 percentage points and places 95.1% of predictions within +3/-3 percentage points of the true score. These accurate continuous predictions support practical go/no-go screening policies that can reduce unnecessary full fine-tuning while retaining most promising runs.

</details>


### [229] [Handling Feature Heterogeneity with Learnable Graph Patches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17667)
*Yifei Sun,Yang Yang,Xiao Feng,Zijun Wang,Haoyang Zhong,Chunping Wang,Lei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出可学习图补丁（Learnable Graph Patches）概念，将图数据分解为最小的语义单元，并通过补丁编码器和聚合器构建领域无关的通用图预训练模型，解决了无文本信息图数据的特征异构性问题，实现了跨域迁移和性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图基础模型难以处理无文本信息的图数据中的特征异构性，导致模型在不同数据集间缺乏可迁移性，亟需一种领域无关的通用图预训练方法。

Method: 提出可学习图补丁作为图数据的最小语义单元，通过展开节点特征并构建对应补丁结构来分解图；设计补丁编码器提取每个单元的知识，补丁聚合器学习单元组合方式，构建领域无关的预训练框架。

Result: 该方法成功实现多领域图数据联合预训练，在各种下游数据集和任务上表现增强，且预训练数据量增加时下游性能持续提升。

Conclusion: 可学习图补丁范式有效解决了图数据的特征异构性问题，为构建真正的图基础模型提供了可行路径，具有良好的跨域迁移能力和可扩展性。

Abstract: In recent years, the rapid development of foundation models and graph pre-training technologies has spurred increasing interest in constructing a universal pre-trained graph model or Graph Foundation Model (GFM). However, a significant challenge is that existing models are unable to address feature heterogeneity in graph data without textual information, which hinders the transferability of graph models across different datasets. To bridge this gap, we propose the concept of learnable graph patches, which we regard as the smallest semantic units of any graph data. We decompose the graph into learnable graph patches by unfolding the node features and constructing corresponding patch structures separately. We then design a framework that mines transferable information from graph data across domains. Specifically, after extracting graph patches, we propose a patch encoder to extract knowledge from each unit and a patch aggregator to learn how the units are combined into a whole. Due to its domain-agnostic nature, the model can be applied to downstream data across different domains. Furthermore, we analyze the connection between our method and existing graph models, as well as the transferability of the node embeddings it generates. Empirically, our method not only achieves the capability to use multi-domain graphs for pre-training, but also shows enhanced performance across various downstream datasets and tasks. Moreover, we observe consistent improvement in downstream performance as the volume of pre-training data increases.

</details>


### [230] [ASTEROID: A Spatiotemporal Information Transformer for Forecasting Multi-Step Time Series of Molecular Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17668)
*Kexin Wu,Luonan Chen,Renxiao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了ASTEROID框架，一种基于Transformer架构的数据驱动方法，通过将MD轨迹建模为高维时空序列并集成时空信息变换方程，实现了多步原子坐标直接预测，在多个量子力学分子数据集上取得了比现有方法更高的预测精度，同时显著降低了传统MD模拟的计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子动力学模拟计算成本高昂，尤其对于需要长期分析的大规模系统；准确预测MD模拟结果既具有科学挑战性，也具有重要的实际应用价值。

Method: 将MD轨迹重构为高维时空序列，集成时空信息（STI）变换方程到Transformer架构中。空间依赖上采用局部-全局自注意力机制捕捉短程和长程相互作用；时间依赖上采用编码器-解码器结构整合全局上下文与自回归预测。

Result: 在多个量子力学分子数据集上，ASTEROID在多步预测精度上超越现有方法，并显著降低了传统MD模拟的计算成本；支持在扩展时间尺度上的迭代多步预测。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一种鲁棒且可泛化的数据驱动范式，用于加速分子动力学模拟。

Abstract: Molecular dynamics (MD) simulation is computationally demanding, particularly for large-scale systems requiring long-term analysis. Accurate forecast of the outcomes of a MD simulation is not only an attractive scientific challenge but also has substantial practical value. In this work, we developed a data-driven framework, termed ASTEROID (Advanced Spatiotemporal TransformER fOr Inferring Dynamics), that can directly predict multi-step atomic coordinates, avoiding conventional iterative integration. For this purpose, our ASTEROID reformulates MD trajectories as high-dimensional spatiotemporal sequences and integrates the Spatiotemporal Information (STI) Transformation equation into a Transformer architecture. The core innovation of ASTEROID lies in its ability to model multiscale spatiotemporal dependencies. In particular, for spatial dependencies, a local-global self-attention mechanism captures both short- and long-range interactions. For temporal dependencies, an encoder-decoder structure integrates global context with autoregressive forecasting. ASTEROID was evaluated on several quantum-mechanics derived molecular datasets. Our results indicate that ASTEROID achieved not only a higher level of accuracy in multi-step prediction than existing methods on various benchmarks, but also significantly reduced computational cost of conventional MD simulation. Moreover, the model supports iterative multi-step forecasting over an extended time scale. This work establishes a robust and generalizable data-driven paradigm for accelerating MD simulations.

</details>


### [231] [EnvRL: Learn from Environment Dynamics in Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17680)
*Zhitong Wang,Songze Li,Hao Peng,Shuzheng Si,Yi Wang,Maosong Sun,Juanzi Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EnvRL框架，通过在强化学习中引入状态预测和逆动力学两个辅助目标，让LLM Agent从交互轨迹中学习环境动态，显著提升在ALFWorld和WebShop等长周期任务上的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习方法在长周期Agent任务中仅使用稀疏的结果奖励，忽略了交互轨迹中蕴含的丰富环境动态信息。本文认为交互经验本身可作为隐式监督信号，揭示环境转移机制，帮助Agent构建更准确的内部环境模型。

Method: 提出EnvRL框架，在标准RL目标基础上增加两个辅助学习目标：状态预测（预测下一状态）和逆动力学（根据状态变化推断动作），使Agent从自身交互经验中内化环境动态，实现联合优化。

Result: 在ALFWorld上，Qwen-2.5-1.5B-Instruct+GRPO的成功率从72.8%提升至77.4%；在WebShop上从56.8%提升至67.0%。

Conclusion: 通过引入环境动态学习辅助目标，能够有效提升LLM Agent在长周期任务中的策略学习效果，验证了利用交互轨迹中隐式环境信息的有效性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for training Large Language Models (LLMs) as agents. However, conventional RL methods for long-horizon agentic tasks often struggle with sparse outcome rewards. Intuitively, this overlooks the rich environment dynamics information contained in rollout interaction trajectories. We argue that the interaction experience inherently serves as an implicit supervision signal, reveals the underlying transition mechanisms of the environment, and enables the agent to construct a more accurate internal model of the environment.. Therefore, in this work, we investigate how to leverage this additional signal to improve policy learning. Specifically, we propose EnvRL, a framework that incorporates environment dynamics learning into agentic RL via two auxiliary objectives: state prediction and inverse dynamics. By jointly optimizing with the primary RL objective, we encourage the agent to internalize environment dynamics from its own interaction experience. Extensive experiments on two long-horizon agentic benchmarks demonstrate that EnvRL achieves significant improvements on success-rates over RL-only baselines, e.g., when trained with GRPO, lifting Qwen-2.5-1.5B-Instruct from 72.8% to 77.4% on ALFWorld, and from 56.8% to 67.0% on WebShop.

</details>


### [232] [Delta-Based Target Reformulation for Short-Term Electricity Load Forecasting Using LSTM and Transformer Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17692)
*Vansh Bansal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于差分（delta）的目标重构方法用于短期电力负荷预测，将直接预测绝对负荷值改为预测相邻时间步的负荷变化量。实验表明该方法在小时级预测中使所有模型的MAPE降低超过50%，在日前预测中则对LSTM和Transformer等深度序列模型有效，但对LightGBM效果有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习模型（如LSTM、Transformer）在短期负荷预测中表现良好，但大多数研究直接预测绝对负荷值，未明确处理目标变量的非平稳性。受ARIMA模型中经典时间序列差分技术的启发，本文旨在通过差分目标重构来稳定学习目标、降低预测难度。

Method: 提出基于差分（delta）的目标重构方法：模型预测相邻时间步之间的负荷变化量，最终预测通过加上最后一个观测到的实际负荷值来重构。使用印度多年逐小时真实电力负荷数据，结合NASA POWER气象变量和日历特征，评估LSTM、Transformer和LightGBM在绝对预测和差分预测两种设定下的表现，实验包括小时级和日前级两个预测时间范围。

Result: 差分目标重构在小时级预测中一致提升所有模型的预测精度，MAPE降低超过50%；在日前预测中，差分目标仅对深度序列模型（LSTM和Transformer）有益，LightGBM在绝对预测设定下仍具竞争力。

Conclusion: 差分目标重构是一种有效的归纳偏置，但其有效性依赖于模型类型和预测时间范围。对于神经网络模型，差分重构能显著提升预测性能；对于树模型如LightGBM，效果则因预测范围而异。

Abstract: Accurate short-term electricity load forecasting is critical for the reliable and economic operation of modern power systems, under non-stationarity arising from weather variability, calendar effects, and evolving consumption patterns. While deep learning models such as LSTMs and Transformers show promising performance, most existing studies focus on direct absolute load prediction without explicitly addressing target non-stationarity.
  Motivated by classical time-series differencing techniques in ARIMA models, this paper investigates a delta-based target reformulation for short-term electricity load forecasting using deep learning. Instead of directly predicting absolute load values, the proposed formulation trains models to predict the change in load between consecutive time steps, with final forecasts reconstructed using the last observed load. This aims to stabilize the learning target and reduce forecasting difficulty.
  Using multi-year, hourly real-world electricity load data from India, augmented with meteorological variables from the NASA POWER project and calendar features, this study evaluates LSTM and Transformer models under both formulations, benchmarking them against LightGBM. Experiments are conducted for hour-ahead and day-ahead horizons, assessing performance via Mean Absolute Error (MAE) and Mean Absolute Percentage Error (MAPE).
  Results show that delta-based reformulation consistently improves forecasting accuracy for hour-ahead prediction across all evaluated models, yielding MAPE reductions of over 50% compared to absolute formulations. For day-ahead forecasting, delta targets specifically benefit deep sequence models (LSTM and Transformer), while LightGBM remains competitive under the absolute formulation. These findings indicate that while delta reformulation is a powerful inductive bias for neural networks, its efficacy is model- and horizon-dependent.

</details>


### [233] [Confusion-Aware Transfer Teacher Curriculum Learning Framework: Disentangling Scoring and Pacing Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17706)
*Savini Kommalage,Sanka Mohottala,Asiri Gawesha,Dulara Madhusanka,Menan Velayuthan,Dharshana Kasthurirathna,Mahima Milinda Alwis Weerasinghe,Charith Abhayaratne*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文通过解耦课程学习中的难度评分与节奏调度两个因素，提出两种评估协议，并在Transfer Teacher框架下验证了混淆感知难度评分的效果。实验表明，在完整数据上课程学习无显著提升，但在低数据场景（20%）下，混淆感知的课程排序相比随机排序提升高达8.7个百分点，展示了其作为数据高效训练方法的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 课程学习将样本难度评分与训练节奏两个设计选择耦合在一起，使得难以归因性能提升来源于哪个组件。论文旨在解耦这两个因素，分别评估评分函数与调度策略的独立贡献。

Method: 提出了两种评估协议：(1) 阶段式测试子集，用于独立于课程训练验证评分函数；(2) 基线方法，对随机顺序数据应用相同的调度策略。在Transfer Teacher框架下，提出了一种混淆感知的难度评分，同时考虑正确类别的置信度与错误类别的概率分布。

Result: 在CIFAR-10数据集上，ResNet-18和VGG-16的实验中，混淆感知评分生成的难度排序与人类直觉一致。在完整数据上，课程学习和反课程学习均未优于标准训练。但在20%数据量下，混淆感知课程排序比随机排序提升8.7个百分点。

Conclusion: 仅改进评分函数不足以克服Transfer Teacher框架中课程学习的已知失败模式。但混淆感知的课程排序在低数据场景下表现出显著的数据效率优势，表明TTF有潜力成为数据高效的训练方法。

Abstract: Curriculum learning couples two design choices, how samples are scored by difficulty and how harder samples are paced into training, making it difficult to attribute observed gains to either component. We disentangle these factors with two evaluation protocols: stage-wise test subsets that validate scoring functions independently of curriculum training, and a baseline that applies the same pacing schedule to randomly ordered data. Within the Transfer Teacher framework (TTF), we use these protocols to evaluate a confusion-aware difficulty score that considers both correct-class confidence and the probability distribution over incorrect classes. On CIFAR-10 with ResNet-18 and VGG-16, the proposed score produces model-interpretable difficulty rankings that align with human intuition. However, at full data, neither curriculum nor anti-curriculum ordering improves accuracy over standard training, indicating that improving the scoring function alone is insufficient to overcome the known failure modes of curriculum learning in TTF. In contrast, We find that confusion-aware curriculum ordering result in consistent data-efficiency benefits, outperforming random ordering by up to 8.7% points at the 20% data regime, suggesting the potential of TTF as a data-efficient training method.

</details>


### [234] [A fairness-aware extension of Stochastic Multicriteria Acceptability Analysis for ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17756)
*Guilherme Dean Pelegrina,Renata Pelissari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SMAA-Fair，一种在随机多准则可接受性分析中显式纳入群体公平性的扩展方法，通过重排加权使公平排序贡献更大，实验证明该方法在不损失对偏好不确定性鲁棒性的前提下提升了受保护群体在有利位置的表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SMAA方法能处理不确定性和不完整偏好信息，但未明确考虑排序结果中群体公平性问题。

Method: 提出SMAA-Fair框架，对SMAA生成的模拟排序根据群体公平性水平进行重新加权，使更公平的排序对可接受性指标和中心权重向量的贡献更大。采用统计均等、rKL和nDKL作为公平性指标，通过期望排序和最大可接受性排序从公平性调整后的可接受性矩阵导出最终排序。

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据上的数值实验表明，SMAA-Fair能够提升受保护群体在有利排序位置中的代表性，同时保持对偏好不确定性的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SMAA-Fair为排序问题提供了一种与聚合模型无关的公平性增强方法，可集成不同的公平性度量，在提升公平性的同时保留了SMAA原有的鲁棒性优势。

Abstract: Fairness has become a central concern in ranking problems involving individuals or social groups, particularly under the Responsible Artificial Intelligence agenda. In Multi-Criteria Decision Analysis, Stochastic Multicriteria Acceptability Analysis (SMAA) provides a robust framework for handling uncertainty and incomplete preference information, but it does not explicitly address fairness in the resulting rankings. This paper proposes SMAA-Fair, a fairness-aware extension of SMAA for ranking problems. The approach reweights the simulated rankings generated by SMAA according to their level of group fairness, so that fairer rankings contribute more strongly to the acceptability indices and central weights vector. The framework is independent of the aggregation model and can incorporate different fairness metrics. In this study, Statistical Parity, normalized discounted Kullback--Leibler divergence (rKL) and normalized discounted cumulative Kullback--Leibler divergence (nDKL) are adopted. Rankings are derived from the fairness-adjusted acceptability matrix using expected ranking and maximum acceptability ranking. We also derive the central weight according to the degree of fairness in the obtained rankings. Numerical experiments with synthetic and real data show that SMAA-Fair improves the representation of protected groups among favourable ranking positions, while preserving robustness to preference uncertainty.

</details>


### [235] [Blind Recovery of Latent Domains via Unsupervised Symmetry Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17782)
*Onur Efe,Arkadas Ozakin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无监督框架，通过发现数据分布的对称性来恢复潜在域和信号，适用于观测为一般线性变换（非卷积）的盲逆问题。该方法将观测建模为潜在随机场信号的线性测量，通过优化浅层群卷积网络并施加平稳性和局部性正则化，学习潜在对称性动作和滤波器，从而将非结构化观测映射为基于对称性的表征。实验证明该方法能有效恢复潜在域和信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统盲反卷积仅适用于卷积型退化，无法处理一般线性变换（如损坏域结构）的盲逆问题。本文旨在提出一种普适性更强的无监督框架，不依赖特定的退化模型先验，而是通过数据自身的对称性结构来发现潜在域和信号。

Method: 将观测建模为潜在随机场信号的线性测量；优化一个浅层群卷积网络；施加平稳性（stationarity）和局部性（locality）正则化；网络学习潜在对称性动作（symmetry action）和滤波器，将非结构化观测映射到基于对称性的表征。

Result: 在随机过程、Ising模型、打乱/比特加扰图像以及神经记录等多个任务上，该方法成功恢复了潜在域和信号，证明了对称性发现作为无监督结构学习和盲逆问题新方向的有效性。

Conclusion: 数据分布中的对称性发现为盲逆问题提供了一条新路径，能够在不依赖具体退化先验的情况下恢复潜在的域结构和信号，具有广阔的推广价值。

Abstract: Primary motivation in blind inverse problems is to recover signals of interest from corrupted observations without knowing the obfuscating mechanism. Blind deconvolution is a prominent approach when the corruption is convolutional, but it is not applicable when general linear transformations obfuscate the domain structure. In this work, we propose an unsupervised framework for recovering latent domains and signals by discovering symmetries of the data distribution. Our framework models observations as linear measurements of signals sampled from a latent random field, and optimizes a shallow group-convolutional network by imposing stationarity and locality regularization at the model output. The model learns a latent symmetry action and an appropriate filter, thereby mapping unstructured observations to a symmetry-based representation that reveals latent signals. Experiments on stochastic processes, Ising models, shuffled and bit-scrambled images, and neural recordings show that the method recovers latent domains and signals from unstructured observations, suggesting symmetry discovery as a new direction for unsupervised structure learning and blind inverse problems.

</details>


### [236] [Continual Self-Improvement with Lightweight Experiential Latent Memories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17803)
*Vaggelis Dorovatas,Nancy Kalaj,Rahaf Aljundi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在线学习方法，将大语言模型在测试时产生的推理轨迹（reasoning traces）通过无监督强化学习（多数投票作为奖励信号）蒸馏为轻量级软提示记忆（soft prompt memories），使模型能够从自身经验中持续学习，而无需外部监督或离线训练。该方法仅需极少量参数（约模型参数的0.001%）和少量梯度步数，即可在数学推理任务上超越零样本和原始轨迹ICL基线，甚至媲美全参数更新和离线训练的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在推理时会生成丰富的推理轨迹，但这些轨迹在推理结束后被丢弃，模型无法从中学习并积累知识。现有方法（如直接对原始轨迹进行ICL或自反思）因缺乏抽象表示而难以泛化。作者希望将测试时的瞬时计算转化为持久可复用的知识，实现无外部监督的在线持续学习。

Method: 1) 揭示原始推理轨迹直接用于ICL的局限性：token级别的复用缺乏抽象性，无法有效迁移；2) 受无监督强化学习启发，提出使用自生成测试时信号（多数投票）作为奖励，对每个实例进行轻量级训练；3) 设计在线蒸馏方法，将推理计算转化为紧凑的模块化潜在记忆（软提示），存储底层推理结构；4) 通过模块化设计避免灾难性遗忘，支持检索和持续改进。

Result: 在具有挑战性的数学推理基准上，该方法显著优于零样本和原始轨迹ICL基线；跨数据集迁移有效；性能与全参数更新和离线训练相竞争；参数效率极高（仅0.001%模型参数）。

Conclusion: 从原始推理轨迹到学习到的潜在表示的转变是关键，建议未来的在线学习应聚焦于从推理计算中蒸馏抽象知识，而非直接复用token序列。

Abstract: Large language models achieve strong reasoning performance by scaling inference-time compute, yet remain fundamentally stateless, discarding the rich, self-produced reasoning traces generated during this process. We investigate whether models can instead learn online from this experience, converting transient computation (reasoning traces) into persistent reusable knowledge, and without external supervision or access to future data. We show that In-Context Learning (ICL) over raw reasoning traces fails to generalize, reflecting a fundamental limitation of token-level reuse: individual traces lack the abstraction needed for transfer, even after refinement (e.g. self-reflection). In contrast, drawing inspiration from recent works on unsupervised reinforcement learning, we find that lightweight per-instance training with self-generated test-time signals (majority voting) as rewards yields substantial gains, often surpassing full-dataset offline training, motivating a shift from raw traces to learned latent representations. Building on this insight, we propose an online method that distills inference-time compute spent on encountered problems into compact modular latent memories capturing the underlying reasoning structure. These memories are stored and retrieved for future inputs, enabling continual improvement while avoiding catastrophic forgetting through modular design. Importantly, our method is highly efficient, parametrized as extremely lightweight soft prompt memories (~0.001% of model parameters) and trained with only a few gradient steps, yet achieving performance competitive with full parametric updates and offline training. Across challenging mathematical reasoning benchmarks, our approach significantly outperforms zero-shot and raw data ICL baselines, while transferring effectively across datasets.

</details>


### [237] [QueryMarket: Cost-Aware Online Active Learning in Data Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17805)
*Xiwen Huang,Pierre Pinson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QueryMarket是一个受市场启发的在线主动学习框架，其中OVBAL通过D-最优性准则和指数遗忘估计样本边际效用，在滚动预算约束下实现成本感知的标签购买，适应非平稳流数据和异构标签成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时数据流中的标签获取是主要瓶颈，现有在线主动学习很少统一考虑定价、信息增益和滚动预算约束，尤其是在概念漂移场景下。

Method: 提出QueryMarket框架和OVBAL算法：利用D-最优性准则结合指数遗忘机制估计每个样本的边际效用，在滚动预算约束下执行成本感知的标签购买决策。

Result: 在合成数据和真实太阳能发电预测任务中，OVBAL在卖方定价方案下特别有效，在两种定价方案下均展现出更优的长期误差-成本权衡。

Conclusion: OVBAL提供了一个简单、完全在线的决策规则，能够有效适应非平稳流和异构标签成本，在实际任务中展现出更优的长周期性价比。

Abstract: Data acquisition is a major bottleneck for learning in real-time streams: analysts must decide on the fly which labels to purchase while respecting a rolling budget. However, existing online active learning rarely unifies pricing, information gain, and rolling budget constraints under concept drift. We introduce QueryMarket, a market-inspired framework that queries each incoming data point based on its estimated utility to the model and its price. Within this framework, we propose OVBAL (online variance-based active learning), which integrates data pricing with information-driven selection by estimating each sample's marginal utility via a D-optimality criterion with exponential forgetting and executing cost-aware purchases under rolling budget constraints. OVBAL yields a simple, fully online decision rule that adapts to nonstationary streams and heterogeneous label costs. Experiments on synthetic data and a real-world solar power generation forecasting task show that OVBAL is particularly effective under seller-centric pricing and yields a more favorable long-run error-cost trade-off in the real-world task under both pricing schemes.

</details>


### [238] [No-Free-Fairness: Fundamental Limits and Trade-offs in Learning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17810)
*Khoat Than*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"无免费公平"定理，证明学习系统中的不公平现象源于任务固有成本、有限样本学习和模型表达力不足三个根本性来源，而非仅因数据偏差或优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示不公平性并非仅由数据偏差或次优优化导致，而是决策问题内在结构、有限数据约束和模型表达能力的固有产物，从而正确定位公平性问题的根源。

Method: 通过理论推导建立三组不可能性定理：(1)子群不可约成本下的公平-成本权衡前沿；(2)有限样本学习诱导的非平凡子群差异及严格相对公平的统计瓶颈；(3)模型类表达能力不足导致的不公平。

Result: 识别出三个独立的公平性不可能来源：任务固有成本与性能的权衡、理想条件下有限样本导致的统计瓶颈（指数级样本需求）、模型类表达力限制。结论适用于超越标准监督学习的广泛框架。

Conclusion: 公平性需要明确的权衡取舍，应作为核心设计考量而非事后修正。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish a set of theoretical impossibility results, termed the No-Free-Fairness theorems, that identify three fundamental sources of disparity in learning systems. First, we show that when a task exhibits irreducible cost on a subgroup, any decision rule must trade off overall performance with disparity, yielding an inherent fairness--cost frontier. Second, we prove that even in ideal, noise-free settings where a perfectly fair and accurate solution exists, finite-sample learning alone induces nontrivial subgroup disparity, ruling out distribution-free fairness guarantees. More seriously, enforcing strict relative fairness creates a statistical bottleneck: achieving low cost may require exponentially many samples. Third, we show that limitations of the model class can independently induce disparity: if the model cannot represent accurate solutions for a subgroup, fairness remains unattainable regardless of data or training procedure. Overall, these results demonstrate that unfairness is not solely a consequence of biased data or suboptimal optimization, but arises from the intrinsic structure of decision problems, the constraints of finite data, and the expressivity of models. Our framework applies broadly beyond standard supervised learning, and suggests that achieving fairness requires explicit trade-offs and should be treated as a core design consideration.

</details>


### [239] [Conservation Laws for Modern Neural Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17816)
*Viet-Hoang Tran,Vinh Khanh Bui,Tan Lai Ngoc,Nam Nguyen,Tuan Dam,Tan M. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对现代深度学习架构（GELU/SiLU/SwiGLU激活函数、多头注意力机制、混合专家模型等）中的梯度下降守恒律问题，提出了统一的理论框架，并通过实验验证了理论预测的不变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过参数化模型的隐式偏差通过梯度流的守恒律体现，但现有的守恒律理论主要针对线性网络和ReLU网络，对现代架构（GELU、SiLU、SwiGLU激活函数、多头注意力、位置编码、混合专家模型等）的守恒律鲜有研究。

Method: 发展了一个统一的理论框架，用于刻画前馈网络（GELU/SiLU/SwiGLU）、多头注意力机制（正弦/旋转位置编码）以及混合专家模型（多种门控设计）中梯度下降的守恒律。

Result: 在理论上推导了上述现代架构的守恒律，并通过实验验证了所预测的不变量与实际训练过程一致。

Conclusion: 该统一框架成功将守恒律分析从线性/ReLU网络推广到多种现代深度学习架构，有助于理解过参数化模型的隐式偏差与泛化能力。

Abstract: Understanding gradient descent dynamics is key to explaining the success of over-parameterized models, where implicit bias manifests through conservation laws in gradient flow. While such laws are well understood for linear and ReLU networks, they remain largely unexplored for modern architectures. This work develops a unified framework to characterize conservation laws for contemporary models, including feedforward networks with GELU, SiLU, and SwiGLU activations, multihead attention with sinusoidal and rotary positional encodings, and Mixture-of-Experts architectures under diverse gating designs. Our theoretical findings are supported by experiments that validate the predicted invariants.

</details>


### [240] [Functional Equivalence in Attention: A Comprehensive Study with Applications to Linear Mode Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17830)
*Viet-Hoang Tran,Vinh Khanh Bui,Van-Hoan Trinh,Tan Lai Ngoc,Tan M. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文形式化研究了Transformer中位置编码对功能等价性的影响，发现正弦位置编码保留了原始注意力机制的对称结构，而旋转位置编码（RoPE）显著缩小了对称群从而增强了表达力，并通过线性模式连通性实验验证了位置编码的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力机制架构（尤其是Transformer）中参数空间存在非单射性，不同参数配置可实现相同功能，但现有研究多聚焦于经典全连接/卷积网络和原始多头注意力，忽视了位置编码对架构对称性的根本性重塑，因此需要系统研究位置编码如何影响功能等价性。

Method: 对正弦位置编码和旋转位置编码（RoPE）两种最广泛使用的变体进行形式化分析，比较它们对注意力机制对称群的影响；并利用线性模式连通性（linear mode connectivity）和参数对齐算法进行实证验证。

Result: 正弦位置编码保留了原始注意力等价结构的对称性，而RoPE显著减小了对称群，从而提升表达力；不同位置编码下Transformer的线性模式连通性存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 位置编码是影响Transformer对称性和表达力的关键因素，RoPE因更强的约束能力在实践中表现更优，该发现为RoPE的广泛应用提供了理论解释。

Abstract: Neural network parameter spaces are inherently non-injective, as distinct parameter configurations can realize identical functions through functional equivalence. While this symmetry is well understood in classical fully connected and convolutional models, it becomes substantially more intricate in modern attention-based architectures. Existing analyses of multihead attention have largely focused on the vanilla formulation, overlooking positional encodings that fundamentally reshape architectural symmetries. In this work, we provide a formal study of functional equivalence in Transformers with positional encodings. Focusing on the two most widely used variants--sinusoidal and rotary positional encodings (RoPE)--we show that sinusoidal encodings preserve the equivalence structure of vanilla attention, whereas rotary encodings significantly reduce the symmetry group, thereby enhancing expressivity. This offers a principled explanation for the growing prominence of RoPE in practice. We further examine how positional encodings affect linear mode connectivity, and through an alignment algorithm, empirically demonstrate that the presence and variability of connectivity across Transformer settings crucially depend on the positional encoding.

</details>


### [241] [From Drift to Coherence: Stabilizing Beliefs in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17832)
*SongEun Kim,Seungyoo Lee,Edwin Fong,Hyungi Lee,Juho Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现LLM在多项选择题回答中存在早期信念漂移（违反鞅性质），但自回归重采样后信念会自稳定并收敛到一致预测分布，并据此提出种子答案提示策略和自一致性损失两种方法来加速稳定、减少漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究在受控的上下文学习设置中发现LLM的预测信念违反了鞅性质这一关键一致性条件，而本文希望在更典型的通用多项选择题回答场景中重新审视这一问题。

Method: 提出提示式预测重采样（PPR），让LLM对同一问题生成多个答案序列以研究信念动态；基于观察提出种子答案提示策略和自一致性损失微调方法。

Result: PPR揭示了早期信念漂移（鞅性质违反），但经过多次重采样后信念自稳定并收敛；所提方法能显著减少信念漂移并提高预测一致性，且不牺牲准确率。

Conclusion: LLM在多项选择题回答中虽存在早期鞅性质违反，但通过重采样可实现信念自稳定；提出的两种方法能有效改善预测一致性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are often hypothesized to perform implicit Bayesian inference, yet a key coherence condition, the martingale property of predictive beliefs, has been shown to fail in controlled synthetic in-context learning settings. We revisit this question in a more typical usage regime: generic multiple-choice question answering. Exploiting the discrete answer space, we compute exact predictive distributions and study belief dynamics induced by autoregressive answer resampling. We introduce prompted predictive resampling (PPR), where an LLM generates a sequence of answers to the same question. Empirically, PPR reveals early-stage belief drift, indicating martingale violations. However, after sufficient resampling steps, the belief process self-stabilizes and converges to a coherent predictive distribution. Based on this observation, we further propose (i) a seed-answer prompting strategy to accelerate stabilization, and (ii) a self-consistency loss that amortizes early-stage drift into the model via fine-tuning. Experiments on multiple-choice QA benchmarks show that our methods substantially reduce belief drift and improve predictive coherence without sacrificing accuracy.

</details>


### [242] [Meta-classification of one-class classification models using ranking correlation and nearest neighbor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17858)
*Toshitaka Hayashi,Hamido Fujita,Dalibor Cimr,Richard Cimler,Jitka Kühnová*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种对一类分类（OCC）模型进行元分类的方法，将OCC模型表示为正常性排序，利用最近邻和排序相关性度量进行分类。实验表明该方法能高精度按数据集分类OCC模型，并能按算法分类。本质上，OCC模型分类等同于将多个样本作为单一输入的数据集分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将机器学习技术应用于ML模型本身是一个未被探索的方向，本文旨在填补这一空白，通过对一类分类（OCC）模型进行元分类来探索这一可能性。

Method: 将OCC模型表示为正常性排序（normality rankings），使用最近邻和排序相关性度量（ranking-correlation metrics）作为分类手段。由于所有ML模型可近似为OCC模型，该方法具有通用性。

Result: 当类别标签对应数据集时，该方法取得了高分类精度；当训练数据集包含相同类别时，也能对算法进行分类。此外，实验使用睡眠记录数据展示了数据集分类的效果。

Conclusion: OCC模型的分类本质上等同于将多个样本视为单一输入的数据集分类。该方法为OCC模型、数据集和排序的分类提供了统一解决方案。

Abstract: Machine Learning (ML) techniques have been applied to various problems. However, applying ML to ML models is an unexplored direction. For this purpose, this paper considers a meta-classification of one-class classification (OCC) models, because all ML models could be approximated as OCC models. The proposal represents OCC models as normality rankings and classifies them using nearest-neighbor and ranking-correlation metrics. The experiment classifies OCC models, where classes correspond to training datasets, algorithms, and hyperparameters. The proposal achieves high accuracy when class labels are datasets. Moreover, it can classify algorithms when the training datasets contain the same class. In addition, the discussion highlights that the classification of OCC models is essentially the classification of datasets that treats multiple samples as a single input. The experiment demonstrates the classification of datasets using sleeping records. The proposed method can provide a unified solution for classifying OCC models, datasets, and rankings. Source code is uploaded to the public repository https://github.com/ToshiHayashi/ClassOCC.

</details>


### [243] [AnchorKV: Safety-Aware KV Cache Compression via Soft Penalty with a Refusal Anchor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17872)
*Ning Ni,Yingjie Lao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AnchorKV是一种针对KV缓存压缩的即插即用修改方法，通过在键空间中对与有害提示相关的方向进行惩罚性偏置，在保持较小效用损失的同时显著提升安全性对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存压缩方法在良性负载上表现良好，但无法防御越狱攻击或在激进驱逐策略下降低安全对齐。需要一种既能保持压缩效率又能增强安全性的方法。

Method: 构建离线安全锚点（safety anchor），采用表示工程中的均值差异方法适配到KV缓存中逐层键投影空间；基于该锚点设计软惩罚令牌选择规则，在保留原始压缩策略的同时对有害方向进行偏置。

Result: 以少量效用损失为代价，显著提升安全性对齐；当惩罚系数为零时退化为原始压缩器。

Conclusion: AnchorKV有效弥合了KV缓存压缩效率与LLM安全对齐之间的鸿沟，是一种轻量级且实用的改进方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) outperform earlier architectures on generative inference and long-context tasks, but their large size introduces significant challenges in memory usage, energy cost, and on-device deployment. Since scaling pre-trained language models improves downstream capability \cite{zhao2023survey}, the key-value (KV) cache becomes a dominant inference bottleneck. Recent KV cache compression methods \cite{jo2025fastkv,li2024snapkv,zhou2024dynamickv} reduce this cost by retaining only a subset of attention-relevant tokens. However, while these approaches preserve accuracy on benign workloads, their compression policies either fail to defend against jailbreak attacks \cite{jiang2024robustkv} or degrade safety alignment under aggressive eviction.
  We propose AnchorKV, a drop-in modification to KV cache compression that biases token retention scores away from directions in key space associated with harmful prompts. AnchorKV constructs an offline safety anchor by adapting a difference-of-means representation engineering approach \cite{arditi2024refusal,zou2023representation} to the layer-specific key projection space used in KV caching. Based on this anchor, a soft penalty token selection rule trades a small amount of utility for substantially improved safety alignment, while reducing to the original compressor when the penalty is zero.

</details>


### [244] [Monotonic Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks: A Theoretical and Empirical Study of Monotonicity as an Inductive Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17886)
*Mikhail Krasnov,Carolina Fortuna,Blaž Bertalanič*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MKAN，一种通过指数重参数化B样条系数、正边权值和单调基激活函数实现硬单调性保证的KAN变体，所有参数均满足单调性约束且训练无需投影；理论上证明了表示代价定理：任何产生球状语义邻域划分的C^K特征提取器，在非单调坐标数为k时，至多需要2N^*个节点即可实现等价单调表示；实验表明MKAN在SMM/ICML-2024基准上优于现有方法，且是唯一同时具备硬单调性和KAN逐边功能透明性的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单调神经网络多为MLP或流模型，缺乏逐边功能透明度；MonoKAN虽引入单调性但仅约束部分参数且需投影训练，存在局限。作者旨在设计一种兼具硬单调性保证、无条件约束、可训练性与KAN可解释性优势的网络结构。

Method: 1) 通过指数重参数化B样条系数确保单调性；2) 采用正边权值约束；3) 使用单调基激活函数；4) 推导表示代价定理，给出单调编码器的理论尺寸规则N'=N^*+k≤2N^*；5) 标准无约束梯度下降训练。

Result: 1) 在SMM/ICML-2024基准上与SOTA单调NN持平；2) 唯一结合硬无条件单调性与KAN逐边功能透明性的方法；3) 2N^*理论预测在四个真实数据集的自监督特征尺寸搜索中得到验证；4) 在合成单调生成数据集上，MKAN的Spearman对齐显著优于KAN、MLP和线性基线。

Conclusion: MKAN成功弥合了单调性约束与KAN可解释性之间的鸿沟，提供了理论上严谨、实践上有效的单调神经网络方案，其表示代价定理为单调编码器设计提供了通用指导原则。

Abstract: Monotonicity has been a long-running architectural inductive bias for neural networks, motivated by tabular, scientific, and economic settings where outputs are known to respond monotonically to certain inputs. Existing approaches are MLP- or flow-based and lack per-edge functional transparency; the only Kolmogorov--Arnold Network (KAN) variant with monotonicity, MonoKAN, enforces the constraint only on a restricted parameter subset and requires a projection-style training procedure. We close this gap with \textbf{MKAN}, a KAN with hard monotonicity guaranteed for \emph{all} parameter values via exponential reparameterization of B-spline coefficients, positive edge weights, and a monotone base activation. Training reduces to standard unconstrained gradient descent. Our headline theoretical contribution is a \emph{representation-cost} theorem: any $C^K, K >0$ feature extractor inducing a ball-shaped semantic-neighborhood partition admits a monotone realization of the equivalent neighborhood structure at $N' = N^* + k \le 2N^*$, where $k$ is the number of non-monotone coordinates of the original. The bound is architecture-agnostic and gives a principled sizing rule for monotone encoders. Empirically, MKAN is competitive with state-of-the-art monotone NNs on the SMM/ICML-2024 benchmark while being the only method that combines hard unconstrained monotonicity with KAN's per-edge functional transparency; the $2N^*$ prediction is validated in a self-supervised feature-size sweep on four real datasets, and on a controlled monotone-generative dataset MKAN recovers ground-truth factors with substantially higher Spearman alignment than KAN, MLP, and linear baselines.

</details>


### [245] [Dimensionality Controls When Modularity Helps in Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17889)
*Kathrin Korte,Christian Medeiros Adriano,Joachim Winther Pedersen,Eleni Nisioti,Sebastian Risi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了模块化架构、任务相似性和表示维度如何共同影响组合持续学习。在高维度"惰性"机制下，模块化与单一网络性能相当；在低维度"丰富"机制下，模块化架构表现出更优的组合性和可解释性，其任务特异性子空间根据任务相似性呈现梯度重叠或分离。初始化尺度诱导的表示机制是决定模块化结构功能效益的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究组合学习系统中可塑性（获取新知识）与稳定性（保留已学组件）之间的平衡机制，特别是模块化架构、任务相似性和表示维度如何共同影响持续学习中的干扰与迁移。

Method: 采用序贯A-B-A范式，比较任务分区的循环网络与单一网络基线，通过权重量级操作诱导高维（惰性）和低维（丰富）表示机制，分析不同条件下的性能与内部几何结构。

Result: 高维惰性机制下两种架构性能相似；低维丰富机制下模块化网络形成梯度任务特异性子空间（相似任务重叠、中等任务部分对齐、不同任务分离），比单一网络更具组合性和可解释性。

Conclusion: 初始化尺度诱导的表示机制（与表示维度共变）是决定模块化结构在持续学习中何时具有功能效益的关键因素；应将安全性和鲁棒性视为表示子空间的自适应分配问题，而非固定分离或共享。

Abstract: Compositional learning systems must balance plasticity, the ability to acquire new knowledge, with stability, the preservation of previously learned components, especially when tasks share structure and risk interference. We study how modular architecture, task similarity, and representational dimensionality jointly shape compositional continual learning in a sequential A-B-A paradigm, comparing a task-partitioned recurrent network to a single-network baseline while inducing high- and low-dimensional regimes via weight-scale manipulations. In a high-dimensional "lazy" regime, both architectures achieve similar performance and internal geometry, suggesting that explicit modular structure has little impact when representations are weakly constrained. In a lower-dimensional "rich" regime, modularity becomes decisive: the modular network develops graded task-specific subspaces that overlap for similar tasks, partially align for moderately dissimilar tasks, and separate for dissimilar tasks, yielding a more compositional and interpretable organization than the single network. These findings identify the representational regime induced by initialization scale, which co-varies with representational dimensionality, as a key factor governing when compositional, modular structure is functionally beneficial in continual learning, and support viewing safety and robustness as problems of adaptive allocation of representational subspaces rather than fixed separation versus sharing.

</details>


### [246] [KANLib -- An Modular, Extensible and Fast Kolmogorov-Arnold Network Implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17927)
*Julian Hoever,Gregor Schiele*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出KANLib，一个模块化、可扩展且计算高效的KAN架构开发与评估框架，统一了现有KAN实现的核心概念，支持多种基函数类型和架构定制，在基准测试中展现了与参考实现相当的预测性能和更高的计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KAN研究面临高计算成本和不同框架间功能支持不一致的困境，缺乏一个统一、高效且功能完备的开发平台来推动KAN的实际研究与应用。

Method: KANLib采用模块化软件架构，统一了PyKAN、EfficientKAN、FastKAN等现有实现的核心概念，支持两种基函数类型、自适应网格缩放、网格扩展及细粒度架构定制，并保持与标准PyTorch工作流的兼容性。

Result: 在California Housing基准上，KANLib复现了参考KAN实现的预测行为，达到了具有竞争力的计算效率，并支持探索超出标准KAN公式的架构变体，且对预测性能影响微小。

Conclusion: KANLib为未来可扩展和可拓展KAN架构的研究提供了稳健的基础框架。

Abstract: Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) have recently emerged as a promising alternative to traditional multilayer perceptrons by replacing linear weights with learnable univariate functions. Despite their theoretical advantages in interpretability and expressiveness, practical research of KANs remains difficult due to high computational costs and inconsistent feature support across existing frameworks. This paper introduces KANLib, a modular, extensible, and computationally efficient framework for developing and evaluating KAN architectures. KANLib unifies core concepts from existing implementations, including PyKAN, EfficientKAN, and FastKAN, within a consistent software architecture that emphasizes flexibility, feature parity, and high performance. The framework supports two basis function types, adaptive grid rescaling, grid extension, and fine-grained architectural customization while maintaining compatibility with standard PyTorch workflows. Experimental evaluation on the California Housing benchmark demonstrates that KANLib reproduces the predictive behavior of established reference KAN implementations while achieving competitive computational efficiency. Furthermore, the framework enables the exploration of architectural variations beyond standard KAN formulations with only minor impacts on predictive performance. Overall, KANLib provides a robust foundation for future research on scalable and extensible KAN architectures.

</details>


### [247] [Predictive Analytics in E-Commerce for CustomerBehavior Forecasting using hybrid Ret-DNN withXGBoost Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17931)
*Degala Pushpa Sri,Mayank Atreya,Lakshmi. H,Navin Chhibber,Mukesh Soni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合Ret-DNN与XGBoost的模型，用于预测电商平台客户购买行为。基于英国在线零售商近50万条交易数据，经预处理后由Ret-DNN提取特征，再由XGBoost输出客户购买概率，最终MAE达0.2193，优于单一Ret-DNN模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商平台难以理解客户行为并预测其未来购买，现有方法无法有效捕捉零售数据中的时间特征和表格动态。

Method: 提出混合Ret-DNN与XGBoost模型：先对约50万条英国在线零售商交易数据进行数据清洗、异常处理、时序特征提取、特征编码及z-score归一化等预处理，然后通过Ret-DNN提取客户交易完整上下文的特征，最后将提取的特征输入XGBoost模型预测客户购买概率。

Result: 所提出的Ret-DNN XGBoost模型取得MAE为0.2193，优于现有Ret-DNN模型。

Conclusion: 混合Ret-DNN与XGBoost模型能有效捕捉零售数据中的时序特征与表格动态，在客户购买行为预测任务上表现优于单一深度神经网络模型。

Abstract: In recent years, electronic (E) commerce services have rapidly increased in the daily lives of people, which helpsthem to purchase products online. However, retail platforms have struggled to understand customer behavior and make it difficult to predict their future purchases. To overcome these challenges, this study proposes a hybrid Retail Deep NeuralNetwork (Ret-DNN) with an Extreme Gradient Boosting(XGBoost) model for capturing temporal features and tabular dynamics of retail data. First, data were sourced from a UnitedKingdom (UK)-based online retailer that contains transactions with almost 500,000 records. Then, the collected data were pre-processed using a series of techniques, such as data cleaning, outlier handling, temporal feature extraction, feature encoding, and z-score normalization, to ensure that the data were ready for model training and testing. Subsequently, the preprocessed data were fed into the Ret-DNN model, which acts as a feature extractor to understand the complete context of customer transactions. Further, the extracted data were fed as input into the XGBoost model, which predicted the final output as the purchase probability of customers. Finally, the proposed Ret-DNN XGBoost model achieved better results by attaining aMean Absolute Error (MAE) 0.2193 when compared to the existing Ret-DNN model.
  Keywords: Customer behavior forecasting, extreme gradientboosting, electronic commerce, predictive analytic, retail deepneural networks.

</details>


### [248] [SoftMoE: Soft Differentiable Routing for Mixture-of-Experts in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17952)
*Mikołaj Zasada,Łukasz Struski,Jacek Tabor,Marcin Kurdziel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SoftMoE通过可微的软top-k路由替代离散top-k路由，在保持自回归模型兼容性的同时，允许模型学习跨层的专家分配，以更少的激活专家达到或超越稀疏MoE的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏MoE中离散的top-k路由不可微，迫使每个输入激活固定数量的专家，导致计算效率低下。

Method: 提出SoftMoE，用截断的软top-k LapSum松弛替代离散路由，实现路由的梯度优化；同时参数化每层平均激活专家数并施加全局预算约束，使模型学习跨层分配专家容量。

Result: SoftMoE在语言建模和下游任务上达到与稀疏MoE相当或更优的性能，同时激活显著更少的专家；学到的分配高度非均匀，后面层激活更多专家。

Conclusion: SoftMoE通过可微路由和可学习的专家分配，在保持自回归建模兼容性的前提下，更高效地利用计算资源。

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures enable scaling LLM parameters under a fixed inference budget by activating only a small subset of experts via top-$k$ routing. While this preserves causality and suits autoregressive language models, the discrete top-$k$ operator is not differentiable, forcing a fixed number of active experts per input and resulting in inefficient use of computation. We propose SoftMoE, which replaces discrete routing with a truncated soft top-$k$ LapSum relaxation, allowing gradient-based optimization of expert routing. We further parameterize the mean number of active experts per layer and impose a global budget constraint, enabling the model to learn how to allocate expert capacity across layers. SoftMoE remains fully compatible with autoregressive modeling and achieves performance comparable to or better than sparse MoE on language modeling and downstream tasks, while activating significantly fewer experts. Notably, the learned allocation is highly non-uniform, with later layers activating more experts. The source code is publicly available$^\dagger$.

</details>


### [249] [Multiple cyclicity and Wavelet Decomposition with Channel Correlation for Long-term Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17996)
*Bin Wang,Heming Yang,Jinfang Sheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: McWC是一个长期时间序列预测模型，通过分别建模周期性、趋势和通道间相关性，并结合多级小波分解与频域损失函数，在6个真实数据集上实现了SOTA性能和高计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期时间序列预测工作忽略了真实世界中时间序列数据的通道间相关性，导致预测次优；同时现有模型依赖复杂设计捕获多样化信息，导致计算效率低下。

Method: McWC首先通过多层周期构建模块从数据中解耦周期信息，然后使用多层感知机提取通道间相关性，接着利用多级小波分解模块建模并融合多层高频和低频信息，最后聚合各组件结果得到输出。同时，通过频域损失函数解耦通道内自相关性。

Result: 在六个真实世界数据集上，McWC实现了最先进的性能，展现出优异的计算效率和历史信息提取能力。

Conclusion: 分别建模周期性、趋势和通道间相关性是提升长期时间序列预测性能的有效途径，且能在保持高效计算的同时实现优异预测效果。

Abstract: Cyclicity and trend are important components of time series data and many studies based on cyclicity and trend have achieved good results in long-term time series forecasting. However, we believe that current work neglects the influence of real-world inter-channel correlations in time series data which leads to suboptimal predictions. Furthermore, these models rely on complex designs to capture diverse information so that resulting in low computational efficiency. To address this challenge, we propose McWC, a long-term time series forecasting model that separately models the cyclicity, trend, and inter-channel correlations. Specifically, McWC first decouples cyclical information from data using a multi-layer cyclicity construction module. Then, it extracts inter-channel correlations using multi-layer perceptron. Next, it models and fuses the multi-layer high-frequency and low-frequency information from data using a multi-level wavelet decomposition module. Finally, it aggregates the results of different components to obtain the output. Simultaneously, we decouple intra-channel autocorrelations by calculating a loss function in the frequency domain. Experiments on six real-world datasets demonstrate that McWC achieves state-of-the-art performance, exhibiting excellent computational efficiency and historical information extraction capabilities.

</details>


### [250] [Half a Link can Be Enough to Predict a Whole Link: Understanding Generalization in Knowledge Graph Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18001)
*Cosimo Gregucci,Obaidah Theeb,Daniel Hernandez,Antonio Vergari,Steffen Staab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了知识图谱基础模型（KGFM）在零样本泛化中的关键机制：模型在未见KG上的表现取决于测试三元组中半链接（half-links）是否被观测到，并提出四类场景的细粒度分类法作为诊断协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KGFM声称能零样本泛化到未见知识图谱，但其泛化机制尚不明确，缺乏对何时以及如何鲁棒泛化的理解。

Method: 提出"半链接"（half-link）概念（即(h,r)或(r,t)），将测试三元组按半链接是否在推理图中被观测分为四类场景，并在该分类下进行严格的层次化对比分析。

Result: SoTA KGFM依赖已见半链接进行预测，而未见半链接会带来不同的挑战；模型在四类场景下的性能表现不均匀。
